# DDVR (Denoising Direct Volume Rendering)
**Master thesis:** Varying Step Size Ray Marching with Denoising for Direct Volume Rendering.

The program was primarily developed on Linux (Ubuntu) and has been partially tested on Windows and macOS.


**Abstract**  
*Real-time direct volume rendering remains a computationally demanding task due to the large number of ray marching steps required to achieve visually pleasing results. This thesis investigates techniques to reduce the computational cost of ray marching, with a particular focus on varying step size strategies and denoising methods.*

<p align="center">
  <img src="./data/imgs/image.png" alt="alt text" />
</p>


## Renderer
### Direct Volume Renderer
A direct volume renderer has been implemented, supporting both 3D and 4D (time-varying) volumes.

![alt text](./data/imgs/screenshot.png)

**Time-varying Volume**
<img src="./data/imgs/screenshot.gif" alt="Gray-Scott" width="1080"/>
More rendered screenshots at [Gallery](Gallery.md)

### Usage
View and edit `config/app_config.json` and `config/gfx_config.json`
```
cd build
./DDVR
```

### Preparation and Build
#### Volume Data
##### Download Volume Data
```bash
cd data
wget --mirror -np -nH --cut-dirs=2 -R "index.html*" "https://home.cit.tum.de/~gaoa/data/"
```
##### Use Your Own Volume Data

Only `.npy` (NumPy) volume data is supported. Convert `.mhd` volumes to `.npy` via:

```bash
pip install -r scripts/requirements.txt
python3 scripts/mhd_to_numpy.py
```

Update the volume data path in `config/app_config.json`.


#### Color Mapping

To generate colormap contents:

```bash
python3 -m pip install -r scripts/requirements.txt
python3 scripts/colormaps.py
```
#### Build without OIDN and trained denoiser

```bash
mkdir build && cd build
cmake ..
cmake --build .
```

#### Prerequisites for OIDN
<details>
<summary> Details </summary>

Only **GPU-based denoising with CUDA and Metal** is currently implemented. For CUDA, make sure the **CUDA toolkit** is installed.

**Windows**

```bash
git config core.longpaths true
scoop install ispc
cd DVR_Denoise
conan install .. --output-folder=build -s build_type=[Debug/Release] --build=missing -o "OIDN_ACTIVATE=True"
```

**Linux (Ubuntu)**

```bash
sudo apt install ispc libtbb-dev
```
</details>

#### Prerequisites for trained denoiser
<details>
<summary> Details: </summary>

`onnxruntime` is used to infer our trained model. To install `onnxruntime`:

```bash
conan install .. --output-folder=build -s build_type=[Debug/Release] --build=missing -o "TRAINED_DENOISER_ACTIVATE=True"
```
</details>


#### Build the project with OIDN and trained denoiser
Activate the options via conan (optional):
```bash
pushd build
conan install .. -s build_type=Debug --build=missing -o "OIDN_ACTIVATE=True" -o "TRAINED_DENOISER_ACTIVATE=True"
```

Build with preset generated by conan:
```bash
cd build
cmake .. --preset conan-[release/debug]
cmake --build . -j
```

-o "IMGUI_TEST_ENGINE=True"
```

### conanfile.py
```
import shutil
from conan import ConanFile
from conan.tools.cmake import CMakeToolchain, CMakeDeps, cmake_layout

class DVRPackage(ConanFile):
    settings = "os", "compiler", "build_type", "arch"

    options = {
        "OIDN_ACTIVATE": [True, False],
        "TRAINED_DENOISER_ACTIVATE": [True, False],
        "IMGUI_TEST_ENGINE": [True, False]
    }
    default_options = {
        "OIDN_ACTIVATE": False,
        "TRAINED_DENOISER_ACTIVATE": False,
        "IMGUI_TEST_ENGINE": False
    }

    def layout(self):
        self.folders.build = "build"
        self.folders.generators = "build/generators"

    def requirements(self):
        if self.options.OIDN_ACTIVATE and self.settings.os == "Windows":
            self.requires("onetbb/2022.0.0")
        if self.options.TRAINED_DENOISER_ACTIVATE and self.settings.os != "Macos":
            self.requires("cutlass/3.5.1", override=True)
            self.requires("onnxruntime/1.18.1")
        if self.settings.os == "Macos":
            self.requires("llvm-openmp/20.1.6")

    def configure(self):
        if self.options.TRAINED_DENOISER_ACTIVATE:
            self.options["onnxruntime"].with_cuda = True

    def generate(self):
        tc = CMakeToolchain(self)

        if shutil.which("ninja"):
            tc.generator = "Ninja"

        tc.variables["OIDN_ACTIVATE"] = self.options.OIDN_ACTIVATE
        if self.settings.os != "Macos":
            tc.variables["TRAINED_DENOISER_ACTIVATE"] = self.options.TRAINED_DENOISER_ACTIVATE
        tc.variables["CMAKE_EXPORT_COMPILE_COMMANDS"] = True
        tc.variables["IMGUI_TEST_ENGINE"] = self.options.IMGUI_TEST_ENGINE
        tc.generate()
        deps = CMakeDeps(self)
        deps.generate()
```

### experiments/utils.py
```
import os
from PIL import Image
import numpy as np
from skimage.metrics import peak_signal_noise_ratio, structural_similarity
import torch
import lpips

def calculate_psnr(img1, img2):
    # img1, img2: numpy arrays, range [0,255]
    # Convert to float32 to avoid overflow issues
    img1 = img1.astype(np.float32)
    img2 = img2.astype(np.float32)
    mse = np.mean((img1 - img2) ** 2)
    if mse == 0:
        return float('inf')
    max_pixel = 255.0
    psnr = 20 * np.log10(max_pixel / np.sqrt(mse))
    return psnr

def calculate_ssim(img1, img2):
    # img1, img2: numpy arrays, range [0,255]
    # Convert to float32 and normalize to [0,1] for SSIM calculation
    img1_norm = img1.astype(np.float32) / 255.0
    img2_norm = img2.astype(np.float32) / 255.0
    # Use channel_axis for color images (axis 2 for RGB)
    return structural_similarity(img1_norm, img2_norm, channel_axis=2, data_range=1.0)

def load_image(image_path):
    # Load an image from a file and convert it to a numpy array
    img = Image.open(image_path).convert("RGB")
    return np.array(img)

def calculate_lpips(img1, img2, net='alex'):
    """
    Calculate LPIPS (Learned Perceptual Image Patch Similarity) between two images.
    
    Args:
        img1, img2: numpy arrays, range [0,255], shape (H, W, 3)
        net: network to use ('alex', 'vgg', 'squeeze')
    
    Returns:
        LPIPS distance (lower is better, 0 means identical)
    """
    # Initialize LPIPS model
    loss_fn = lpips.LPIPS(net=net)
    
    # Convert numpy arrays to tensors and normalize to [-1, 1]
    def preprocess(img):
        # Convert from [0,255] to [0,1]
        img = img.astype(np.float32) / 255.0
        # Convert from [0,1] to [-1,1]
        img = img * 2.0 - 1.0
        # Convert from HWC to CHW and add batch dimension
        img = torch.from_numpy(img).permute(2, 0, 1).unsqueeze(0)
        return img
    
    img1_tensor = preprocess(img1)
    img2_tensor = preprocess(img2)
    
    # Calculate LPIPS
    with torch.no_grad():
        lpips_distance = loss_fn(img1_tensor, img2_tensor)
    
    return float(lpips_distance.item())

def total_variation(img):
    # Calculate the total variation of an image.
    # img: numpy array, shape (H, W, C)
    img = img.astype(np.float32) / 255.0
    # Compute the gradient
    dx = np.diff(img, axis=1)  # Shape: (H, W-1, C)
    dy = np.diff(img, axis=0)  # Shape: (H-1, W, C)
    
    # To make shapes compatible, we need to crop the arrays
    # Take the common region: (H-1, W-1, C)
    dx_cropped = dx[:-1, :, :]  # Remove last row: (H-1, W-1, C)
    dy_cropped = dy[:, :-1, :]  # Remove last column: (H-1, W-1, C)
    
    # Compute the total variation
    tv = np.sum(np.sqrt(dx_cropped**2 + dy_cropped**2))
    return tv
```

### nn/lstm_inference.py
```
import torch
import argparse
import onnx
import torch
from onnx2torch import convert
from torchvision import transforms
from PIL import Image
import numpy as np
import glob 
import os
import torchvision.utils as vutils

# Note: This is not for test/validate the model!
# Test and validation are done directly in the renderer.

def load_model(device, onnx_model_path):
    """
    Load an ONNX model and convert it to a PyTorch model.
    """
    try:
        onnx_model = onnx.load(onnx_model_path)
        torch_model = convert(onnx_model)
        torch_model.eval()
        torch_model = torch_model.to(device)
        return torch_model
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Error loading ONNX model: {e}")
        return None

transform = transforms.Compose([
    transforms.ToTensor()
])

def load_images(device, input_imageset_folder):
    """    Load the noisy image, noise image, and motion vector image from the input folder.
    """ 
    noisy_images = []
    noise_images = []
    motion_vector_images = []
    image_files = glob.glob(os.path.join(input_imageset_folder, "*.jpg"))
    for images_path in image_files:
        if images_path.endswith("_noise.jpg"):
            noise_images.append(transform(Image.open(images_path).convert("RGB")).unsqueeze(0).to(device))
        elif images_path.endswith("_motion_vector.jpg"):
            motion_vector_images.append(transform(Image.open(images_path).convert("RGB")).unsqueeze(0).to(device))
        else:
            noisy_images.append(transform(Image.open(images_path).convert("RGB")).unsqueeze(0).to(device))

    print(len(noisy_images))
    noisy_seq = torch.cat(noisy_images, dim=0).unsqueeze(0) if noisy_images else None
    noise_seq = torch.cat(noise_images, dim=0).unsqueeze(0) if noise_images else None
    motion_vector_seq = torch.cat(motion_vector_images, dim=0).unsqueeze(0) if motion_vector_images else None
    print(noisy_seq.shape if noisy_seq is not None else 'No noisy images loaded')
    print(noise_seq.shape if noise_seq is not None else 'No noise images loaded')
    print(motion_vector_seq.shape if motion_vector_seq is not None else 'No motion vector images loaded')
    return noisy_seq, noise_seq, motion_vector_seq

if __name__ == "__main__":
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Inference an onnx model")
    parser.add_argument("--onnx_model_path", type=str, help="Path to the ONNX model file")
    parser.add_argument("--input_imageset_folder", type=str, help="Path to the input image set folder (contains noisy, noise and motion vector images)")
    parser.add_argument("--output_path", type=str, default="output.jpg", help="Path to save the output image")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    device = torch.device("cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu")
    print(f"Using device: {device}")

    torch_model = load_model(device, args.onnx_model_path)

    noisy_sequence, noise_sequence, motion_vector_sequence = load_images(device, args.input_imageset_folder)

    with torch.no_grad():
        output = torch_model(noisy_sequence, motion_vector_sequence, noise_sequence)
        output = output.clamp(0, 1)
        vutils.save_image(output[0], args.output_path)
        print(f"Inference completed. Output saved to {args.output_path}")
```

### nn/unet_inference.py
```
import torch
import argparse
import onnx
import torch
from onnx2torch import convert
from torchvision import transforms
from PIL import Image
import numpy as np

def load_model(device, onnx_model_path):
    """
    Load an ONNX model and convert it to a PyTorch model.
    """
    try:
        onnx_model = onnx.load(onnx_model_path)
        torch_model = convert(onnx_model)
        torch_model.eval()
        torch_model = torch_model.to(device)
        return torch_model
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Error loading ONNX model: {e}")
        return None

transform = transforms.Compose([
    transforms.ToTensor()
])

def load_images(device, noisy_image_path, noise_image_path):
    """
    Load the noisy image and step size noise pattern
    """
    noisy_image = transform(Image.open(noisy_image_path).convert("RGB")).unsqueeze(0).to(device)
    noise_image = transform(Image.open(noise_image_path).convert("RGB")).unsqueeze(0).to(device)
    return noisy_image, noise_image

# Example usage:
# python3 nn/inference.py --onnx_model_path nn/models/denoising_model_final.onnx --input_noisy_image ./build/export_image.jpg  --input_noise_image ./build/export_image_noise.jpg --output_path nn/output.jpg
if __name__ == "__main__":
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Inference an onnx model")
    parser.add_argument("--onnx_model_path", type=str, help="Path to the ONNX model file")
    parser.add_argument("--input_noisy_image", type=str, help="Path to the input noisy image file (numpy array)")
    parser.add_argument("--input_noise_image", type=str, help="Path to the input noise image file (numpy array)")
    parser.add_argument("--output_path", type=str, default="output.jpg", help="Path to save the output image")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    device = torch.device("cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu")
    print(f"Using device: {device}")

    torch_model = load_model(device, args.onnx_model_path)

    noisy_image, noise_image = load_images(device, args.input_noisy_image, args.input_noise_image)

    # Perform inference
    with torch.no_grad():
        output = torch_model(noisy_image, noise_image)
        output = output.squeeze(0).cpu().numpy()
        output = (output * 255).astype('uint8')
        output_image = Image.fromarray(output.transpose(1, 2, 0))
        output_image.save(args.output_path)
        print(f"Inference completed. Output saved to {args.output_path}")
```

### renderer/app_config.cpp
```
#include "app_config.h"

#include <nlohmann/json.hpp>

AppConfig::AppConfig(const std::filesystem::path &path) : m_path{path}
{
    bool result = loadFromFile();
    if (!result)
    {
        throw std::runtime_error("Failed to load app config file");
    }
    std::cout << "App config loaded successfully" << std::endl;

    // set internal configurations
    InternalConfig.lstmDatasetExport = false;
    InternalConfig.visualizeRLS      = false;
}

bool AppConfig::loadFromFile()
{
    std::ifstream file(m_path);
    if (!file.is_open())
    {
        return false;
    }

    nlohmann::json jsonConfig;
    file >> jsonConfig;

    VolumetricConfig.volumetricPath   = jsonConfig["volumes"]["path"];
    VolumetricConfig.volumeUnitLength = jsonConfig["volumes"]["unit_length"];
    VolumetricConfig.stepSize         = jsonConfig["volumes"]["step_size"];
    VolumetricConfig.timeStep4D       = jsonConfig["volumes"]["4d_time_step"];

    // noise
    NoiseConfig.varyingStepSize           = jsonConfig["noise"]["vary_step_size"];
    NoiseConfig.noiseColor                = (jsonConfig["noise"]["noise_color"] == "blue") ? 1 : 0;
    NoiseConfig.UniformNoise.min          = jsonConfig["noise"]["uniform_noise"]["min"];
    NoiseConfig.UniformNoise.max          = jsonConfig["noise"]["uniform_noise"]["max"];
    NoiseConfig.GaussianNoise.min         = jsonConfig["noise"]["gaussian_noise"]["min"];
    NoiseConfig.GaussianNoise.max         = jsonConfig["noise"]["gaussian_noise"]["max"];
    NoiseConfig.ExponentialNoise.min      = jsonConfig["noise"]["exponential_noise"]["min"];
    NoiseConfig.ExponentialNoise.max      = jsonConfig["noise"]["exponential_noise"]["max"];
    NoiseConfig.NoiseGenerationShaderPath = jsonConfig["noise"]["per_frame_update_shader_path"];

    // camera
    CameraConfig.fov              = jsonConfig["camera"]["fov"];
    CameraConfig.near             = jsonConfig["camera"]["near"];
    CameraConfig.far              = jsonConfig["camera"]["far"];
    CameraConfig.recordingCapture = jsonConfig["camera"]["recording_capture"];

    // basic shaders
    RaymarchingShaderPath    = jsonConfig["shaders"]["raymarching_shader_path"];
    OutputVertexShaderPath   = jsonConfig["shaders"]["output_vertex_shader_path"];
    OutputFragmentShaderPath = jsonConfig["shaders"]["output_fragment_shader_path"];

    // denoiser configurations
    Denoiser.ScreenSpaced.MeanFilter.shaderPath = jsonConfig["denoiser"]["screen_spaced"]["mean_filter"]["shader_path"];
    Denoiser.ScreenSpaced.MeanFilter.kernelSize = jsonConfig["denoiser"]["screen_spaced"]["mean_filter"]["kernel_size"];

    Denoiser.ScreenSpaced.GaussianFilter.shaderPath =
        jsonConfig["denoiser"]["screen_spaced"]["gaussian_filter"]["shader_path"];
    Denoiser.ScreenSpaced.GaussianFilter.kernelSize =
        jsonConfig["denoiser"]["screen_spaced"]["gaussian_filter"]["kernel_size"];

    Denoiser.ScreenSpaced.MedianFilter.shaderPath =
        jsonConfig["denoiser"]["screen_spaced"]["median_filter"]["shader_path"];
    Denoiser.ScreenSpaced.MedianFilter.kernelSize =
        jsonConfig["denoiser"]["screen_spaced"]["median_filter"]["kernel_size"];

    Denoiser.ScreenSpaced.BilateralFilter.shaderPath =
        jsonConfig["denoiser"]["screen_spaced"]["bilateral_filter"]["shader_path"];
    Denoiser.ScreenSpaced.BilateralFilter.kernelSize =
        jsonConfig["denoiser"]["screen_spaced"]["bilateral_filter"]["kernel_size"];
    Denoiser.ScreenSpaced.BilateralFilter.sigmaSpatial =
        jsonConfig["denoiser"]["screen_spaced"]["bilateral_filter"]["sigma_s"];
    Denoiser.ScreenSpaced.BilateralFilter.sigmaColor =
        jsonConfig["denoiser"]["screen_spaced"]["bilateral_filter"]["sigma_r"];
    Denoiser.ScreenSpaced.BilateralFilter.kernelSize =
        jsonConfig["denoiser"]["screen_spaced"]["bilateral_filter"]["kernel_size"];

    Denoiser.ScreenSpaced.TotalVariation.shaderPath =
        jsonConfig["denoiser"]["screen_spaced"]["total_variation"]["shader_path"];
    Denoiser.ScreenSpaced.TotalVariation.lambda = jsonConfig["denoiser"]["screen_spaced"]["total_variation"]["lambda"];
    Denoiser.ScreenSpaced.TotalVariation.iterations =
        jsonConfig["denoiser"]["screen_spaced"]["total_variation"]["iterations"];

    Denoiser.ScreenSpaced.WienerFilter.shaderPath =
        jsonConfig["denoiser"]["screen_spaced"]["wiener_filter"]["shader_path"];
    Denoiser.ScreenSpaced.WienerFilter.kernelSize =
        jsonConfig["denoiser"]["screen_spaced"]["wiener_filter"]["kernel_size"];

    // temporal denoisers
    auto temporal = jsonConfig["denoiser"]["temporal"];

    Denoiser.TemporalWeightedMean.reprojectionShaderPath =
        temporal["temporal_weighted_mean"]["reprojection_shader_path"];
    Denoiser.TemporalWeightedMean.denoiserShaderPath = temporal["temporal_weighted_mean"]["denoiser_shader_path"];
    Denoiser.TemporalWeightedMean.perFrameUpdate     = temporal["temporal_weighted_mean"]["steps_per_frame_update"];

    Denoiser.TemporalRLS.reprojectionShaderPath = temporal["temporal_rls"]["reprojection_shader_path"];
    Denoiser.TemporalRLS.denoiserShaderPath     = temporal["temporal_rls"]["denoiser_shader_path"];
    Denoiser.TemporalRLS.lambdaStatic           = temporal["temporal_rls"]["lambda_static"];
    Denoiser.TemporalRLS.lambdaMoving           = temporal["temporal_rls"]["lambda_moving"];
    Denoiser.TemporalRLS.perFrameUpdate         = temporal["temporal_rls"]["steps_per_frame_update"];

    Denoiser.TemporalRLS.filterType = temporal["temporal_rls"]["filter_type"];
    if (Denoiser.TemporalRLS.filterType != "mean" && Denoiser.TemporalRLS.filterType != "median")
    {
        std::cerr << "Unknown filter type: " << Denoiser.TemporalRLS.filterType
                  << "Supported strings are median or mean. Using default 'median' filter type." << std::endl;
        Denoiser.TemporalRLS.filterType = "median";
    }

    Denoiser.TemporalRLS.filterLocation = temporal["temporal_rls"]["filter_location"];
    if (Denoiser.TemporalRLS.filterLocation != "before" && Denoiser.TemporalRLS.filterLocation != "after" &&
        Denoiser.TemporalRLS.filterLocation != "none")
    {
        std::cerr << "Unknown filter location: " << Denoiser.TemporalRLS.filterLocation
                  << "Supported strings are after, before, none. Using default 'after' filter location." << std::endl;
        Denoiser.TemporalRLS.filterLocation = "before";
    }

    auto dlConfig = jsonConfig["denoiser"]["dl"];

    Denoiser.OIDN.gpu                   = dlConfig["oidn"]["gpu"];
    Denoiser.OIDN.preprocessShaderPath  = dlConfig["oidn"]["preprocess_shader_path"];
    Denoiser.OIDN.postprocessShaderPath = dlConfig["oidn"]["postprocess_shader_path"];

    Denoiser.UNet.cudaProcess = dlConfig["unet"]["cuda_process"];
    Denoiser.UNet.nnModelPath = dlConfig["unet"]["model_path"];

    Denoiser.LSTM.cudaProcess = dlConfig["lstm"]["cuda_process"];
    Denoiser.LSTM.nnModelPath = dlConfig["lstm"]["model_path"];

    MotionVectorPostProcessingPath = jsonConfig["motion_vector_post_processing_path"];
    UIThemePath                    = jsonConfig["ui_theme_path"];
    ColorMapsPath                  = jsonConfig["color_maps_folder"];
    ColorMap                       = jsonConfig["color_map"];

    ExportFilePath = jsonConfig["export_file_name"];
    ExportFilePath += ".jpg";

    return true;
}
```

### renderer/app_config.h
```
#pragma once
#include <filesystem>
#include <fstream>
#include <iostream>
#include <string>

class AppConfig
{
  public:
    AppConfig(const std::filesystem::path &path);

    struct CameraConfig
    {
        float fov;
        float near;
        float far;
        bool  recordingCapture;
    } CameraConfig;

    struct DenoiserConfig
    {
        struct ScreenSpacedConfig
        {
            struct GaussianFilterConfig
            {
                std::string shaderPath;
                int         kernelSize;
            } GaussianFilter;
            struct MeanFilteringconfig
            {
                std::string shaderPath;
                int         kernelSize;
            } MeanFilter;
            struct BilateralFilterConfig
            {
                std::string shaderPath;
                int         kernelSize;
                float       sigmaSpatial;
                float       sigmaColor;
            } BilateralFilter;
            struct MedianFilterConfig
            {
                std::string shaderPath;
                int         kernelSize;
            } MedianFilter;
            struct TotalVariationConfig
            {
                std::string shaderPath;
                float       lambda;
                int         iterations;
            } TotalVariation;
            struct WienerFilterConfig
            {
                std::string shaderPath;
                int         kernelSize;
                float       noiseVariance;
            } WienerFilter;
        } ScreenSpaced;

        struct TemporalWeightedMeanConfig
        {
            std::string reprojectionShaderPath;
            std::string denoiserShaderPath;
            bool        perFrameUpdate;
        } TemporalWeightedMean;

        struct TemporalRLSConfig
        {
            std::string reprojectionShaderPath;
            std::string denoiserShaderPath;
            float       lambdaStatic;
            float       lambdaMoving;
            bool        perFrameUpdate;
            std::string filterType;
            std::string filterLocation;
        } TemporalRLS;

        struct OIDNConfig
        {
            bool        gpu;
            std::string preprocessShaderPath;
            std::string postprocessShaderPath;
        } OIDN;

        struct UNetConfig
        {
            std::string nnModelPath;
            bool        cudaProcess;
        } UNet;

        struct LSTMConfig
        {
            std::string nnModelPath;
            bool        cudaProcess;
        } LSTM;
    } Denoiser;

    struct VolumetricConfig
    {
        std::string volumetricPath;
        float       volumeUnitLength;
        float       stepSize;
        float       timeStep4D;
    } VolumetricConfig;

    struct NoiseConfig
    {
        bool varyingStepSize;
        int  noiseColor;
        struct UniformNoise
        {
            float min;
            float max;
        } UniformNoise;

        struct GaussianNoise
        {
            float min;
            float max;
        } GaussianNoise;

        struct ExponentialNoise
        {
            float min;
            float max;
        } ExponentialNoise;

        std::string NoiseGenerationShaderPath;

    } NoiseConfig;

    // configurations that are not loaded from file
    struct InternalConfig
    {
        bool lstmDatasetExport;        // export motion vector for fixed step size case
        bool visualizeRLS;             // export data for visualize RLS
    } InternalConfig;

    std::string RaymarchingShaderPath;
    std::string OutputVertexShaderPath;
    std::string OutputFragmentShaderPath;

    std::string MotionVectorPostProcessingPath;
    std::string UIThemePath;
    std::string ColorMapsPath;
    std::string ColorMap;
    std::string ExportFilePath;

  private:
    bool loadFromFile();

  private:
    const std::filesystem::path &m_path;
};
```

### renderer/camera.cpp
```
#include "camera.h"
#include "imgui.h"
#include "utils/container.h"
#include "utils/utils.h"
#include <glm/ext/matrix_clip_space.hpp>
#include <glm/ext/matrix_transform.hpp>

void Camera::CamDeviceData::SetupProjection(const AppConfig &config, const GFX::GfxConfig &gfxConfig)
{
    projection =
        glm::perspective(glm::radians(config.CameraConfig.fov), gfxConfig.WindowWidth / (float) gfxConfig.WindowHeight,
                         config.CameraConfig.near, config.CameraConfig.far);
    near = config.CameraConfig.near;
    far  = config.CameraConfig.far;
}

void Camera::UpdateDeviceData()
{
    float radYaw   = glm::radians(cameraData.yaw);
    float radPitch = glm::radians(cameraData.pitch);

    glm::vec3 position;
    position.x              = cameraData.target.x + cameraData.distance * cos(radPitch) * sin(radYaw);
    position.y              = cameraData.target.y + cameraData.distance * sin(radPitch);
    position.z              = cameraData.target.z + cameraData.distance * cos(radPitch) * cos(radYaw);
    camDeviceData.view      = glm::lookAt(position, cameraData.target, glm::vec3(0, 1, 0));
    camDeviceData.transform = glm::inverse(camDeviceData.view);
}

void Camera::CreateCameraDeviceBuffer(GFX::VkContext &context)
{
    Utils::CreateAndCopyBuffer(context, &camDeviceData, sizeof(CamDeviceData),
                               vk::BufferUsageFlagBits::eUniformBuffer | vk::BufferUsageFlagBits::eTransferDst,
                               VMA_MEMORY_USAGE_GPU_ONLY, CameraDeviceBuffer);
    CameraDeviceBuffer->SetName("Camera Buffer");
}

void Camera::CreatePreviousCameraDeviceBuffer(GFX::VkContext &context)
{
    Utils::CreateAndCopyBuffer(context, &camDeviceData, sizeof(CamDeviceData),
                               vk::BufferUsageFlagBits::eStorageBuffer | vk::BufferUsageFlagBits::eUniformBuffer |
                                   vk::BufferUsageFlagBits::eTransferDst,
                               VMA_MEMORY_USAGE_GPU_ONLY, PreviousCameraDeviceBuffer);
    PreviousCameraDeviceBuffer->SetName("Previous Camera Buffer");
}

void Camera::DrawCameraRoutineUI()
{
    if (!IsPlaying)
    {
        // UIs
        if (ImGui::CollapsingHeader("Camera routine"))
        {
            ImGui::InputText("Camera routine file", m_rountineRecordingPath.data(), sizeof(m_rountineRecordingPath));
            if (ImGui::Button(m_recordingButtonName[IsRecording ? 1 : 0].c_str()))
            {
                IsRecording = !IsRecording;
                if (IsRecording && std::filesystem::exists(m_rountineRecordingPath))
                {
                    std::filesystem::remove(m_rountineRecordingPath);
                }
            }

            if (ImGui::Button("Play camera movement"))
            {
                if (!std::filesystem::exists(m_rountineRecordingPath))
                {
                    std::cerr << "Camera routine file does not exist, please record first!" << std::endl;
                    return;
                }

                IsPlaying    = !IsPlaying;
                PlayingFrame = -1;
                if (IsPlaying)
                {
                    m_routineFile.open(m_rountineRecordingPath);
                }

                // recording
                if (IsRecordingFrame)
                {
                    auto        now = std::chrono::system_clock::now();
                    std::time_t t   = std::chrono::system_clock::to_time_t(now);

                    std::stringstream ss;
                    ss << std::put_time(std::localtime(&t), "Recording_%Y-%m-%d_%H-%M-%S");
                    std::string folderName = ss.str();

                    std::filesystem::create_directory(folderName);
                    RecordingFilesFolder = ss.str();
                }
            }
        }
    }

    // handle recording
    if (IsRecording)
    {
        std::ofstream file(m_rountineRecordingPath, std::ios::app);
        if (file.is_open())
        {
            file << cameraData.yaw << " " << cameraData.pitch << " " << cameraData.distance << std::endl;
        }
        else
        {
            std::cerr << "Unable to open file for writing camera routine" << std::endl;
        }
    }

    // handle camera playing
    if (IsPlaying && m_routineFile.is_open())
    {
        std::string line;
        if (std::getline(m_routineFile, line))
        {
            std::istringstream iss(line);
            float              yaw, pitch, distance;
            yaw      = cameraData.yaw;
            pitch    = cameraData.pitch;
            distance = cameraData.distance;

            iss >> cameraData.yaw >> cameraData.pitch >> cameraData.distance;
            if (cameraData.yaw != yaw || cameraData.pitch != pitch || cameraData.distance != distance)
            {
                camDeviceData.moved = true;
            }
            else
            {
                camDeviceData.moved = false;
            }

            PlayingFrame++;
        }
        else
        {
            IsPlaying = false;
            m_routineFile.close();
            std::cout << "End of camera routine" << std::endl;
            cameraData.yaw      = 0.0f;
            cameraData.pitch    = 0.0f;
            cameraData.distance = 3.0f;
        }

        UpdateDeviceData();
        g_DirtyBuffers.insert({CameraDeviceBuffer->Buffer, 0, sizeof(Camera::CamDeviceData), &camDeviceData});
    }
}
```

### renderer/camera.h
```
#pragma once
#include "app_config.h"
#include "buffer.h"
#include "context.h"
#include "gfx_config.h"
#include <glm/glm.hpp>
#include <optional>

/**
 * @brief Camera holder
 */
class Camera
{
  public:
    void DrawCameraRoutineUI();
    void UpdateDeviceData();
    void CreateCameraDeviceBuffer(GFX::VkContext &context);
    void CreatePreviousCameraDeviceBuffer(GFX::VkContext &context);

  public:
    // camera data that will be passed to the shader
    struct CamDeviceData
    {
        alignas(4) int moved = false;

        alignas(16) glm::mat4 transform;
        alignas(16) glm::mat4 view;
        alignas(16) glm::mat4 projection;
        alignas(4) float near;
        alignas(4) float far;

        void SetupProjection(const AppConfig &config, const GFX::GfxConfig &gfxConfig);

    } camDeviceData;

    // camera data helper
    struct CameraData
    {
        float     yaw = 0.0f, pitch = 0.0f;
        glm::vec3 target   = glm::vec3(0.0f);
        float     distance = 3.0f;
    } cameraData;

  public:
    std::optional<GFX::VkBufferObject> CameraDeviceBuffer;
    std::optional<GFX::VkBufferObject> PreviousCameraDeviceBuffer;

    bool        IsRecording{false};
    bool        IsPlaying{false};
    bool        IsRecordingFrame{false};
    uint32_t    PlayingFrame = 0;
    std::string RecordingFilesFolder;

  private:
    std::string                m_rountineRecordingPath{"camera_routine.txt"};
    std::array<std::string, 2> m_recordingButtonName = {"Record camera movement", "Stop recording"};
    std::ifstream              m_routineFile;
};

### renderer/color_mapping.cpp
```
#include "color_mapping.h"

#include "imgui_internal.h"
#include "third-party/libnpy.h"

ColorMapping::ColorMapping(GFX::VkContext &context, const std::filesystem::path &colorMapsPath,
                           const std::string &initialColorMap) :
    m_context(context), m_colorMapsPath{colorMapsPath}, m_customColorMapping(false), m_initialColorMap{initialColorMap}
{
    LoadPredefinedColorMapping();
}

void ColorMapping::DrawUI()
{
    ImGui::SeparatorText("Color Mapping");

    if (ImGui::Checkbox("Custom Color Mapping", &m_customColorMapping))
    {
        CreateOrUpdateTransferTexture();
    }

    if (m_customColorMapping)
    {
        if (ImGui::CollapsingHeader("Editor"))
        {
            DrawCustomColorMappingUI();
            if (ImGui::Button("Apply"))
            {
                // reupload texture
                CreateOrUpdateTransferTexture();
            }
        }
    }
    else
    {
        std::vector<const char *> predefinedColorMappingNamesCStr;
        for (const auto &name : PredefinedColorMappingNames)
        {
            predefinedColorMappingNamesCStr.push_back(name.c_str());
        }

        if (ImGui::Combo("ColorMap", &selectedIndex, predefinedColorMappingNamesCStr.data(),
                         static_cast<int>(predefinedColorMappingNamesCStr.size())) ||
            DrawAlphaEditor())
        {
            // reupload texture
            CreateOrUpdateTransferTexture();
        }
    }

    ImGui::Text("Preview");
    DrawTextureDataPreview(m_customColorMapping);
}

void ColorMapping::CreateCustomColorMappingData()
{
    const int textureSize = 256;
    m_textureData.resize(textureSize * 4);

    for (int i = 0; i < textureSize; ++i)
    {
        float t = float(i) / float(textureSize - 1);

        for (size_t j = 0; j < Positions.size() - 1; ++j)
        {
            if (t >= Positions[j] && t <= Positions[j + 1])
            {
                float     localT = (t - Positions[j]) / (Positions[j + 1] - Positions[j]);
                glm::vec4 color  = glm::mix(ControlPoints[j], ControlPoints[j + 1], localT);

                m_textureData[i * 4 + 0] = static_cast<uint8_t>(glm::clamp(color.r, 0.0f, 1.0f) * 255.0f);
                m_textureData[i * 4 + 1] = static_cast<uint8_t>(glm::clamp(color.g, 0.0f, 1.0f) * 255.0f);
                m_textureData[i * 4 + 2] = static_cast<uint8_t>(glm::clamp(color.b, 0.0f, 1.0f) * 255.0f);
                m_textureData[i * 4 + 3] = static_cast<uint8_t>(glm::clamp(color.a, 0.0f, 1.0f) * 255.0f);
                break;
            }
        }
    }
}

void ColorMapping::CreateOrUpdateTransferTexture()
{
    if (m_customColorMapping)
    {
        CreateCustomColorMappingData();
    }
    else
    {
        ApplyPredefinedColorMapping();
    }

    if (!TransferTexture.has_value())
    {
        TransferStagingBuffer.emplace(m_context, m_textureData.size() * sizeof(uint8_t),
                                      vk::BufferUsageFlagBits::eTransferSrc, VMA_MEMORY_USAGE_CPU_ONLY);
        TransferStagingBuffer->CopyData(m_textureData.data());
        TransferTexture.emplace(m_context, *TransferStagingBuffer, 256, 1, 1, vk::Format::eR8G8B8A8Unorm);
        TransferTexture->TransitionImageLayout(vk::ImageLayout::eShaderReadOnlyOptimal);
    }
    else
    {
        TransferStagingBuffer->CopyData(m_textureData.data());
        TransferTexture->UpdateImageBuffer(*TransferStagingBuffer);
    }
}

void ColorMapping::LoadPredefinedColorMapping()
{
    if (!std::filesystem::exists(m_colorMapsPath))
    {
        std::cout << "Color mapping file not found: " << m_colorMapsPath << std::endl;
        return;
    }
    for (const auto &entry : std::filesystem::recursive_directory_iterator(m_colorMapsPath))
    {
        if (entry.is_regular_file() && entry.path().extension() == ".npy")
        {
            npy::npy_data data = npy::read_npy<float>(entry.path().generic_string());
            if (data.shape.size() == 2 && data.shape[1] == 4 && data.shape[0] == 256)
            {
                PredefinedColorMappingNames.push_back(entry.path().stem().string());
                PredefinedColorMappings.insert(PredefinedColorMappings.end(), data.data.begin(), data.data.end());
                if (entry.path().stem() == m_initialColorMap)
                {
                    selectedIndex = PredefinedColorMappingNames.size() - 1;
                }
            }
        }
    }
    if (PredefinedColorMappingNames.empty() || PredefinedColorMappings.empty())
    {
        throw std::runtime_error("No valid color mapping files found.");
    }
}

void ColorMapping::ApplyPredefinedColorMapping()
{
    size_t offset = selectedIndex * 256 * 4;
    m_textureData.resize(256 * 4);
    std::transform(PredefinedColorMappings.begin() + offset, PredefinedColorMappings.begin() + offset + 256 * 4,
                   m_textureData.begin(), [](const auto &value) { return static_cast<uint8_t>(value * 255.0f); });

    // apply alpha
    for (int i = 0; i < 256; ++i)
    {
        float  t          = (static_cast<float>(i) / 255.0f) * (AlphaPoints.size() - 1);
        size_t leftIndex  = static_cast<size_t>(t);
        size_t rightIndex = std::min(leftIndex + 1, AlphaPoints.size() - 1);

        float alphaLeft  = AlphaPoints[leftIndex];
        float alphaRight = AlphaPoints[rightIndex];

        float  alpha         = alphaLeft + (alphaRight - alphaLeft) * (t - leftIndex);
        size_t index         = i * 4 + 3;
        m_textureData[index] = static_cast<uint8_t>(alpha * 255.0f);
    }
}

//-------------------------------------------------------------
// Custom draing
//-------------------------------------------------------------

inline ImVec2 operator+(const ImVec2 &lhs, const ImVec2 &rhs)
{
    return ImVec2(lhs.x + rhs.x, lhs.y + rhs.y);
}
inline ImVec2 operator-(const ImVec2 &lhs, const ImVec2 &rhs)
{
    return ImVec2(lhs.x - rhs.x, lhs.y - rhs.y);
}

bool ColorMapping::DrawAlphaEditor()
{
    if (ImGui::CollapsingHeader("Alpha Editor"))
    {
        ImVec2 canvas_size = ImVec2(300, 100);
        bool   updated     = false;

        std::vector<ImVec2> points;
        points.reserve(AlphaPoints.size());
        float offsetX = canvas_size.x / (AlphaPoints.size() - 1);
        for (size_t i = 0; i < AlphaPoints.size(); ++i)
        {
            points.push_back(ImVec2(offsetX * i, canvas_size.y - AlphaPoints[i] * canvas_size.y));
        }

        ImVec2 canvas_pos = ImGui::GetCursorScreenPos();
        ImRect bb(canvas_pos, canvas_pos + canvas_size);
        ImGui::ItemSize(bb);
        ImGui::ItemAdd(bb, 0);

        ImVec2 origin     = ImGui::GetItemRectMin();
        ImVec2 canvas_end = ImGui::GetItemRectMax();

        ImDrawList *draw_list = ImGui::GetWindowDrawList();
        draw_list->AddRectFilled(origin, canvas_end, IM_COL32(30, 30, 30, 255));
        draw_list->AddRect(origin, canvas_end, IM_COL32(200, 200, 200, 255));

        // Connect points
        for (size_t i = 0; i + 1 < points.size(); ++i)
        {
            ImVec2 p1 = ImVec2(origin.x + points[i].x, origin.y + points[i].y);
            ImVec2 p2 = ImVec2(origin.x + points[i + 1].x, origin.y + points[i + 1].y);
            draw_list->AddLine(p1, p2, IM_COL32(0, 255, 0, 255), 2.0f);
        }

        // Handle dragging
        ImGuiIO &io = ImGui::GetIO();
        for (size_t i = 0; i < points.size(); ++i)
        {
            ImVec2 &p          = points[i];
            ImVec2  screen_pos = ImVec2(origin.x + p.x, origin.y + p.y);

            draw_list->AddCircleFilled(screen_pos, 6.0f, IM_COL32(255, 255, 255, 255));

            ImGui::SetCursorScreenPos(ImVec2(screen_pos.x - 6, screen_pos.y - 6));
            ImGui::InvisibleButton(("pt" + std::to_string(i)).c_str(), ImVec2(12, 12));

            if (ImGui::IsItemActive() && ImGui::IsMouseDragging(ImGuiMouseButton_Left))
            {
                ImVec2 delta = io.MouseDelta;
                p.y += delta.y;
                p.y            = ImClamp(p.y, 0.0f, canvas_size.y);
                AlphaPoints[i] = 1 - p.y / canvas_size.y;
                updated        = true;
            }
        }

        ImGui::SetCursorScreenPos(ImVec2(canvas_pos.x, canvas_pos.y + canvas_size.y + 10));
        return updated;
    }
    return false;
}

void ColorMapping::DrawCustomColorMappingUI()
{
    // Draw control points editor
    for (int i = 0; i < ControlPoints.size(); ++i)
    {
        ImGui::PushID(i);
        ImGui::ColorEdit4("Color", (float *) &ControlPoints[i], ImGuiColorEditFlags_NoInputs);
        ImGui::SliderFloat("Position", &Positions[i], 0.0f, 1.0f);
        ImGui::SliderFloat("Alpha", &ControlPoints[i].w, 0.0f, 1.0f);
        if (ImGui::Button("Remove"))
        {
            ControlPoints.erase(ControlPoints.begin() + i);
            Positions.erase(Positions.begin() + i);
            --i;
        }
        ImGui::PopID();
    }

    if (ImGui::Button("Add Point"))
    {
        ControlPoints.push_back({1.0f, 1.0f, 1.0f, 1.0f});
        Positions.push_back(1.0f);
    }

    // Ensure positions are sorted
    for (int i = 1; i < Positions.size(); ++i)
    {
        if (Positions[i] < Positions[i - 1])
            Positions[i] = Positions[i - 1];
    }
}

void ColorMapping::DrawTextureDataPreview(bool customColorMapping)
{
    ImVec2      gradientSize = ImVec2(ImGui::GetContentRegionAvail().x, 32.0f);
    ImDrawList *drawList     = ImGui::GetWindowDrawList();
    ImVec2      p            = ImGui::GetCursorScreenPos();

    auto drawGradientRect = [&](int x, const ImVec4 &color) {
        drawList->AddRectFilled(ImVec2(p.x + x, p.y), ImVec2(p.x + x + 1, p.y + gradientSize.y), ImColor(color));
    };

    if (customColorMapping)
    {
        for (int x = 0; x < (int) gradientSize.x; ++x)
        {
            float t = x / gradientSize.x;

            size_t idx = 0;
            while (idx + 1 < Positions.size() && t > Positions[idx + 1])
                ++idx;

            float localT = (t - Positions[idx]) / (Positions[idx + 1] - Positions[idx]);

            glm::vec4 c0    = ControlPoints[idx];
            glm::vec4 c1    = ControlPoints[idx + 1];
            ImVec4    color = ImLerp(ImVec4(c0.x, c0.y, c0.z, c0.w), ImVec4(c1.x, c1.y, c1.z, c1.w), localT);

            drawGradientRect(x, color);
        }
    }
    else
    {
        if (m_textureData.empty())
        {
            ImGui::Text("No texture data available.");
            return;
        }

        for (int x = 0; x < (int) gradientSize.x; ++x)
        {
            float t     = x / gradientSize.x;
            int   index = static_cast<int>(t * 255.0f) * 4;

            if (index + 3 < m_textureData.size())
            {
                ImVec4 color = ImVec4(m_textureData[index + 0] / 255.0f, m_textureData[index + 1] / 255.0f,
                                      m_textureData[index + 2] / 255.0f, m_textureData[index + 3] / 255.0f);

                drawGradientRect(x, color);
            }
        }
    }

    ImGui::Dummy(gradientSize);
}

#pragma once

#include <filesystem>

#include "glm/glm.hpp"

/**
 * @brief Color mapping class for handling color maps and transfer functions.
 */
class ColorMapping
{
  public:
    ColorMapping(GFX::VkContext &context, const std::filesystem::path &colorMapsPath,
                 const std::string &initialColorMap);

    void CreateOrUpdateTransferTexture();

    // UI
    void DrawUI();

  private:
    void CreateCustomColorMappingData();
    void LoadPredefinedColorMapping();
    void ApplyPredefinedColorMapping();
    bool DrawAlphaEditor();
    void DrawCustomColorMappingUI();

  public:
    // custom color mapping data
    std::vector<glm::vec4> ControlPoints = {
        {0.0f, 0.0f, 0.3f, 1.0f},        // deep blue
        {0.0f, 0.5f, 1.0f, 1.0f},        // light blue
        {0.2f, 1.0f, 0.2f, 1.0f},        // green
        {1.0f, 0.2f, 0.2f, 1.0f},        // red
        {1.0f, 0.5f, 0.2f, 1.0f}         // orange
    };
    std::vector<float> Positions = {0.0f, 0.25f, 0.5f, 0.75f, 1.0f};

    std::vector<float> AlphaPoints = {0.7f, 0.7f, 0.7f, 0.7f, 0.7f};

    int                      selectedIndex = 0;
    std::vector<std::string> PredefinedColorMappingNames;
    std::vector<float>       PredefinedColorMappings;

    std::optional<GFX::VkImageObject>  TransferTexture;

  private:
    GFX::VkContext             &m_context;
    std::vector<uint8_t>        m_textureData;
    const std::filesystem::path m_colorMapsPath;
    bool                        m_customColorMapping = false;
    const std::string          &m_initialColorMap;
};

### renderer/dvr.cpp
```
#include "dvr.h"
#include "denoisers/bilateral_filter.h"
#include "denoisers/gaussian_filter.h"
#include "denoisers/lstm_denoiser.h"
#include "denoisers/lstm_denoiser_gpu.h"
#include "denoisers/mean_filter.h"
#include "denoisers/median_filter.h"
#include "denoisers/oidn_denoiser.h"
#include "denoisers/oidn_denoiser_gpu.h"
#include "denoisers/temporal_rls.h"
#include "denoisers/temporal_weighted_mean.h"
#include "denoisers/total_variation.h"
#include "denoisers/unet_denoiser.h"
#include "denoisers/unet_denoiser_gpu.h"
#include "denoisers/wiener_filter.h"
#include "utils/container.h"
#include "utils/utils.h"

DVR::DVR(GFX::GfxConfig &graphicsConfig, AppConfig &config) : m_graphicsConfig(graphicsConfig), m_config(config)
{
    m_swapchain.emplace(m_context, m_window);

    // initialize data
    SetupCamera();
    LoadVolumetricData();
    m_generalUbos.stepSize         = m_config.VolumetricConfig.stepSize;
    m_generalUbos.motionVectorCalc = m_config.InternalConfig.lstmDatasetExport ? 1 : 0;

    UpdateBoundingBox();
    auto minMax                = std::minmax_element(m_volumeData3D.Data.begin(), m_volumeData3D.Data.end());
    m_generalUbos.scalarMinMax = {*minMax.first, *minMax.second};
    Utils::CreateAndCopyBuffer(m_context, &m_generalUbos, sizeof(GeneralUbos),
                               vk::BufferUsageFlagBits::eUniformBuffer | vk::BufferUsageFlagBits::eTransferDst,
                               VMA_MEMORY_USAGE_GPU_ONLY, m_generalUbosBuffer);
    m_generalUbosBuffer->SetName("General Buffer");

    // prepare render passes
    InitRaymarchingPass();

    if (m_denoiserType != 0)
    {
        InitDenoiserPass(m_denoiserType);
        m_config.NoiseConfig.varyingStepSize = true;
        m_noise.OnVaryStepSize();
        m_noise.UpdateNoiseTexture();
    }

    InitOutputPass();
    m_computeCommandBuffer.emplace(m_context);
    m_graphicsCommandBuffer.emplace(m_context);

    // setup synchronization
    for (int i = 0; i < m_context.MaxFramesInFlight; ++i)
    {
        m_imageAvailableSemaphores.emplace_back(m_context.Device.createSemaphore(vk::SemaphoreCreateInfo{}));
    }

    // initialize user interface
    m_userInterface.emplace(m_context, m_window, *m_outputRenderPass);
    m_userInterface->LoadTheme(config.UIThemePath);

    startTime = ImGui::GetTime();
}

void DVR::LoadVolumetricData()
{
    m_volumeHandler.LoadVolumeData<float>(m_config.VolumetricConfig.volumetricPath);
    m_volumeData3D           = m_volumeHandler.VolumeFrames[m_volumeHandler.FrameIndex];
    m_generalUbos.dimensions = glm::vec3(m_volumeData3D.Shape[1], m_volumeData3D.Shape[2], m_volumeData3D.Shape[0]);
    m_generalUbos.bVolume4D  = m_volumeHandler.IsTimeVaryingVolume ? 1 : 0;

    GFX::VkBufferObject stagingBuffer(m_context, m_volumeData3D.Data.size() * sizeof(float),
                                      vk::BufferUsageFlagBits::eTransferSrc, VMA_MEMORY_USAGE_CPU_ONLY);
    stagingBuffer.CopyData(m_volumeData3D.Data.data());

    m_volumeData3DImage.emplace(m_context, stagingBuffer, m_volumeData3D.Shape[1], m_volumeData3D.Shape[2],
                                m_volumeData3D.Shape[0], vk::Format::eR32Sfloat);
    m_volumeData3DImage->SetName("Volume Data 3D Image");

    m_volumeData3DNextFrame =
        m_volumeHandler
            .VolumeFrames[(m_volumeHandler.FrameIndex + 1) % static_cast<int>(m_volumeHandler.VolumeFrames.size())];
    GFX::VkBufferObject stagingBufferNextFrame(m_context, m_volumeData3DNextFrame.Data.size() * sizeof(float),
                                               vk::BufferUsageFlagBits::eTransferSrc, VMA_MEMORY_USAGE_CPU_ONLY);
    stagingBufferNextFrame.CopyData(m_volumeData3DNextFrame.Data.data());
    m_volumeData3DNextFrameImage.emplace(m_context, stagingBufferNextFrame, m_volumeData3DNextFrame.Shape[1],
                                         m_volumeData3DNextFrame.Shape[2], m_volumeData3DNextFrame.Shape[0],
                                         vk::Format::eR32Sfloat);

    if (m_volumeHandler.IsTimeVaryingVolume)
    {
        if (m_config.VolumetricConfig.timeStep4D > 0.0f)
        {
            m_timeStep = m_config.VolumetricConfig.timeStep4D;
        }
        else
        {
            std::cerr << "Warning: Invalid time step for 4D volume. Using default value of 0.1.\n";
            m_timeStep = 0.1f;
        }
    }
}

void DVR::SetupCamera()
{
    m_camera.UpdateDeviceData();
    m_camera.camDeviceData.SetupProjection(m_config, m_graphicsConfig);
    m_camera.CreateCameraDeviceBuffer(m_context);
    m_camera.CreatePreviousCameraDeviceBuffer(m_context);
    m_camera.IsRecordingFrame = m_config.CameraConfig.recordingCapture;
}

void DVR::InitRaymarchingPass()
{
    // update noise texture
    if (m_config.NoiseConfig.varyingStepSize)
    {
        m_noise.OnVaryStepSize();
    }
    else
    {
        m_noise.OnNonVaryStepSize();
    }

    // initialize textures for color mapping
    m_colorMapping.CreateOrUpdateTransferTexture();

    auto computeShaderCode = Utils::ReadFile(m_config.RaymarchingShaderPath);

    // non-noisy output image
    m_rawOutputImage.emplace(m_context, m_graphicsConfig.WindowWidth, m_graphicsConfig.WindowHeight, 1,
                             vk::Format::eR8G8B8A8Unorm, true);
    m_rawOutputImage->TransitionImageLayout(vk::ImageLayout::eGeneral);
    m_rawOutputImage->SetName("Non-noisy Output Image");

    // temporal
    m_positionDepthBufferImage.emplace(m_context, m_graphicsConfig.WindowWidth, m_graphicsConfig.WindowHeight, 1,
                                       vk::Format::eR32G32B32A32Sfloat);
    m_positionDepthBufferImage->TransitionImageLayout(vk::ImageLayout::eGeneral);
    m_positionDepthBufferImage->SetName("Current Position Depth Buffer Image");
    m_motionVectorImage.emplace(m_context, m_graphicsConfig.WindowWidth, m_graphicsConfig.WindowHeight, 1,
                                vk::Format::eR8G8B8A8Unorm, true);
    m_motionVectorImage->TransitionImageLayout(vk::ImageLayout::eGeneral);
    m_motionVectorImage->SetName("Motion Vector Image");

    // descriptorset input
    GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::ElementUniformBuffer generalDescriptorElement{*m_generalUbosBuffer};
    GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::ElementUniformBuffer cameraDescriptorElement{*m_camera.CameraDeviceBuffer};
    GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::ElementStorageBuffer previousCameraDescriptorElement{
        *m_camera.PreviousCameraDeviceBuffer};

    GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::ElementStorageImage noiseDescriptorImage{*m_noise.NoiseTexture};
    GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::ElementStorageImage outputDescriptorImage{*m_rawOutputImage};
    GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::ElementStorageImage positionDepthImage{*m_positionDepthBufferImage};
    GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::ElementStorageImage motionVectorImage{*m_motionVectorImage};

    GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::ElementImageSampler volumetricDescriptorElement{*m_volumeData3DImage};
    GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::ElementImageSampler volumetric4DNextFrameDescriptorElement{
        *m_volumeData3DNextFrameImage};
    GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::ElementImageSampler colorMappingDescriptorElement{*m_colorMapping.TransferTexture};

    m_rmDynamicDescriptorSet.emplace(m_context, std::vector<GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::IDescriptorElement *>{
                                                    &generalDescriptorElement,
                                                    &cameraDescriptorElement,
                                                    &noiseDescriptorImage,
                                                    &colorMappingDescriptorElement,
                                                });

    m_rmStaticDescriptorSet.emplace(m_context, std::vector<GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::IDescriptorElement *>{
                                                   &outputDescriptorImage,
                                                   &volumetricDescriptorElement,
                                                   &volumetric4DNextFrameDescriptorElement,
                                                   &positionDepthImage,
                                                   &previousCameraDescriptorElement,
                                                   &motionVectorImage,
                                               });

    m_rmPass.emplace(m_context, computeShaderCode,
                     std::vector<vk::DescriptorSetLayout>{m_rmDynamicDescriptorSet->DescriptorSetLayout,
                                                          m_rmStaticDescriptorSet->DescriptorSetLayout});

    auto motionVectorShaderCode = Utils::ReadFile(m_config.MotionVectorPostProcessingPath);

    GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::ElementStorageImage motionVectorOutputImage{*m_motionVectorImage};

    m_motionVectorDescriptorSet.emplace(m_context, std::vector<GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::IDescriptorElement *>{
                                                       &cameraDescriptorElement,
                                                       &previousCameraDescriptorElement,
                                                   });

    m_motionVectorPass.emplace(m_context, motionVectorShaderCode,
                               std::vector<vk::DescriptorSetLayout>{m_motionVectorDescriptorSet->DescriptorSetLayout});
}

void DVR::InitDenoiserPass(int &denoiserType)
{
    m_generalUbos.accumulation     = false;
    m_generalUbos.motionVectorCalc = m_config.InternalConfig.lstmDatasetExport ? 1 : 0;
    m_noise.PerFrameUpdate         = false;

    switch (denoiserType)
    {
        case 0:
            m_noise.PerFrameUpdate  = m_config.InternalConfig.lstmDatasetExport;
            m_denoiser              = nullptr;
            m_config.ExportFilePath = to_string(m_noise) + ".jpg";
            break;
        case 1:
            m_denoiser              = std::make_unique<MeanFilter>(m_context, m_config, *m_noise.NoiseTexture);
            m_config.ExportFilePath = to_string(m_noise) + "_meanfilter.jpg";
            std::cout << "Mean filter denoiser\n";
            break;
        case 2:
            m_denoiser              = std::make_unique<GaussianFilter>(m_context, m_config);
            m_config.ExportFilePath = to_string(m_noise) + "_gaussianfilter.jpg";
            std::cout << "Gaussian filter denoiser\n";
            break;
        case 3:
            m_denoiser              = std::make_unique<BilateralFilter>(m_context, m_config);
            m_config.ExportFilePath = to_string(m_noise) + "_bilateralfilter.jpg";
            std::cout << "Bilateral filter denoiser\n";
            break;
        case 4:
            m_denoiser              = std::make_unique<MedianFilter>(m_context, m_config);
            m_config.ExportFilePath = to_string(m_noise) + "_medianfilter.jpg";
            std::cout << "Median filter denoiser\n";
            break;
        case 5:
            m_denoiser              = std::make_unique<TotalVariation>(m_context, m_config);
            m_config.ExportFilePath = to_string(m_noise) + "_totalvariation.jpg";
            std::cout << "Total variation denoiser\n";
            break;
        case 6:
            m_denoiser              = std::make_unique<WienerFilter>(m_context, m_config, m_noise);
            m_config.ExportFilePath = to_string(m_noise) + "_wienerfilter.jpg";
            std::cout << "Wiener filter denoiser\n";
            break;
        case 7:
            m_denoiser = std::make_unique<TemporalWMDenoiser>(m_context, m_config, *m_positionDepthBufferImage,
                                                              *m_noise.NoiseTexture, m_camera);
            m_config.ExportFilePath = to_string(m_noise) + "_temporalweightedmean.jpg";
            if (m_config.Denoiser.TemporalWeightedMean.perFrameUpdate)
            {
                m_noise.PerFrameUpdate = true;
            }
            m_generalUbos.motionVectorCalc = true;
            std::cout << "Temporal denoiser\n";
            break;
        case 8:
            m_denoiser =
                std::make_unique<RLSDenoiser>(m_context, m_config, *m_positionDepthBufferImage, m_noise, m_camera);
            m_config.ExportFilePath = to_string(m_noise) + "_rls.jpg";
            if (m_config.Denoiser.TemporalRLS.perFrameUpdate)
            {
                m_noise.PerFrameUpdate = true;
            }
            m_generalUbos.motionVectorCalc = true;
            std::cout << "RLS temporal denoiser\n";
            break;
        case 9:
#ifdef OIDN_ON
#ifndef __APPLE__        // vulkan external memory is not supported on Apple platforms, hence gpu->cpu needed
            if (m_config.Denoiser.OIDN.gpu)
            {
                m_denoiser = std::make_unique<OIDN_DenoiserGPU>(m_context, m_config);
            }
            else
#endif
            {
                m_denoiser = std::make_unique<OIDN_Denoiser>(m_context, m_config);
            }
            m_config.ExportFilePath = to_string(m_noise) + "_oidn.jpg";
            std::cout << "Open Image Denoise denoiser\n";
#else
            std::cout << "Open Image Denoise is not built, using no denoiser\n";
            m_denoiser = nullptr;
#endif
            break;
        case 10:
#ifdef TRAINED_DENOISER_ON
            if (m_config.Denoiser.UNet.cudaProcess)
            {
                m_denoiser = std::make_unique<UNetDenoiserGPU>(m_context, m_config, m_noise);
                std::cout
                    << "Currently unstable!!! Please expect some bugs, especially when setting options changed!\n";
            }
            else
            {
                m_denoiser = std::make_unique<UNetDenoiser>(m_context, m_config, m_noise);
            }
            m_config.ExportFilePath = to_string(m_noise) + "_unetdenoiser.jpg";
            std::cout << "Trained UNet denoiser\n";
#else
            std::cout << "Trained UNet is not built, using no denoiser\n";
            m_denoiser = nullptr;
#endif
            break;
        case 11:
#ifdef TRAINED_DENOISER_ON
            m_noise.PerFrameUpdate         = true;
            m_generalUbos.motionVectorCalc = true;
            if (m_config.Denoiser.LSTM.cudaProcess)
            {
                m_denoiser = std::make_unique<LSTMDenoiserGPU>(m_context, m_config, m_noise, *m_motionVectorImage);
                std::cout
                    << "Currently unstable!!! Please expect some bugs, especially when setting options changed!\n";
            }
            else
            {
                m_denoiser = std::make_unique<LSTMDenoiser>(m_context, m_config, m_noise, *m_motionVectorImage);
            }
            m_config.ExportFilePath = to_string(m_noise) + "_lstmdenoiser.jpg";
            std::cout << "Trained LSTM denoiser\n";
#else
            std::cout << "Trained LSTM is not built, using no denoiser\n";
            m_denoiser = nullptr;
#endif
            break;
        default:
            std::cout << "Unknown denoiser type\n";
            return;
    }

    if (m_denoiser)
    {
        m_denoiser->InitPass(*m_rawOutputImage);
    }

    g_DirtyBuffers.insert({m_generalUbosBuffer->Buffer, 0, sizeof(GeneralUbos), &m_generalUbos});

    if (m_noise.PerFrameUpdate)
    {
        m_noise.SetEllapsedTime(&m_generalUbos.ellapsedTime);
        m_noise.InitPass();
    }
}

void DVR::InitOutputPass()
{
    auto vertexShaderCode   = Utils::ReadFile(m_config.OutputVertexShaderPath);
    auto fragmentShaderCode = Utils::ReadFile(m_config.OutputFragmentShaderPath);

    GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::ElementImageSampler outputSampler{m_denoiser == nullptr ? *m_rawOutputImage :
                                                                                          *m_denoiser->OutputImage};

    GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::ElementUniformBuffer cameraDescriptorElement{*m_camera.CameraDeviceBuffer};
    GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::ElementStorageBuffer previousCameraDescriptorElement{
        *m_camera.PreviousCameraDeviceBuffer};

    m_outputDescriptorSet.emplace(m_context,
                                  std::vector<GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::IDescriptorElement *>{
                                      &outputSampler, &cameraDescriptorElement, &previousCameraDescriptorElement});

    m_outputRenderPass.emplace(m_context, vertexShaderCode, fragmentShaderCode, *m_swapchain,
                               std::vector<vk::DescriptorSetLayout>{m_outputDescriptorSet->DescriptorSetLayout});
}

void DVR::Run()
{
    m_window.ShowWindow();

    while (!m_window.ShouldClose())
    {
        m_window.Update();

        float deltaTime = ImGui::GetIO().DeltaTime;
        m_timeCounter += deltaTime;
        m_generalUbos.interpolationFactor = std::clamp(m_timeCounter / m_timeStep, 0.0f, 1.0f);

        if (m_timeCounter >= m_timeStep && m_generalUbos.interpolationFactor >= 1.0f &&
            m_volumeHandler.IsTimeVaryingVolume)
        {
            UpdateVolume();
            m_timeCounter                     = 0.0f;
            m_generalUbos.interpolationFactor = 0.0f;
        }

        UpdateUI();
        HandleInput();

        StartNewFrame();
        RecordCommands();
        SubmitCommands();
        PresentOutput();

        m_currentFrame = (m_currentFrame + 1) % m_context.MaxFramesInFlight;
    }
    m_context.Device.waitIdle();
}

void DVR::UpdateVolume()
{
    m_volumeHandler.UpdateFrameIndex();
    if (m_generalUbos.pingpong4D == 0)
    {
        m_volumeData3D =
            m_volumeHandler
                .VolumeFrames[(m_volumeHandler.FrameIndex + 1) % static_cast<int>(m_volumeHandler.VolumeFrames.size())];
        GFX::VkBufferObject stagingBuffer(m_context, m_volumeData3D.Data.size() * sizeof(float),
                                          vk::BufferUsageFlagBits::eTransferSrc, VMA_MEMORY_USAGE_CPU_ONLY);
        stagingBuffer.CopyData(m_volumeData3D.Data.data());
        m_volumeData3DImage->TransitionImageLayout(vk::ImageLayout::eTransferDstOptimal);
        m_volumeData3DImage->CopyBufferToImage(stagingBuffer);
        m_volumeData3DImage->TransitionImageLayout(vk::ImageLayout::eShaderReadOnlyOptimal);
        m_generalUbos.pingpong4D = 1;
    }
    else
    {
        m_volumeData3DNextFrame =
            m_volumeHandler
                .VolumeFrames[(m_volumeHandler.FrameIndex + 1) % static_cast<int>(m_volumeHandler.VolumeFrames.size())];
        GFX::VkBufferObject stagingBufferNextFrame(m_context, m_volumeData3DNextFrame.Data.size() * sizeof(float),
                                                   vk::BufferUsageFlagBits::eTransferSrc, VMA_MEMORY_USAGE_CPU_ONLY);
        stagingBufferNextFrame.CopyData(m_volumeData3DNextFrame.Data.data());
        m_volumeData3DNextFrameImage->TransitionImageLayout(vk::ImageLayout::eTransferDstOptimal);
        m_volumeData3DNextFrameImage->CopyBufferToImage(stagingBufferNextFrame);
        m_volumeData3DNextFrameImage->TransitionImageLayout(vk::ImageLayout::eShaderReadOnlyOptimal);
        m_generalUbos.pingpong4D = 0;
    }
}

void DVR::UpdateUI()
{
    // general informations
    m_generalUbos.ellapsedTime = ImGui::GetTime() - startTime;
    g_DirtyBuffers.insert({m_generalUbosBuffer->Buffer, 0, sizeof(GeneralUbos), &m_generalUbos});

    m_userInterface->StartUI();
    ImGui::Text("File name: %s", m_config.VolumetricConfig.volumetricPath
                                     .substr(m_config.VolumetricConfig.volumetricPath.find_last_of('/') + 1)
                                     .c_str());
    m_userInterface->DrawVolumePropertyUI(m_generalUbos.dimensions, m_generalUbos.bbMin, m_generalUbos.bbMax,
                                          m_volumeData3D.MinValue, m_volumeData3D.MaxValue);
    ImGui::Text("Ellapsed time: %.2f seconds", m_generalUbos.ellapsedTime);
    if (m_volumeHandler.IsTimeVaryingVolume)
    {
        ImGui::Text("Frame: %d / %d", m_volumeHandler.FrameIndex,
                    static_cast<int>(m_volumeHandler.VolumeFrames.size()));
    }

    // unit length
    ImGui::SeparatorText("Settings");
    if (m_userInterface->DrawDistanceUI(m_config.VolumetricConfig.volumeUnitLength))
    {
        UpdateBoundingBox();
        g_DirtyBuffers.insert({m_generalUbosBuffer->Buffer, 0, sizeof(GeneralUbos), &m_generalUbos});
    }

    // rotation
    if (ImGui::SliderFloat2("Rotation Adjustment", &m_generalUbos.rotationAngles.x, -6, 6))
    {
        g_DirtyBuffers.insert({m_generalUbosBuffer->Buffer, 0, sizeof(GeneralUbos), &m_generalUbos});
    }

    // step sizes
    if (m_userInterface->DrawStepSizeUI(m_config.VolumetricConfig.stepSize))
    {
        m_generalUbos.stepSize = m_config.VolumetricConfig.stepSize;
        g_DirtyBuffers.insert({m_generalUbosBuffer->Buffer, 0, sizeof(GeneralUbos), &m_generalUbos});
    }

    // density
    if (m_userInterface->DrawIlluminationScaleUI(m_generalUbos.illuminationScale))
    {
        g_DirtyBuffers.insert({m_generalUbosBuffer->Buffer, 0, sizeof(GeneralUbos), &m_generalUbos});
    }

    // color mapping
    m_colorMapping.DrawUI();

    // vary step size
    if (m_noise.DrawNoiseUI())
    {
        // denoiser should be updated if vary step size bool is changed
        OnDenoiserMethodChanged();
    }

    // denoiser configuraitons
    if (m_config.NoiseConfig.varyingStepSize)
    {
        if (m_userInterface->DrawDenoiserSelectionUI(m_denoisersName, m_denoiserType))
        {
            OnDenoiserMethodChanged();
        }

        if (m_denoiser)
        {
            m_denoiser->DrawDenoiserUI();
        }
    }

    // export
    ImGui::SeparatorText("Export options");
    ImGui::InputText("Export path", m_config.ExportFilePath.data(), m_config.ExportFilePath.size() + 1,
                     ImGuiInputTextFlags_CallbackResize, UserInterface::InputTextCallback, &m_config.ExportFilePath);
    if (ImGui::Button("Export"))
    {
        ExportOutput();
        std::cout << "Exported image to " << m_config.ExportFilePath << std::endl;
    }

    m_camera.DrawCameraRoutineUI();
    m_userInterface->EndUI();
}

void DVR::StartNewFrame()
{
    vk::Result result =
        m_context.Device.waitForFences({m_swapchain->InFlightFences[m_currentFrame]}, VK_TRUE, UINT64_MAX);
    if (result != vk::Result::eSuccess)
    {
        throw std::runtime_error("Failed to wait for fence");
    }

    auto [acquireResult, imageIndex] =
        m_swapchain->Swapchain.acquireNextImage(UINT64_MAX, *m_imageAvailableSemaphores[m_currentFrame], nullptr);
    this->m_imageIndex = imageIndex;
    if (acquireResult != vk::Result::eSuccess)
        throw std::runtime_error("Failed to acquire next image, resize not implemented!");

    m_computeCommandBuffer->CommandBuffers[m_currentFrame].reset(vk::CommandBufferResetFlagBits::eReleaseResources);
    m_context.Device.resetFences({m_swapchain->InFlightFences[m_currentFrame]});
}

void DVR::RecordCommands()
{
    m_computeCommandBuffer->StartRecord(m_currentFrame);
    m_graphicsCommandBuffer->StartRecord(m_currentFrame);

    BufferContainer::UpdateDirtyBuffers(m_computeCommandBuffer->GetCommandBuffer());

    // compute pass
    if (m_noise.PerFrameUpdate && m_config.NoiseConfig.varyingStepSize)
    {
        m_noise.RecordPass(*m_computeCommandBuffer, m_currentFrame);
    }

    m_computeCommandBuffer->StartComputePass(*m_rmPass);
    m_computeCommandBuffer->BindDescriptorSet(*m_rmPass, {m_rmDynamicDescriptorSet->DescriptorSets[m_currentFrame],
                                                          m_rmStaticDescriptorSet->DescriptorSets[m_currentFrame]});
    m_computeCommandBuffer->Dispatch((m_graphicsConfig.WindowWidth + 15) / 16,
                                     (m_graphicsConfig.WindowHeight + 15) / 16, 1);

    // denoiser pass
    if (m_denoiser)
    {
        m_denoiser->RecordPass(*m_computeCommandBuffer, m_currentFrame);
    }

    if (m_generalUbos.motionVectorCalc)
    {
        m_computeCommandBuffer->StartComputePass(*m_motionVectorPass);
        m_computeCommandBuffer->BindDescriptorSet(*m_motionVectorPass,
                                                  {m_motionVectorDescriptorSet->DescriptorSets[m_currentFrame]});
        m_computeCommandBuffer->Dispatch((m_graphicsConfig.WindowWidth + 15) / 16,
                                         (m_graphicsConfig.WindowHeight + 15) / 16, 1);
    }

    // graphics pass
    m_graphicsCommandBuffer->StartGraphicsPass(*m_outputRenderPass, m_currentFrame);
    m_graphicsCommandBuffer->BindDescriptorSet(*m_outputRenderPass,
                                               {m_outputDescriptorSet->DescriptorSets[m_currentFrame]});
    m_graphicsCommandBuffer->Draw(3, 1, 0, 0);
    m_userInterface->Record(m_graphicsCommandBuffer->CommandBuffers[m_currentFrame]);
    m_graphicsCommandBuffer->EndGraphicsPass();

    m_computeCommandBuffer->EndRecord();
    m_graphicsCommandBuffer->EndRecord();
}

void DVR::SubmitCommands() const
{
    // submit compute pass
    vk::SubmitInfo computeSubmitInfo{};
    computeSubmitInfo.commandBufferCount   = 1;
    computeSubmitInfo.pCommandBuffers      = &*m_computeCommandBuffer->CommandBuffers[m_currentFrame];
    computeSubmitInfo.signalSemaphoreCount = 1;
    computeSubmitInfo.pSignalSemaphores    = &*m_rmPass->Semaphores[m_currentFrame];
    m_context.ComputeQueue.submit(computeSubmitInfo);

    // submit graphics pass
    std::vector<vk::Semaphore>          waitSemaphores = {*m_imageAvailableSemaphores[m_currentFrame],
                                                          *m_rmPass->Semaphores[m_currentFrame]};
    std::vector<vk::PipelineStageFlags> waitStages     = {vk::PipelineStageFlagBits::eColorAttachmentOutput,
                                                          vk::PipelineStageFlagBits::eComputeShader};

    vk::SubmitInfo outputSubmitInfo{};
    outputSubmitInfo.commandBufferCount   = 1;
    outputSubmitInfo.pCommandBuffers      = &*m_graphicsCommandBuffer->CommandBuffers[m_currentFrame];
    outputSubmitInfo.waitSemaphoreCount   = 2;
    outputSubmitInfo.pWaitSemaphores      = waitSemaphores.data();
    outputSubmitInfo.pWaitDstStageMask    = waitStages.data();
    outputSubmitInfo.signalSemaphoreCount = 1;
    outputSubmitInfo.pSignalSemaphores    = &*m_outputRenderPass->Semaphores[m_imageIndex];
    m_context.GraphicsQueue.submit(outputSubmitInfo, m_swapchain->InFlightFences[m_currentFrame]);
}

void DVR::PresentOutput() const
{
    // present the graphics pass
    vk::PresentInfoKHR presentInfo{};
    presentInfo.waitSemaphoreCount = 1;
    presentInfo.pWaitSemaphores    = &*m_outputRenderPass->Semaphores[m_imageIndex];
    presentInfo.swapchainCount     = 1;
    presentInfo.pSwapchains        = &*m_swapchain->Swapchain;
    presentInfo.pImageIndices      = &m_imageIndex;

    vk::Result result = m_context.GraphicsQueue.presentKHR(presentInfo);
    if (result != vk::Result::eSuccess)
    {
        throw std::runtime_error("Failed to present swapchain image");
    }

#ifdef IMGUI_ENABLE_TEST_ENGINE
    m_userInterface->PostSwap();
#endif
}

void DVR::UpdateBoundingBox()
{
    std::tie(m_generalUbos.bbMin, m_generalUbos.bbMax) =
        m_volumeHandler.GetBoundingBox(m_config.VolumetricConfig.volumeUnitLength, m_generalUbos.dimensions);
}

void DVR::OnDenoiserMethodChanged()
{
    if (!m_config.NoiseConfig.varyingStepSize)
    {
        m_denoiserType = 0;
    }

    m_context.Device.waitIdle();

    InitDenoiserPass(m_denoiserType);
    InitOutputPass();
}

void DVR::HandleInput()
{
    if (m_camera.IsPlaying && m_config.CameraConfig.recordingCapture)
    {
        if (m_volumeHandler.IsTimeVaryingVolume && m_shouldReset)
        {
            m_volumeHandler.ForceUpdateFrameIndex(0);
            m_timeCounter = m_timeStep;        // force update
            m_shouldReset = false;
        }
        std::filesystem::path file = std::filesystem::path(m_camera.RecordingFilesFolder) /
                                     ("Frame" + std::to_string(m_camera.PlayingFrame) + ".jpg");
        ExportOutput(file.string());
    }
    else
    {
        m_shouldReset = true;
    }

    if (ImGui::GetIO().WantCaptureMouse)
    {
        m_window.ResetScroll();
        return;
    }

    bool  bufferUpdated     = false;
    int   moveSensitivity   = 10;
    float scrollSensitivity = 0.5f;

    // handle mouse drag input
    auto rotationX = -glm::radians(GFX::Window::DeltaX);
    auto rotationY = glm::radians(GFX::Window::DeltaY);

    if (rotationX != 0.0f || rotationY != 0.0f)
    {
        float sensitivity = moveSensitivity * ImGui::GetIO().DeltaTime * 100;
        auto &cameraData  = m_camera.cameraData;
        cameraData.yaw += rotationX * sensitivity;
        cameraData.pitch += rotationY * sensitivity;
        cameraData.pitch = std::clamp(cameraData.pitch, -89.0f, 89.0f);
        bufferUpdated    = true;
    }

    // mouse scroll input
    float scroll = m_window.GetScrollY();
    if (scroll != 0.0f)
    {
        auto &cameraData = m_camera.cameraData;
        cameraData.distance -= scroll * scrollSensitivity;
        cameraData.distance = std::clamp(cameraData.distance, 0.1f, 100.0f);
        bufferUpdated       = true;
    }

    // update camera buffer
    if (!m_camera.IsPlaying)
    {
        m_camera.camDeviceData.moved = bufferUpdated;
    }

    m_camera.UpdateDeviceData();
    g_DirtyBuffers.insert(
        {m_camera.CameraDeviceBuffer->Buffer, 0, sizeof(Camera::CamDeviceData), &m_camera.camDeviceData});
}

void DVR::ExportOutput(const std::string &filename)
{
    auto null_pos = m_config.ExportFilePath.find('\0');
    if (null_pos != std::string::npos)
    {
        m_config.ExportFilePath.resize(null_pos);
    }

    auto name = filename.empty() ? m_config.ExportFilePath : filename;

    if (m_denoiser != nullptr)
    {
        m_exporter.ExportImage(*m_denoiser->OutputImage, name);
    }
    else
    {
        m_exporter.ExportImage(*m_rawOutputImage, name);
    }

    if (m_config.NoiseConfig.varyingStepSize)
    {
        auto extractNoiseName = Utils::InsertStringBeforeExtension(name, "_noise");
        m_exporter.ExportImage(*m_noise.NoiseTexture, extractNoiseName);

        if (m_generalUbos.motionVectorCalc)
        {
            auto extractMotionVectorImageName = Utils::InsertStringBeforeExtension(name, "_motion_vector");
            m_exporter.ExportImage(*m_motionVectorImage, extractMotionVectorImageName);
        }
    }
}
```

### renderer/dvr.h
```
#pragma once

#include "app_config.h"
#include "camera.h"

#pragma once

/**
 * @brief Direct Volume Rendering (DVR) class
 */
class DVR
{
  public:
    DVR(GFX::GfxConfig &graphicsConfig, AppConfig &config);
    void Run();

  private:
    // Initialization
    void LoadVolumetricData();
    void SetupCamera();
    void InitRaymarchingPass();
    void InitDenoiserPass(int &denoiserType);
    void InitOutputPass();

    // Main loop
    void UpdateVolume();
    void UpdateUI();
    void StartNewFrame();
    void RecordCommands();
    void SubmitCommands() const;
    void PresentOutput() const;

    // Helpers
    void UpdateBoundingBox();
    void OnDenoiserMethodChanged();
    void HandleInput();
    void ExportOutput(const std::string &filename = {});

  private:
    GFX::GfxConfig &m_graphicsConfig;
    AppConfig      &m_config;

    // volume loader
    VolumeHandler m_volumeHandler;
    VolumeData3D  m_volumeData3D;
    VolumeData3D  m_volumeData3DNextFrame;        // only for 4D volume

    // general
    GFX::Window                           m_window{m_graphicsConfig};
    GFX::VkContext                        m_context{m_graphicsConfig, m_window};
    std::optional<GFX::VkSwapchainObject> m_swapchain;

    // general
    struct GeneralUbos
    {
        // volumetric
        alignas(16) glm::vec3 dimensions;
        alignas(16) glm::vec3 bbMin;
        alignas(16) glm::vec3 bbMax;

        // rotation adjustment of volumetric in the box
        alignas(8) glm::vec2 rotationAngles = {0, 0};
        alignas(8) glm::vec2 scalarMinMax;

        alignas(4) float stepSize;
        alignas(4) float illuminationScale = 1.0f;
        alignas(4) float ellapsedTime      = 0.0f;

        alignas(4) int accumulation     = false;        // change to false later
        alignas(4) int motionVectorCalc = false;

        // 4d volumes
        alignas(4) int bVolume4D             = false;
        alignas(4) float interpolationFactor = 0.0f;
        alignas(4) int pingpong4D            = 0;

    } m_generalUbos;

    std::optional<GFX::VkBufferObject> m_generalUbosBuffer;

    // time
    float startTime = 0.0f;

    Noise        m_noise{m_context, m_config};
    ColorMapping m_colorMapping{m_context, m_config.ColorMapsPath, m_config.ColorMap};

    std::unique_ptr<IDenoiser> m_denoiser;
    int                        m_denoiserType = 0;

    const std::vector<const char *> m_denoisersName = {
        "No denoiser",        "Mean Filter",   "Gaussian Filter",        "Bilateral Filter", "Median Filter",
        "Total Variation",    "Wiener Filter", "Weighted Mean Temporal", "RLS Temporal",
#if defined(OIDN_ON)
        "Open Image Denoise",
#else
        "Open Image Denoise (disabled)",
#endif

#if defined(TRAINED_DENOISER_ON)
        "Trained UNet",       "Trained LSTM",
#else
        "Trained UNet (disabled)",
        "Trained LSTM (disabled)",
#endif
    };

    // Camera
    Camera m_camera;

    // Volumes
    std::optional<GFX::VkImageObject> m_volumeData3DImage;
    std::optional<GFX::VkImageObject> m_volumeData3DNextFrameImage;

    // Exporter
    Exporter m_exporter{m_context};

    // Compute shader
    std::optional<GFX::VkImageObject> m_rawOutputImage;

    // reprojection
    std::optional<GFX::VkImageObject> m_positionDepthBufferImage;
    std::optional<GFX::VkImageObject> m_motionVectorImage;

    // ray marching
    std::optional<GFX::VkPassObject>          m_rmPass;
    std::optional<GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject> m_rmDynamicDescriptorSet;
    std::optional<GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject> m_rmStaticDescriptorSet;

    // motion vector post process pass
    std::optional<GFX::VkPassObject>          m_motionVectorPass;
    std::optional<GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject> m_motionVectorDescriptorSet;

    // Graphics pass
    std::optional<GFX::VkPassObject>          m_outputRenderPass;
    std::optional<GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject> m_outputDescriptorSet;
    std::optional<UserInterface>              m_userInterface;

    std::optional<GFX::VkCommandBufferObject> m_computeCommandBuffer;
    std::optional<GFX::VkCommandBufferObject> m_graphicsCommandBuffer;

    std::vector<vk::raii::Semaphore> m_imageAvailableSemaphores;

    uint32_t m_currentFrame = 0;
    uint32_t m_imageIndex   = 0;

    // 4D volume
    float m_timeStep    = 0;
    float m_timeCounter = 0;
    float m_shouldReset = true;
};
