### renderer/noise.cpp
```
#define _USE_MATH_DEFINES
#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>
#include <iostream>
#include <omp.h>
#include <random>

#include "imgui.h"
#include "noise.h"
#include "utils/container.h"
#include "utils/utils.h"

Noise::Noise(GFX::VkContext &context, AppConfig &appConfig) :
    m_context(context),
    m_textureWidth(context.Config().WindowWidth),
    m_textureHeight(context.Config().WindowHeight),
    m_config(appConfig),
    NoiseColor(appConfig.NoiseConfig.noiseColor)
{
    Noise2D.resize(m_textureWidth * m_textureHeight, glm::vec2(0.0f));

    if (NoiseColor == 1)
    {
        m_poissonDiskSamples = PoissonDiskSamples(m_textureWidth, m_textureHeight, minDist);
    }
}

void Noise::OnNonVaryStepSize()
{
    for (auto &value : Noise2D)
    {
        value = glm::vec2(0.0f);
    }

    if (!NoiseStagingBuffer.has_value() || !NoiseTexture.has_value())
    {
        CreateBufferAndTextures();
    }
}

void Noise::OnVaryStepSize()
{
    switch (DestributionTypes)
    {
        case 0:
            UniformDist();
            break;
        case 1:
            GaussianDist();
            break;
        case 2:
            ExponentialDist();
            break;
        default:
            std::cerr << "Unknown distribution type" << std::endl;
            break;
    }

    if (!NoiseStagingBuffer.has_value() || !NoiseTexture.has_value())
    {
        CreateBufferAndTextures();
    }
}

/**
 * May need to called per frame, hence parallelized on cpu to increase performance.
 */
void Noise::UniformDist()
{
    auto &noiseMinValue = m_config.NoiseConfig.UniformNoise.min;
    auto &noiseMaxValue = m_config.NoiseConfig.UniformNoise.max;

    if (NoiseColor == 1)
    {
        std::random_device                    rd;
        std::mt19937                          gen(rd());
        std::uniform_real_distribution<float> dist(noiseMinValue, noiseMaxValue);

        Noise2D = std::vector<glm::vec2>(m_textureWidth * m_textureHeight, glm::vec2(noiseMinValue, 0.0f));

        for (const auto &p : m_poissonDiskSamples)
        {
            int x                           = (int) p.x % m_textureWidth;
            int y                           = (int) p.y % m_textureHeight;
            Noise2D[y * m_textureWidth + x] = {dist(gen), 0.0f};
        }
    }
    else
    {
#pragma omp parallel
        {
            std::random_device                    rd;
            std::mt19937                          gen(rd() + omp_get_thread_num());
            std::uniform_real_distribution<float> dist(noiseMinValue, noiseMaxValue);

#pragma omp for schedule(static)
            for (int i = 0; i < Noise2D.size(); ++i)
            {
                Noise2D[i] = glm::vec2(dist(gen), 0.0);
            }
        }
    }
}

/**
 * May need to called per frame, hence parallelized on cpu to increase performance.
 */
void Noise::GaussianDist()
{
    auto &noiseMinValue = m_config.NoiseConfig.GaussianNoise.min;
    auto &noiseMaxValue = m_config.NoiseConfig.GaussianNoise.max;

    auto standardDeviation = (noiseMinValue + noiseMaxValue) / 6.0f;
    auto mean              = (noiseMinValue + noiseMaxValue) / 2.0f;

    if (NoiseColor == 1)
    {
        std::random_device              rd;
        std::mt19937                    gen(rd());
        std::normal_distribution<float> dist(mean, standardDeviation);

        Noise2D = std::vector<glm::vec2>(m_textureWidth * m_textureHeight, glm::vec2(noiseMinValue, 0.0f));

        for (const auto &p : m_poissonDiskSamples)
        {
            int x                           = (int) p.x % m_textureWidth;
            int y                           = (int) p.y % m_textureHeight;
            Noise2D[y * m_textureWidth + x] = {dist(gen), 0.0f};
        }
    }
    else
    {
#pragma omp parallel
        {
            std::random_device              rd;
            std::mt19937                    gen(rd() + omp_get_thread_num());
            std::normal_distribution<float> dist(mean, standardDeviation);

#pragma omp for schedule(static)
            for (int i = 0; i < Noise2D.size(); ++i)
            {
                float value = std::clamp(dist(gen), noiseMinValue, noiseMaxValue);
                Noise2D[i]  = glm::vec2(value, 0);
            }
        }
    }
}

/**
 * May need to called per frame, hence parallelized on cpu to increase performance.
 */
void Noise::ExponentialDist()
{
    auto &noiseMinValue = m_config.NoiseConfig.ExponentialNoise.min;
    auto &noiseMaxValue = m_config.NoiseConfig.ExponentialNoise.max;

    double lambda = -std::log(1.0 - 0.99) / (noiseMaxValue - noiseMinValue);

    if (NoiseColor == 1)
    {
        std::random_device                   rd;
        std::mt19937                         gen(rd());
        std::exponential_distribution<float> dist(lambda + omp_get_thread_num());

        Noise2D = std::vector<glm::vec2>(m_textureWidth * m_textureHeight, glm::vec2(noiseMinValue, 0.0f));

        for (const auto &p : m_poissonDiskSamples)
        {
            int x = (int) p.x % m_textureWidth;
            int y = (int) p.y % m_textureHeight;
            Noise2D[y * m_textureWidth + x] =
                glm::vec2(std::clamp(dist(gen) + noiseMinValue, noiseMinValue, noiseMaxValue), 0);
        }
    }
    else
    {
#pragma omp parallel
        {
            std::random_device                   rd;
            std::mt19937                         gen(rd());
            std::exponential_distribution<float> dist(lambda + omp_get_thread_num());

#pragma omp for schedule(static)
            for (int i = 0; i < Noise2D.size(); ++i)
            {
                Noise2D[i] = glm::vec2(std::clamp(dist(gen) + noiseMinValue, noiseMinValue, noiseMaxValue), 0);
            }
        }
    }
}

bool Noise::DrawNoiseUI()
{
    auto &varyStepSize = m_config.NoiseConfig.varyingStepSize;
    ImGui::SeparatorText("Varying Step Size");
    bool   result        = ImGui::Checkbox("Varying step size", &varyStepSize);
    float *noiseMinValue = nullptr;
    float *noiseMaxValue = nullptr;

    if (varyStepSize)
    {
        bool ncolor           = NoiseColor;
        bool noiseColorResult = false;
        noiseColorResult |= ImGui::Checkbox("Blue noise", &ncolor);
        result |= noiseColorResult;
        NoiseColor = ncolor ? 1 : 0;
        if (NoiseColor == 1)
        {
            noiseColorResult |= ImGui::InputFloat("Min distance", &minDist);
            result |= noiseColorResult;
        }

        if (!PerFrameUpdate)
        {
            if (noiseColorResult && minDist > 0.0f && NoiseColor == 1)
            {
                m_poissonDiskSamples = PoissonDiskSamples(m_textureWidth, m_textureHeight, minDist);
            }

            result |= ImGui::Combo("Distribution", &DestributionTypes, DistributionNames.data(),
                                   static_cast<int>(DistributionNames.size()));
        }
        else
        {
            // on shader generated noise, we only allow white uniform distribution
            result     = 0;
            NoiseColor = 0;
            ImGui::Text("Distribution: Uniform (on shader)");
        }

        switch (DestributionTypes)
        {
            case 0:
            {
                noiseMinValue = &m_config.NoiseConfig.UniformNoise.min;
                noiseMaxValue = &m_config.NoiseConfig.UniformNoise.max;
                result |= ImGui::InputFloat("Min stepsize", noiseMinValue) && noiseMinValue != 0;
                result |= ImGui::InputFloat("Max stepsize", noiseMaxValue) && noiseMaxValue != 0;
                break;
            }
            case 1:
            {
                noiseMinValue = &m_config.NoiseConfig.GaussianNoise.min;
                noiseMaxValue = &m_config.NoiseConfig.GaussianNoise.max;
                result |= ImGui::InputFloat("Min stepsize", noiseMinValue) && noiseMinValue != 0;
                result |= ImGui::InputFloat("Max stepsize", noiseMaxValue) && noiseMaxValue != 0;
                break;
            }
            case 2:
            {
                noiseMinValue = &m_config.NoiseConfig.ExponentialNoise.min;
                noiseMaxValue = &m_config.NoiseConfig.ExponentialNoise.max;
                result |= ImGui::InputFloat("Min stepsize", noiseMinValue) && noiseMinValue != 0;
                result |= ImGui::InputFloat("Max stepsize", noiseMaxValue) && noiseMaxValue != 0;
                break;
            }
            default:
                std::cerr << "Unknown distribution type" << std::endl;
                break;
        }
    }

    if (result)
    {
        if (varyStepSize)
        {
            if (*noiseMinValue == 0.0f || *noiseMaxValue == 0.0f || *noiseMinValue >= *noiseMaxValue)
            {
                return false;
            }

            OnVaryStepSize();
        }
        else
        {
            OnNonVaryStepSize();
        }
        UpdateNoiseTexture();

        if (m_config.NoiseConfig.varyingStepSize)
        {
            m_config.ExportFilePath = to_string(*this) + ".jpg";
        }
    }

    return result;
}

void Noise::CreateBufferAndTextures()
{
    NoiseStagingBuffer.emplace(m_context, Noise2D.size() * sizeof(glm::vec2), vk::BufferUsageFlagBits::eTransferSrc,
                               VMA_MEMORY_USAGE_CPU_ONLY);
    NoiseStagingBuffer->CopyData(Noise2D.data());

    // R channel: step size, G channel: offset
    NoiseTexture.emplace(m_context, *NoiseStagingBuffer, m_textureWidth, m_textureHeight, 1, vk::Format::eR32G32Sfloat,
                         true);
    NoiseTexture->TransitionImageLayout(vk::ImageLayout::eGeneral);
    NoiseTexture->SetName("Noise Texture");
}

void Noise::UpdateNoiseTexture()
{
    NoiseStagingBuffer->CopyData(Noise2D.data());
    NoiseTexture->UpdateImageBuffer(*NoiseStagingBuffer);
}

std::pair<float, float> Noise::GetMinMaxStepSize() const
{
    if (DestributionTypes == 0)
    {
        return {m_config.NoiseConfig.UniformNoise.min, m_config.NoiseConfig.UniformNoise.max};
    }
    else if (DestributionTypes == 1)
    {
        return {m_config.NoiseConfig.GaussianNoise.min, m_config.NoiseConfig.GaussianNoise.max};
    }
    else if (DestributionTypes == 2)
    {
        return {m_config.NoiseConfig.ExponentialNoise.min, m_config.NoiseConfig.ExponentialNoise.max};
    }
    return {0.0f, 1.0f};        // Default case, should not happen
}

//------------------------------------------------------
// GPU based noise generation
//------------------------------------------------------
void Noise::SetEllapsedTime(float *time)
{
    ellapsedTimePtr         = time;
    m_noiseUbo.ellapsedTime = *ellapsedTimePtr;
}

void Noise::InitPass()
{
    m_noiseUbo.min = m_config.NoiseConfig.UniformNoise.min;
    m_noiseUbo.max = m_config.NoiseConfig.UniformNoise.max;

    if (!m_noiseBuffer.has_value())
    {
        Utils::CreateAndCopyBuffer(m_context, &m_config.NoiseConfig.UniformNoise, sizeof(NoiseUbo),
                                   vk::BufferUsageFlagBits::eUniformBuffer | vk::BufferUsageFlagBits::eTransferDst,
                                   VMA_MEMORY_USAGE_GPU_ONLY, m_noiseBuffer);
        GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::ElementUniformBuffer noiseDescriptorElement{*m_noiseBuffer};
        GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::ElementStorageImage  noiseDescriptorImage{*NoiseTexture};
        m_noiseDescriptorSet.emplace(m_context, std::vector<GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::IDescriptorElement *>{
                                                    &noiseDescriptorElement, &noiseDescriptorImage});

        auto shaderCode = Utils::ReadFile(m_config.NoiseConfig.NoiseGenerationShaderPath);
        m_noisePass.emplace(m_context, shaderCode,
                            std::vector<vk::DescriptorSetLayout>{m_noiseDescriptorSet->DescriptorSetLayout});
    }
}

void Noise::RecordPass(GFX::VkCommandBufferObject &commandBuffer, uint32_t currentFrame)
{
    m_noiseUbo.min          = m_config.NoiseConfig.UniformNoise.min;
    m_noiseUbo.max          = m_config.NoiseConfig.UniformNoise.max;
    m_noiseUbo.ellapsedTime = *ellapsedTimePtr;

    commandBuffer.StartComputePass(*m_noisePass);
    commandBuffer.BindDescriptorSet(*m_noisePass, {m_noiseDescriptorSet->DescriptorSets[currentFrame]});
    commandBuffer.Dispatch(m_textureWidth / 16, m_textureHeight / 16, 1);
    commandBuffer.BarrierImage(NoiseTexture->Image, vk::ImageLayout::eGeneral, vk::ImageLayout::eGeneral,
                               vk::AccessFlagBits::eShaderWrite, vk::AccessFlagBits::eShaderRead,
                               vk::PipelineStageFlagBits::eComputeShader, vk::PipelineStageFlagBits::eComputeShader);
    if (m_noiseUbo.min != 0.0f && m_noiseUbo.max != 0.0f && m_noiseUbo.min < m_noiseUbo.max)
    {
        g_DirtyBuffers.insert({m_noiseBuffer->Buffer, 0, sizeof(NoiseUbo), &m_noiseUbo});
    }
}

std::vector<glm::vec2> Noise::PoissonDiskSamples(int width, int height, float r, int k)
{
    float cell_size = r / std::sqrt(2.0f);
    int   grid_w    = (int) std::ceil(width / cell_size);
    int   grid_h    = (int) std::ceil(height / cell_size);

    // Grid initialized with -1 (empty)
    std::vector<std::vector<int>> grid(grid_w, std::vector<int>(grid_h, -1));

    std::vector<glm::vec2> samples;
    std::vector<int>       active_list;

    std::mt19937                          rng(std::random_device{}());
    std::uniform_real_distribution<float> distX(0.0f, (float) width);
    std::uniform_real_distribution<float> distY(0.0f, (float) height);
    std::uniform_real_distribution<float> distAngle(0.0f, 2.0f * M_PI);
    std::uniform_real_distribution<float> distRadius(r, 2.0f * r);

    // Start with a random point
    float x = distX(rng);
    float y = distY(rng);
    samples.emplace_back(x, y);
    active_list.push_back(0);
    grid[(int) (x / cell_size)][(int) (y / cell_size)] = 0;

    while (!active_list.empty())
    {
        std::uniform_int_distribution<int> distActive(0, active_list.size() - 1);
        int                                activeIndex = distActive(rng);
        int                                idx         = active_list[activeIndex];
        glm::vec2                          base        = samples[idx];
        bool                               found       = false;

        for (int attempt = 0; attempt < k; ++attempt)
        {
            float     theta     = distAngle(rng);
            float     radius    = distRadius(rng);
            glm::vec2 candidate = base + glm::vec2(std::cos(theta), std::sin(theta)) * radius;

            if (candidate.x < 0 || candidate.x >= width || candidate.y < 0 || candidate.y >= height)
                continue;

            int gx = (int) (candidate.x / cell_size);
            int gy = (int) (candidate.y / cell_size);

            bool ok = true;
            for (int i = std::max(gx - 2, 0); i <= std::min(gx + 2, grid_w - 1) && ok; ++i)
            {
                for (int j = std::max(gy - 2, 0); j <= std::min(gy + 2, grid_h - 1); ++j)
                {
                    int neighborIdx = grid[i][j];
                    if (neighborIdx != -1)
                    {
                        glm::vec2 diff = candidate - samples[neighborIdx];
                        if (glm::dot(diff, diff) < r * r)
                        {
                            ok = false;
                            break;
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            if (ok)
            {
                samples.push_back(candidate);
                active_list.push_back((int) samples.size() - 1);
                grid[gx][gy] = (int) samples.size() - 1;
                found        = true;
                break;
            }
        }

        if (!found)
        {
            active_list[activeIndex] = active_list.back();
            active_list.pop_back();
        }
    }

    return samples;
}
```

### renderer/noise.h
```
#pragma once
#include <cstdlib>
#include <glm/glm.hpp>
#include <vector>

#include "app_config.h"
#include "buffer.h"
#include "command_buffer.h"
#include "descriptorset.h"
#include "image.h"
#include "pass.h"

/**
 * @brief vary stepsize texture
 */
class Noise
{
  public:
    Noise(GFX::VkContext &context, AppConfig &appConfig);
    void OnNonVaryStepSize();
    void OnVaryStepSize();
    void UpdateNoiseTexture();

    bool DrawNoiseUI();

    std::pair<float, float> GetMinMaxStepSize() const;
    // GPU based noise generation
    void InitPass();
    void SetEllapsedTime(float *time);
    void RecordPass(GFX::VkCommandBufferObject &commandBuffer, uint32_t currentFrame);

  public:
    std::vector<glm::vec2> Noise2D;

    std::optional<GFX::VkBufferObject> NoiseStagingBuffer;
    std::optional<GFX::VkImageObject>  NoiseTexture;

    int                       NoiseColor;
    std::vector<const char *> NoiseColorNames = {"White", "Blue"};

    int                       DestributionTypes = 0;
    std::vector<const char *> DistributionNames = {"Uniform", "Gaussian", "Exponential"};
    bool                      PerFrameUpdate    = false;

  private:
    void UniformDist();
    void GaussianDist();
    void ExponentialDist();
    void CreateBufferAndTextures();

    std::vector<glm::vec2> PoissonDiskSamples(int width, int height, float r, int k = 30);

  private:
    GFX::VkContext &m_context;
    AppConfig      &m_config;
    uint32_t        m_textureWidth;
    uint32_t        m_textureHeight;

    // GPU based noise generation
    struct NoiseUbo
    {
        float min;
        float max;
        float ellapsedTime;
    } m_noiseUbo;

    std::optional<GFX::VkBufferObject>        m_noiseBuffer;
    std::optional<GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject> m_noiseDescriptorSet;
    std::optional<GFX::VkPassObject>          m_noisePass;
    std::vector<glm::vec2>                    m_poissonDiskSamples;
    float                                    *ellapsedTimePtr = nullptr;
    float                                     minDist         = 1.0f;
};

inline std::ostream &operator<<(std::ostream &os, const Noise &noise)
{
    auto [min, max] = noise.GetMinMaxStepSize();
    auto minStepStr = std::to_string(min);
    auto maxStepStr = std::to_string(max);
    std::replace(minStepStr.begin(), minStepStr.end(), '.', '_');
    std::replace(maxStepStr.begin(), maxStepStr.end(), '.', '_');
    switch (noise.DestributionTypes)
    {
        case 0:
            os << "uniform_min_" << minStepStr << "_max_" << maxStepStr;
            break;
        case 1:
            os << "gaussian_min_" << minStepStr << "_max_" << maxStepStr;
            break;
        case 2:
            os << "exponential_min_" << minStepStr << "_max_" << maxStepStr;
            break;
    }
    return os;
}

inline std::string to_string(const Noise &noise)
{
    std::ostringstream oss;
    oss << noise;
    return oss.str();
}
```

### renderer/user_interface.cpp
```
#include "user_interface.h"

#ifdef IMGUI_ENABLE_TEST_ENGINE
#include "imgui_test_engine/imgui_te_context.h"
#include "imgui_test_engine/imgui_te_ui.h"
#include "utils/utils.h"
#endif

static float cachedFPS      = 0.0f;
static float lastUpdateTime = 0.0f;

UserInterface::UserInterface(const GFX::VkContext &context, const GFX::Window &window, const GFX::VkPassObject &pass) :
    m_context{context}
{
    InitUI(context, window, pass);
#ifdef IMGUI_ENABLE_TEST_ENGINE
    CreateTestEngine();
#endif
}

UserInterface::~UserInterface()
{
#ifdef IMGUI_ENABLE_TEST_ENGINE
    ImGuiTestEngine_Stop(m_engine);
#endif

    ImGui_ImplVulkan_Shutdown();
    ImGui_ImplGlfw_Shutdown();
    ImGui::DestroyContext();

#ifdef IMGUI_ENABLE_TEST_ENGINE
    ImGuiTestEngine_DestroyContext(m_engine);
#endif

    vkDestroyDescriptorPool(*m_context.Device, m_imguiPool, nullptr);
}

void UserInterface::Record(vk::raii::CommandBuffer &commandBuffer) const
{
    ImGui_ImplVulkan_RenderDrawData(ImGui::GetDrawData(), *commandBuffer);
}

void UserInterface::StartUI() const
{
    ImGuiIO &io = ImGui::GetIO();
    ImGui_ImplVulkan_NewFrame();
    ImGui_ImplGlfw_NewFrame();
    ImGui::NewFrame();

    float currentTime = ImGui::GetTime();

    if (currentTime - lastUpdateTime > 1.0f || cachedFPS == 0.0f)
    {
        cachedFPS      = io.Framerate;
        lastUpdateTime = currentTime;
    }
    ImGui::Begin("Information");

    ImGui::Text("GPU: %s", m_context.GPUDeviceName.c_str());
    ImGui::Text("VSync: %s", m_context.Config().VSync ? "On" : "Off");
    ImGui::Text("Framerate: %f", cachedFPS);
}

void UserInterface::EndUI() const
{
    ImGui::End();

#ifdef IMGUI_ENABLE_TEST_ENGINE
    ImGuiTestEngine_ShowTestEngineWindows(m_engine, nullptr);
#endif
    ImGui::Render();
}

void UserInterface::DrawVolumePropertyUI(const glm::vec3 &dimension, const glm::vec3 &bbMin, const glm::vec3 &bbMax,
                                         const float &min, const float &max) const
{
    ImGui::SeparatorText("Volume Proptery");
    if (ImGui::BeginTable("Dimension table", 2, ImGuiTableFlags_Borders | ImGuiTableFlags_RowBg))
    {
        ImGui::TableSetupColumn("Component");
        ImGui::TableSetupColumn("Value");
        ImGui::TableHeadersRow();

        const char *labels[3] = {"X", "Y", "Z"};
        for (int i = 0; i < 3; ++i)
        {
            ImGui::TableNextRow();
            ImGui::TableSetColumnIndex(0);
            ImGui::Text("%s", labels[i]);

            ImGui::TableSetColumnIndex(1);
            ImGui::Text("%.3f", dimension[i]);        // Display value with 3 decimal precision
        }

        ImGui::EndTable();
    }
    ImGui::Text("Volume Range: [%f, %f]", min, max);

    ImGui::Text("Bounding Box Min: %f, %f, %f", bbMin.x, bbMin.y, bbMin.z);
    ImGui::Text("Bounding Box Max: %f, %f, %f", bbMax.x, bbMax.y, bbMax.z);
}

bool UserInterface::DrawDistanceUI(float &distance) const
{
    return ImGui::SliderFloat("Unit Length", &distance, 0.0f, .5f, "%.3f");
}

bool UserInterface::DrawStepSizeUI(float &stepSize) const
{
    return ImGui::SliderFloat("Step size", &stepSize, 0.001f, .1f, "%.3f");
}

bool UserInterface::DrawIlluminationScaleUI(float &scale) const
{
    return ImGui::SliderFloat("Illumination scale", &scale, 1.0f, 20.0f, "%.3f");
}

bool UserInterface::DrawDenoiserSelectionUI(const std::vector<const char *> &denoisersName, int &denoiserType) const
{
    ImGui::SeparatorText("Denoiser");
    ImGui::Text("Select Denoiser");
    return ImGui::Combo("Denoiser", &denoiserType, denoisersName.data(), static_cast<int>(denoisersName.size()));
}

void UserInterface::InitUI(const GFX::VkContext &context, const GFX::Window &window,
                           const GFX::VkPassObject &renderPassObject)
{
    VkDescriptorPoolSize pool_sizes[] = {{VK_DESCRIPTOR_TYPE_SAMPLER, 100},
                                         {VK_DESCRIPTOR_TYPE_COMBINED_IMAGE_SAMPLER, 100},
                                         {VK_DESCRIPTOR_TYPE_SAMPLED_IMAGE, 100},
                                         {VK_DESCRIPTOR_TYPE_STORAGE_IMAGE, 100},
                                         {VK_DESCRIPTOR_TYPE_UNIFORM_TEXEL_BUFFER, 100},
                                         {VK_DESCRIPTOR_TYPE_STORAGE_TEXEL_BUFFER, 100},
                                         {VK_DESCRIPTOR_TYPE_UNIFORM_BUFFER, 100},
                                         {VK_DESCRIPTOR_TYPE_STORAGE_BUFFER, 100},
                                         {VK_DESCRIPTOR_TYPE_UNIFORM_BUFFER_DYNAMIC, 100},
                                         {VK_DESCRIPTOR_TYPE_STORAGE_BUFFER_DYNAMIC, 100},
                                         {VK_DESCRIPTOR_TYPE_INPUT_ATTACHMENT, 100}};

    VkDescriptorPoolCreateInfo pool_info = {};
    pool_info.sType                      = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_DESCRIPTOR_POOL_CREATE_INFO;
    pool_info.flags                      = VK_DESCRIPTOR_POOL_CREATE_FREE_DESCRIPTOR_SET_BIT;
    pool_info.maxSets                    = 1000;
    pool_info.poolSizeCount              = std::size(pool_sizes);
    pool_info.pPoolSizes                 = pool_sizes;
    vkCreateDescriptorPool(*context.Device, &pool_info, nullptr, &m_imguiPool);

    IMGUI_CHECKVERSION();
    ImGui::CreateContext();
    ImGuiIO &io = ImGui::GetIO();
    (void) io;
    io.ConfigFlags |= ImGuiConfigFlags_NavEnableKeyboard;        // Enable Keyboard Controls
    io.ConfigFlags |= ImGuiConfigFlags_NavEnableGamepad;         // Enable Gamepad Controls

    // Setup Platform/Renderer backends
    auto swapchainCapabilities = context.PhysicalDevice.getSurfaceCapabilitiesKHR(*context.Surface);
    ImGui_ImplGlfw_InitForVulkan(window.GetWindow(), true);
    ImGui_ImplVulkan_InitInfo init_info = {};
    init_info.Instance                  = *context.Instance;
    init_info.PhysicalDevice            = *context.PhysicalDevice;
    init_info.Device                    = *context.Device;
    init_info.Queue                     = *context.GraphicsQueue;
    init_info.PipelineCache             = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
    init_info.DescriptorPool            = m_imguiPool;
    init_info.Subpass                   = 0;
    init_info.MinImageCount             = swapchainCapabilities.minImageCount;
    init_info.ImageCount                = m_context.MaxFramesInFlight;
    init_info.MSAASamples               = VK_SAMPLE_COUNT_1_BIT;
    init_info.Allocator                 = nullptr;
    init_info.CheckVkResultFn           = nullptr;
    init_info.RenderPass                = static_cast<VkRenderPass>(*renderPassObject.RenderPass);
    ImGui_ImplVulkan_Init(&init_info);
}

void UserInterface::LoadTheme(const std::filesystem::path &path) const
{
    ImGuiStyle   &style  = ImGui::GetStyle();
    ImVec4       *colors = style.Colors;
    std::ifstream in(path.generic_string());

    if (!std::filesystem::is_regular_file(path))
    {
        std::cerr << "File not found: " << path << std::endl;
        return;
    }

    if (!in.is_open())
        return;

    std::string line;
    while (std::getline(in, line))
    {
        if (line.empty() || line[0] == '[')
            continue;

        auto delimiterPos = line.find('=');
        auto key          = line.substr(0, delimiterPos);
        auto value        = line.substr(delimiterPos + 1);

        if (key == "WindowRounding")
            style.WindowRounding = std::stof(value);
        else if (key == "ChildRounding")
            style.ChildRounding = std::stof(value);
        else if (key == "FrameRounding")
            style.FrameRounding = std::stof(value);
        else if (key == "PopupRounding")
            style.PopupRounding = std::stof(value);
        else if (key == "ScrollbarRounding")
            style.ScrollbarRounding = std::stof(value);
        else if (key == "GrabRounding")
            style.GrabRounding = std::stof(value);
        else if (key == "TabRounding")
            style.TabRounding = std::stof(value);
        else
        {
            for (int i = 0; i < ImGuiCol_COUNT; ++i)
            {
                if (key == ImGui::GetStyleColorName(i))
                {
                    std::stringstream  ss(value);
                    std::string        f;
                    std::vector<float> rgba;
                    while (std::getline(ss, f, ','))
                        rgba.push_back(std::stof(f));
                    if (rgba.size() == 4)
                        colors[i] = ImVec4(rgba[0], rgba[1], rgba[2], rgba[3]);
                    break;
                }
            }
        }
    }

    in.close();
}

int UserInterface::InputTextCallback(ImGuiInputTextCallbackData *data)
{
    if (data->EventFlag == ImGuiInputTextFlags_CallbackResize)
    {
        // Resize string to fit new length
        std::string *str = static_cast<std::string *>(data->UserData);
        str->resize(data->BufTextLen);
        data->Buf = str->data();
    }
    return 0;
}

//----------------------------------------------------------------------
// Automations
//----------------------------------------------------------------------
#ifdef IMGUI_ENABLE_TEST_ENGINE
void UserInterface::CreateTestEngine()
{
    m_engine                          = ImGuiTestEngine_CreateContext();
    ImGuiTestEngineIO &test_io        = ImGuiTestEngine_GetIO(m_engine);
    test_io.ConfigVerboseLevel        = ImGuiTestVerboseLevel_Info;
    test_io.ConfigVerboseLevelOnError = ImGuiTestVerboseLevel_Debug;
    test_io.ConfigRunSpeed            = ImGuiTestRunSpeed_Fast;

    ImGuiTestEngine_Start(m_engine, ImGui::GetCurrentContext());
    ImGuiTestEngine_InstallDefaultCrashHandler();

    // register automations
    StepSizeOnDistributionsAutomation();
    MeanFilterAutomation();
    GaussianFilterAutomation();
    BilateralFilterAutomation();
    MedianFilterAutomation();
    TVFilterAutomation();
    WienerFilterAutomation();
    TemporalMeanAutomation();
    RLSAutomation();
    NoiseDistributionAutomation();
}

void UserInterface::PostSwap() const
{
    ImGuiTestEngine_PostSwap(m_engine);
}

void UserInterface::StepSizeOnDistributionsAutomation() const
{
    auto t      = IM_REGISTER_TEST(m_engine, "automation", "Step size on different distributions");
    t->TestFunc = [](ImGuiTestContext *ctx) {
        ctx->SetRef("Information");
        ctx->ItemCheck("Varying step size");
        std::vector<std::string> distributions = {
            "Distribution/Uniform",
            "Distribution/Gaussian",
            "Distribution/Exponential",
        };
        for (const auto &dist : distributions)
        {
            ctx->ComboClick(dist.c_str());
            for (float step = 0.2f; step < 1.6f; step += 0.2f)
            {
                ctx->ItemInputValue("Max stepsize", step);
                ctx->ItemClick("Export");
            }
        }

        // reset to uniform
        ctx->ComboClick(distributions[0].c_str());
    };
}

void UserInterface::MeanFilterAutomation() const
{
    auto t      = IM_REGISTER_TEST(m_engine, "automation", "Mean filter on different step sizes");
    t->TestFunc = [](ImGuiTestContext *ctx) {
        ctx->SetRef("Information");
        ctx->ItemCheck("Varying step size");
        ctx->ComboClick("Denoiser/Mean Filter");

        for (int i = 0; i < 2; ++i)
        {
            for (float step = 0.2f; step < 1.6f; step += 0.2f)
            {
                ctx->ItemInputValue("Max stepsize", step);
                const char *currentExportPath = ctx->ItemReadAsString("Export path");
                for (int kernelSize = 2; kernelSize < 8; kernelSize += 2)
                {
                    ctx->ItemInputValue("Kernel Size", kernelSize);

                    if (i == 0)
                    {
                        ctx->ItemCheck("Weighted Mean");
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        ctx->ItemUncheck("Weighted Mean");
                    }

                    auto exportFinalPath =
                        Utils::InsertStringBeforeExtension(currentExportPath, "_kernel_" + std::to_string(kernelSize));
                    if (i == 0)
                    {
                        exportFinalPath = Utils::InsertStringBeforeExtension(exportFinalPath, "_weighted");
                    }

                    ctx->ItemInputValue("Export path", exportFinalPath.c_str());

                    ctx->ItemClick("Export");
                }
            }
        }
    };
}

void UserInterface::NoiseDistributionAutomation() const
{
    auto t      = IM_REGISTER_TEST(m_engine, "automation", "Noise distribution automation");
    t->TestFunc = [](ImGuiTestContext *ctx) {
        ctx->SetRef("Information");
        ctx->ItemCheck("Varying step size");
        std::vector<std::string> distributions = {
            "Distribution/Uniform",
            "Distribution/Gaussian",
        };
        for (const auto &dist : distributions)
        {
            ctx->ComboClick(dist.c_str());
            if (dist == "Distribution/Uniform")
            {
                ctx->ItemInputValue("Min stepsize", 0.01f);
                ctx->ItemInputValue("Max stepsize", 0.6f);
            }
            else
            {
                ctx->ItemInputValue("Min stepsize", 0.002f);
                ctx->ItemInputValue("Max stepsize", 0.6f);
            }

            // weighted mean filter
            ctx->ComboClick("Denoiser/Mean Filter");
            ctx->ItemClick("Weighted Mean");
            ctx->ItemInputValue("Kernel Size", 6);
            ctx->ItemClick("Export");

            // gaussian filter
            ctx->ComboClick("Denoiser/Gaussian Filter");
            ctx->ItemInputValue("Kernel Size", 6);
            ctx->ItemClick("Export");

            // bilateral filter
            ctx->ComboClick("Denoiser/Bilateral Filter");
            ctx->ItemInputValue("Kernel Size", 6);
            ctx->ItemClick("Export");

            // median filter
            ctx->ComboClick("Denoiser/Median Filter");
            ctx->ItemInputValue("Kernel Size", 4);
            ctx->ItemClick("Export");

            // wiener filter
            ctx->ComboClick("Denoiser/Wiener Filter");
            ctx->ItemInputValue("Kernel Size", 7);
            ctx->ItemClick("Export");

            // total variation
            ctx->ComboClick("Denoiser/Total Variation");
            ctx->ItemInputValue("Iterations", 21);
            ctx->ItemClick("Export");
        }
    };
}
```

```
#pragma once

{

#endif
};
```

### renderer/vk_cuda_interop.cu
```
#ifdef TRAINED_DENOISER_ON
#include "vk_cuda_interop.h"

void VkCudaInterop::ImportVulkanImage(const GFX::VkImageObject &image, cudaExternalMemory_t &extMem,
                                      cudaTextureObject_t *outTextureObject, cudaSurfaceObject_t *outSurfaceObject,
                                      cudaChannelFormatDesc formatDesc, cudaTextureReadMode readMode)
{
    {
        cudaExternalMemoryHandleDesc cudaExtMemHandleDesc = {};
#if defined(__linux__)
        // cuda will close the fd, hence we need to duplicate it
        int fd = dup(image.Fd);
        if (fd < 0)
        {
            throw std::runtime_error("Failed to duplicate file descriptor for external memory handle");
        }

        cudaExtMemHandleDesc.type      = cudaExternalMemoryHandleTypeOpaqueFd;
        cudaExtMemHandleDesc.handle.fd = fd;
#elif defined(_WIN32) || defined(_WIN64)
        Handle winhandle = image.WinHandle;
        if (!DuplicateHandle(GetCurrentProcess(), image.WinHandle, GetCurrentProcess(), &handleForCuda, 0, FALSE,
                             DUPLICATE_SAME_ACCESS))
        {
            throw std::runtime_error("DuplicateHandle failed on Vulkan Win32 handle");
        }
        cudaExtMemHandleDesc.type                = cudaExternalMemoryHandleTypeOpaqueWin32;
        cudaExtMemHandleDesc.handle.win32.handle = winHandle;
#endif
        cudaExtMemHandleDesc.size = image.GetMemoryRequirementSize();

        auto error = cudaImportExternalMemory(&extMem, &cudaExtMemHandleDesc);
        if (error != cudaSuccess)
        {
            throw std::runtime_error("Failed to import external memory: " + std::string(cudaGetErrorString(error)));
        }

        cudaExternalMemoryMipmappedArrayDesc mipmapDesc = {};
        mipmapDesc.extent                               = make_cudaExtent(image.Extent.width, image.Extent.height, 0);
        mipmapDesc.formatDesc                           = formatDesc;
        mipmapDesc.numLevels                            = 1;
        mipmapDesc.flags                                = 0;

        cudaMipmappedArray_t mipmappedArray;
        error = cudaExternalMemoryGetMappedMipmappedArray(&mipmappedArray, extMem, &mipmapDesc);
        if (error != cudaSuccess)
        {
            cudaDestroyExternalMemory(extMem);
            throw std::runtime_error("Failed to get mapped mipmapped array: " + std::string(cudaGetErrorString(error)));
        }

        cudaArray_t array;
        error = cudaGetMipmappedArrayLevel(&array, mipmappedArray, 0);
        if (error != cudaSuccess)
        {
            cudaDestroyExternalMemory(extMem);
            throw std::runtime_error("Failed to get mipmapped array level: " + std::string(cudaGetErrorString(error)));
        }

        if (outTextureObject)
        {
            cudaResourceDesc resDesc = {};
            resDesc.resType          = cudaResourceTypeArray;
            resDesc.res.array.array  = array;

            cudaTextureDesc texDesc  = {};
            texDesc.addressMode[0]   = cudaAddressModeClamp;
            texDesc.addressMode[1]   = cudaAddressModeClamp;
            texDesc.filterMode       = cudaFilterModePoint;
            texDesc.readMode         = readMode;
            texDesc.normalizedCoords = false;

            error = cudaCreateTextureObject(outTextureObject, &resDesc, &texDesc, nullptr);
            if (error != cudaSuccess)
            {
                cudaDestroyExternalMemory(extMem);
                throw std::runtime_error("Failed to create texture object: " + std::string(cudaGetErrorString(error)));
            }
        }

        if (outSurfaceObject)
        {
            cudaResourceDesc resDesc = {};
            resDesc.resType          = cudaResourceTypeArray;
            resDesc.res.array.array  = array;

            error = cudaCreateSurfaceObject(outSurfaceObject, &resDesc);
            if (error != cudaSuccess)
            {
                cudaDestroyExternalMemory(extMem);
                throw std::runtime_error("Failed to create surface object: " + std::string(cudaGetErrorString(error)));
            }
        }
    }
}
#endif
```

### renderer/vk_cuda_interop.h
```
#ifdef TRAINED_DENOISER_ON
#pragma once

#include "image.h"
#include <cuda.h>
#include <cuda_runtime_api.h>

#include <driver_functions.h>
#include <unistd.h>

/**
 * @brief Vulkan-CUDA interop class for importing Vulkan images into CUDA.
 */
class VkCudaInterop
{
  public:
    void static ImportVulkanImage(const GFX::VkImageObject &image, cudaExternalMemory_t &extMem,
                                  cudaTextureObject_t *outTextureObject, cudaSurfaceObject_t *outSurfaceObject,
                                  cudaChannelFormatDesc formatDesc, cudaTextureReadMode readMode);
};
#endif
```

### renderer/volume_handler.cpp
```
#include "volume_handler.h"

std::pair<glm::vec3, glm::vec3> VolumeHandler::GetBoundingBox(float distance, glm::vec3 dimension) const
{
    auto volumeWidth  = dimension.x * distance;
    auto volumeHeight = dimension.y * distance;
    auto volumeDepth  = dimension.z * distance;

    return std::make_pair(glm::vec3(-volumeWidth / 2.0f, -volumeHeight / 2.0f, -volumeDepth / 2.0f),
                          glm::vec3(volumeWidth / 2.0f, volumeHeight / 2.0f, volumeDepth / 2.0f));
}

void VolumeHandler::UpdateFrameIndex()
{
    if (VolumeFrames.size() <= 1)
    {
        return;
    }

    FrameIndex = (FrameIndex + 1) % VolumeFrames.size();
}

void VolumeHandler::ForceUpdateFrameIndex(int index)
{
    if (VolumeFrames.size() <= 1)
    {
        return;
    }

    FrameIndex = index % VolumeFrames.size();
}
```

### renderer/volume_handler.h
```
#pragma once
#include <fstream>
#include <iostream>
#include <sstream>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

#include "third-party/libnpy.h"
#include "utils/utils.h"
#include <glm/glm.hpp>

struct VolumeData3D
{
    std::array<uint32_t, 3> Shape;
    std::vector<float>      Data;
    float                   MinValue;
    float                   MaxValue;
};

/**
 * @brief VolumeHandler class for loading and managing 3D volume data.
 */
class VolumeHandler
{
  public:
    virtual ~VolumeHandler() = default;

    template <typename T>
    void LoadVolumeData(const std::filesystem::path &path)
    {
        if (!std::filesystem::exists(path))
        {
            throw std::runtime_error("VolumeLoader::LoadVolumeData: file does not exist!");
        }

        if (path.extension() != ".npy")
        {
            throw std::runtime_error("VolumeLoader::LoadVolumeData: unsupported file format! Please use "
                                     "script/xxx_to_npy.py to convert the volume to .npy format.");
        }

        npy::npy_data d = npy::read_npy<T>(path.generic_string());

        if (d.shape.size() != 3 && d.shape.size() != 4)
        {
            throw std::runtime_error("VolumeLoader::LoadVolumeData: unsupported volume shape!");
        }

        if (d.shape.size() == 3)
        {
            VolumeFrames.resize(1);
            VolumeFrames[0].Shape = {static_cast<uint32_t>(d.shape[0]), static_cast<uint32_t>(d.shape[1]),
                                     static_cast<uint32_t>(d.shape[2])};
            VolumeFrames[0].Data.resize(VolumeFrames[0].Shape[0] * VolumeFrames[0].Shape[1] * VolumeFrames[0].Shape[2]);
            std::transform(d.data.begin(), d.data.end(), VolumeFrames[0].Data.begin(),
                           [](const auto &value) { return static_cast<float>(value); });
            auto minMax              = std::minmax_element(VolumeFrames[0].Data.begin(), VolumeFrames[0].Data.end());
            VolumeFrames[0].MinValue = *minMax.first;
            VolumeFrames[0].MaxValue = *minMax.second;
            // convert [-n, n] to [0, 2n]
            for (float &data : VolumeFrames[0].Data)
            {
                data -= VolumeFrames[0].MinValue;
            }
            IsTimeVaryingVolume = false;
        }
        else if (d.shape.size() == 4)
        {
            size_t numFrames = d.shape[0];
            size_t x         = d.shape[1];
            size_t y         = d.shape[2];
            size_t z         = d.shape[3];

            size_t frameSize = x * y * z;
            VolumeFrames.resize(numFrames);

            for (size_t t = 0; t < numFrames; ++t)
            {
                VolumeData3D &vol = VolumeFrames[t];
                vol.Shape         = {static_cast<uint32_t>(x), static_cast<uint32_t>(y), static_cast<uint32_t>(z)};
                vol.Data.resize(frameSize);

                auto startIt = d.data.begin() + t * frameSize;
                auto endIt   = startIt + frameSize;

                std::transform(startIt, endIt, vol.Data.begin(),
                               [](const auto &value) { return static_cast<float>(value); });

                auto minMax  = std::minmax_element(vol.Data.begin(), vol.Data.end());
                vol.MinValue = *minMax.first;
                vol.MaxValue = *minMax.second;

                // Normalize (shift min to 0)
                for (float &v : vol.Data)
                {
                    v -= vol.MinValue;
                }
            }
            IsTimeVaryingVolume = true;
        }
    }

    std::pair<glm::vec3, glm::vec3> GetBoundingBox(float distance, glm::vec3 dimension) const;
    void                            UpdateFrameIndex();
    void                            ForceUpdateFrameIndex(int index);

  public:
    std::vector<VolumeData3D> VolumeFrames;
    bool                      IsTimeVaryingVolume = false;
    int                       FrameIndex          = 0;
};
```

### scripts/colormaps.py
```
# Source: https://github.com/KeKsBoTer/quokka/blob/master/colormaps.py
import json
from matplotlib import pyplot as plt
from os import makedirs
from matplotlib.colors import LinearSegmentedColormap, ListedColormap, Colormap
import numpy as np


def export_colormaps(cm_module, folder: str):
    makedirs(folder, exist_ok=True)
    for name, cmap in vars(cm_module).items():
        if not isinstance(cmap, Colormap):
            continue
        if name.endswith("_r"):
            continue
        if name.startswith("cmr."):
            name = name[4:]
        if isinstance(cmap, ListedColormap):
            data = cmap(np.linspace(0, 1, 256)).astype(np.float32)
            np.save("{}/{}.npy".format(folder, name), data)
        elif isinstance(cmap, LinearSegmentedColormap):
            segments = cmap._segmentdata

            if any(
                not isinstance(segments[c], np.ndarray)
                and not isinstance(segments[c], list)
                and not isinstance(segments[c], tuple)
                for c in ["red", "green", "blue"]
            ):
                data = cmap(np.linspace(0, 1, 256)).astype(np.float32)
                np.save("{}/{}.npy".format(folder, name), data)
            else:
                channels = {
                    c: (
                        segments[c].tolist()
                        if isinstance(segments[c], np.ndarray)
                        else segments[c]
                    )
                    for c in ["red", "green", "blue"]
                }
                if "alpha" in channels:
                    channels["alpha"] = segments["alpha"].tolist()
                with open("{}/{}.json".format(folder, name), "w") as f:
                    json.dump(
                        channels,
                        f,
                        indent=2,
                    )
        else:
            raise TypeError(
                "cmap must be either a ListedColormap or a LinearSegmentedColormap"
            )


export_colormaps(plt.cm, "./colormaps/matplotlib")

import seaborn as sns

export_colormaps(sns.cm, "./colormaps/seaborn")

import cmasher

export_colormaps(cmasher.cm, "./colormaps/cmasher")

class CustomColormaps:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.afmhot_trunc = ListedColormap(plt.colormaps["afmhot"](np.linspace(0.2, 1, 256)))

export_colormaps(CustomColormaps(), "./colormaps/custom")
```

### scripts/create_simple_volume.py
```
import numpy as np
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
import pyvista as pv

# Create a simple 3D volume with values from 0 to 1
def create_simple_volume(shape=(64, 64, 64)):
    """Create a 3D volume with gradient values from 0 to 1"""
    # Create coordinate grids
    x = np.linspace(0, 1, shape[0])
    y = np.linspace(0, 1, shape[1])
    z = np.linspace(0, 1, shape[2])
    
    # Create meshgrid
    X, Y, Z = np.meshgrid(x, y, z, indexing='ij')
    
    # Create volume with gradient values (simple linear combination)
    volume = (X + Y + Z) / 3.0
    
    return volume.astype(np.float32)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    # Create the volume
    volume_data = create_simple_volume()
    
    # Save to numpy file
    np.save('data/simple_volume.npy', volume_data)
    
    print(f"Created volume with shape: {volume_data.shape}")
    print(f"Volume value range: {volume_data.min():.3f} to {volume_data.max():.3f}")
    print("Saved to simple_volume.npy")
```

### scripts/dat_to_numpy.py
```
import numpy as np
import argparse
import pyvista as pv

def parse_dat(dat_path):
    metadata = {}
    with open(dat_path, 'r') as f:
        for line in f:
            if ':' in line:
                key, val = line.strip().split(':', 1)
                metadata[key.strip()] = val.strip()
    return metadata

def format_to_dtype(format_str):
    return {
        'UCHAR': np.uint8,
        'USHORT': np.uint16,
        'FLOAT': np.float32
    }.get(format_str.upper(), None)

def convert_dat_to_npy(dat_file, output_npy):
    meta = parse_dat(dat_file)
    
    raw_file = meta['ObjectFileName']
    dims = tuple(map(int, meta['Resolution'].split()))
    dtype = format_to_dtype(meta['Format'])

    if dtype is None:
        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported format: {meta['Format']}")

    with open(raw_file, 'rb') as f:
        volume = np.fromfile(f, dtype=dtype)

    if volume.size != np.prod(dims):
        raise ValueError(f"Size mismatch: {volume.size} vs expected {np.prod(dims)}")

    volume = volume.reshape(dims[::-1])  # Usually Z, Y, X order
    volume = volume.astype(np.float32)
    np.save(output_npy, volume)
    print(f"Saved to {output_npy}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Convert .dat file to .npy format.")
    parser.add_argument("dat_file", type=str, help="Path to the input .dat file")
    parser.add_argument("output_npy", type=str, help="Path to save the output .npy file")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    convert_dat_to_npy(args.dat_file, args.output_npy)
```

### scripts/mhd_to_numpy.py
```
import SimpleITK as sitk
import numpy as np
import argparse
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
import pyvista as pv

def mhd_to_numpy(mhd_path):
    image = sitk.ReadImage(mhd_path)  # Read .mhd file
    array = sitk.GetArrayFromImage(image)  # Convert to NumPy array
    return array.astype(np.float32)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Convert .mhd file to NumPy array and save as .npy file.")
    parser.add_argument("input_path", type=str, help="Path to the input .mhd file")
    parser.add_argument("output_path", type=str, help="Path to save the output .npy file")
    parser.add_argument("--visualize", action="store_true", help="Visualize the 3D volume using pyvista")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    numpy_array = mhd_to_numpy(args.input_path)
    np.save(args.output_path, numpy_array)

    print("Conversion completed!")
    print("Shape of the NumPy array:", numpy_array.shape)
    print("Datatype of the NumPy array:", numpy_array.dtype)

    if (args.visualize):
        volume = pv.wrap(numpy_array)
        plotter = pv.Plotter()
        plotter.add_volume(volume, cmap="gray", opacity="sigmoid_6")
        plotter.show()
```

### scripts/rls_visualization.py
```
import numpy as np
from PIL import Image
import os
from glob import glob
import matplotlib
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
import argparse

# Initial values
lambda_ = 0.998  # forgetting factor
beta = np.ones((4, 2))  # coefficients for each channel: shape (4, 2)
P = np.eye(2) * 2       # 2x2 inverse covariance matrix (shared for all channels)

print("Initial beta:\n", beta)
print("Initial P:\n", P)
print("---\n")

def rls_update(p_scalar: float, I_noisy: np.ndarray):
    global beta, P
    p_vec = np.array([1.0, p_scalar])  # input with intercept

    # Prediction for all 4 channels
    I_hat = beta @ p_vec
    print("I_hat:", I_hat)

    # Error
    e = I_noisy - I_hat
    print("Error:", e)

    # Gain vector
    denom = lambda_ + p_vec @ (P @ p_vec)
    q = (P @ p_vec) / denom
    print("Gain:", q)

    # Update beta for each RGBA channel
    for i in range(4):
        beta[i] += q * e[i]
    print("Updated beta:\n", beta)

    # Update inverse covariance matrix
    print("OuterPQ:", np.outer(q, p_vec))
    print(f"debug prediction: {np.outer(q, p_vec) @ P}")
    P = (1.0 / lambda_) * (P - np.outer(q, p_vec) @ P)
    print("Updated Prediction:\n", P)

    return I_hat, e, beta, P

def get_files_in_directory(folder):
    files = glob(os.path.join(folder, "Frame*.jpg"))
    frames = {}

    for f in files:
        filename = os.path.basename(f)
        if "_noisy" in filename:
            index = filename.replace("Frame", "").replace("_noisy.jpg", "")
            frames.setdefault(index, {})["noisy"] = f
        elif "_noise" in filename:
            index = filename.replace("Frame", "").replace("_noise.jpg", "")
            frames.setdefault(index, {})["noise"] = f
        else:
            index = filename.replace("Frame", "").replace(".jpg", "")
            frames.setdefault(index, {})["clean"] = f

    result = []
    for index in sorted(frames, key=lambda x: int(x)):
        frame = frames[index]
        if "clean" in frame and "noisy" in frame and "noise" in frame:
            result.append((frame["clean"], frame["noisy"], frame["noise"]))

    return result


if __name__ == "__main__":
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="RLS Export File")
    parser.add_argument("file_path", type=str, default="build/rls_result.txt", help="File containing the RLS output files")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    colors = []
    inputs = []
    with open(args.file_path, "r") as f:
        for line in f:
            if "Input color" in line:
                start = line.find('[') + 1
                end = line.find(']')
                float_str = line[start:end]
                v_color = [float(x.strip()) for x in float_str.split(',') if x.strip()]
                color = np.array(v_color, dtype=np.float32)
                colors.append(color)
            elif "stepSize" in line:
                start = line.find('[') + 1
                end = line.find(']')
                float_str = line[start:end]
                v_step = [float(x.strip()) for x in float_str.split(',') if x.strip()]
                input = np.array(v_step, dtype=np.float32)
                inputs.append(input)

    predictions = []

    for i in range(len(colors)):
        print(f"Processing frame {i+1}/{len(colors)}")
        color = colors[i]
        input = inputs[i]
        print(f"Color: {color}")
        print(f"Input: {input[1]}")

        I_hat, e, beta, P = rls_update(input[1], color)

        # Print results
        p_vec = np.array([1.0, input[0]])
        new_prediction = beta @ p_vec
        predictions.append(new_prediction)
        print(f"Frame {i+1} - New Prediction: {new_prediction}")
        print("---\n")

    predictions = np.array(predictions)
    colors_np = np.array(colors)

    matplotlib.use("pgf")
    matplotlib.rcParams.update({
        "pgf.texsystem": "pdflatex",
        'font.family': 'serif',
        'text.usetex': False,  # Disable LaTeX rendering to avoid \mathdefault issues
        'pgf.rcfonts': False,
        'mathtext.default': 'regular',
    })

    plt.figure(figsize=(5, 3))
    
    # Define colors and styling - keeping original red, green, blue, grey
    color_map = {'R': 'red', 'G': 'green', 'B': 'blue', 'A': 'grey'}
    markers = ['o', 's', '^', 'D']
    
    for c, channel in enumerate(['R', 'G', 'B', 'A']):
        color = color_map[channel]
        
        # Plot estimates with thicker lines
        plt.plot(predictions[:,c], 
                label=f'RLS Estimate ($\\hat{{I}}_{{{channel}}}$)', 
                color=color, 
                linestyle='-', 
                linewidth=1.5,
                alpha=0.8)
        
        # Plot actual values with smaller markers, less frequent sampling for clarity
        sample_indices = np.arange(0, len(colors_np), max(1, len(colors_np)//20))  # Sample every ~5% of points
        plt.scatter(sample_indices, colors_np[sample_indices, c], 
                   marker=markers[c], 
                   s=25, 
                   label=f'Observed ($I_{{{channel}}}$)', 
                   color=color, 
                   alpha=0.7,
                   edgecolors='white',
                   linewidth=0.5)
    
    plt.xlabel('Frame Index', fontsize=11)
    plt.ylabel('Normalized Intensity', fontsize=11)
    # plt.title('Recursive Least Squares Estimation of RGBA Channels', fontsize=12, ha='center')
    
    # Improve legend
    plt.legend(bbox_to_anchor=(1, 1), loc='upper left', fontsize=6, ncol=1,framealpha=0.7,)
    
    # Add grid and improve aesthetics
    plt.grid(True, alpha=0.3, linestyle='--', linewidth=0.5)
    plt.xlim(0, len(predictions)-1)
    
    # Set y-axis limits based on data range with some padding
    y_min = min(np.min(predictions), np.min(colors_np)) - 0.05
    y_max = max(np.max(predictions), np.max(colors_np)) + 0.05
    plt.ylim(y_min, y_max)
    
    plt.tight_layout()
    plt.savefig('rls.pgf', bbox_inches='tight', dpi=300)
    # plt.show()
```

### scripts/wavelet_denoiser.py
```
import argparse
import numpy as np
from skimage.io import imread, imsave
from skimage import img_as_float, img_as_ubyte
from skimage.restoration import denoise_wavelet

if __name__ == "__main__":
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Wavelet denoising of an image.")
    parser.add_argument("input_path", type=str, help="Path to the input image file")
    parser.add_argument("output_path", type=str, help="Path to save the output denoised image file")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    # Read image and convert to float [0, 1]
    image = imread(args.input_path)
    image_float = img_as_float(image)
    print(image_float)

    # Denoise using wavelet
    denoised = denoise_wavelet(
        image_float,
        channel_axis=-1,          # RGB channels
        convert2ycbcr=True,       # optional but often better
        method='BayesShrink',
        mode='soft',
        rescale_sigma=True
    )

    # Convert back to 8-bit for saving
    denoised_uint8 = img_as_ubyte(np.clip(denoised, 0, 1))
    imsave(args.output_path, denoised_uint8)
```

### shaders/bilateral_filter.comp
```
#version 450
#extension GL_ARB_separate_shader_objects : enable
#extension GL_ARB_shading_language_include: enable
#extension GL_EXT_debug_printf : enable

layout(local_size_x = 16, local_size_y = 16, local_size_z = 1) in;

layout(set = 0, binding = 0, rgba8) uniform image2D inputImage;
layout(set = 0, binding = 1, rgba8) uniform image2D outputImage;

layout(set = 0, binding = 2) uniform Ubo {
    float sigmaSpatial;
    float sigmaColor;
    int kernelSize;
} ubo;

void main() {
    ivec2 pixelCoord = ivec2(gl_GlobalInvocationID.xy);
    ivec2 imageSize = imageSize(inputImage);

    if (pixelCoord.x >= imageSize.x || pixelCoord.y >= imageSize.y) {
        return;
    }

    vec4 centerColor = imageLoad(inputImage, pixelCoord);
    float spatialFactor = 1.0 / (2.0 * 3.14159 * ubo.sigmaSpatial * ubo.sigmaSpatial);
    float colorFactor = 1.0 / (2.0 * 3.14159 * ubo.sigmaColor * ubo.sigmaColor);

    vec4 resultColor = vec4(0.0);
    float normalizationFactor = 0.0;

    for (int y = -ubo.kernelSize; y <= ubo.kernelSize; ++y) {
        for (int x = -ubo.kernelSize; x <= ubo.kernelSize; ++x) {
            ivec2 neighborCoord = pixelCoord + ivec2(x, y);

            if (neighborCoord.x < 0 || neighborCoord.y < 0 || neighborCoord.x >= imageSize.x || neighborCoord.y >= imageSize.y) {
                continue;
            }

            vec4 neighborColor = imageLoad(inputImage, neighborCoord);

            float spatialWeight = exp(-float(x * x + y * y) / (2.0 * ubo.sigmaSpatial * ubo.sigmaSpatial));
            float colorWeight = exp(-pow(length(neighborColor.rgb - centerColor.rgb), 2.0) / (2.0 * ubo.sigmaColor * ubo.sigmaColor)) * colorFactor;

            float weight = spatialWeight * colorWeight;

            resultColor += neighborColor * weight;
            normalizationFactor += weight;
        }
    }

    resultColor /= normalizationFactor;
    imageStore(outputImage, pixelCoord, resultColor);
}
```

### shaders/gaussian_filter.comp
```
#version 450
#extension GL_ARB_separate_shader_objects : enable
#extension GL_ARB_shading_language_include: enable
#extension GL_EXT_debug_printf : enable

layout(local_size_x = 16, local_size_y = 16, local_size_z = 1) in;

layout(set = 0, binding = 0, rgba8) uniform image2D inputImage;
layout(set = 0, binding = 1, rgba8) uniform image2D outputImage;
layout(set = 0, binding = 2) uniform Ubo {
    int kernelSize;
} ubo;

void main() {
    ivec2 pixelCoord = ivec2(gl_GlobalInvocationID.xy);
    ivec2 imageSize = imageSize(inputImage);
    vec4 color = vec4(0.0);

    int radius = ubo.kernelSize / 2;

    float sigma = float(radius) / 2.0;
    float twoSigmaSq = 2.0 * sigma * sigma;
    float normalizationFactor = 1.0 / (3.14159 * twoSigmaSq);
    float totalWeight = 0.0;

    for (int y = -radius; y <= radius; ++y) {
        for (int x = -radius; x <= radius; ++x) {
            ivec2 sampleCoord = pixelCoord + ivec2(x, y);
            sampleCoord = clamp(sampleCoord, ivec2(0), imageSize - ivec2(1));
            vec4 sampleColor = imageLoad(inputImage, sampleCoord);
            float distanceSq = float(x * x + y * y);
            float weight = normalizationFactor * exp(-distanceSq / twoSigmaSq);
            color += sampleColor * weight;
            totalWeight += weight;
        }
    }
    color /= totalWeight;

    imageStore(outputImage, pixelCoord, color);
}
```

### shaders/mean_filter.comp
```
#version 450
#extension GL_ARB_separate_shader_objects : enable
#extension GL_ARB_shading_language_include: enable
#extension GL_EXT_debug_printf : enable

layout(local_size_x = 16, local_size_y = 16, local_size_z = 1) in;

layout(set = 0, binding = 0, rgba8) uniform image2D inputImage;

layout(set = 0, binding = 1, rgba8) uniform image2D outputImage;

layout(set = 0, binding = 2) uniform Ubo{
    int kernelSize;
    bool weightedMean;
} ubo;

layout(set = 0, binding = 3, rg32f) uniform image2D noiseImage;

float calculateWeight(float stepSize) {
    float result = 1.0 / stepSize;
    return result;
}

void main() {
    ivec2 pixelCoord = ivec2(gl_GlobalInvocationID.xy);
    ivec2 imageSize = imageSize(inputImage);
    
    vec4 color = vec4(0.0);

    int halfKernel = ubo.kernelSize / 2;

    int count = 0;
    float weightSum = 0;

    for (int y = -halfKernel; y <= halfKernel; ++y) {
        for (int x = -halfKernel; x <= halfKernel; ++x) {
            ivec2 sampleCoord = pixelCoord + ivec2(x, y);
            sampleCoord = clamp(sampleCoord, ivec2(0), imageSize - ivec2(1));
            vec4 sampleColor = imageLoad(inputImage, sampleCoord);
            if (ubo.weightedMean) {
                float noiseOffset = imageLoad(noiseImage, sampleCoord).r;
                float weight = calculateWeight(noiseOffset);
                color += sampleColor * weight;
                weightSum += weight;
            }
            else {
                color += sampleColor;
            }
            count++;
        }
    }

    if (ubo.weightedMean) {
        color /= weightSum;
    }
    else {
        color /= float(count);
    }

    imageStore(outputImage, pixelCoord, color);
}
```

### shaders/median_filter.comp
```
#version 450
#extension GL_ARB_separate_shader_objects : enable
#extension GL_ARB_shading_language_include: enable
#extension GL_EXT_debug_printf : enable

layout(local_size_x = 16, local_size_y = 16, local_size_z = 1) in;

layout(set = 0, binding = 0, rgba8) uniform image2D inputImage;
layout(set = 0, binding = 1, rgba8) uniform image2D outputImage;

layout(set = 0, binding = 2) uniform Ubo {
    int kernelSize;
} ubo;

void main() {
    ivec2 pixelCoord = ivec2(gl_GlobalInvocationID.xy);
    int radius = ubo.kernelSize / 2;
    
    float kernelR[25];
    float kernelG[25];
    float kernelB[25];
    float kernelA[25];

    // load the kernel
    int index = 0;
    for (int dx = -radius; dx <= radius; ++dx) {
        for (int dy = -radius; dy <= radius; ++dy) {
            ivec2 neighborCoord = pixelCoord + ivec2(dx, dy);
            neighborCoord = clamp(neighborCoord, ivec2(0, 0), imageSize(inputImage) - ivec2(1, 1));
            vec4 color = imageLoad(inputImage, neighborCoord);
            kernelR[index] = color.r;
            kernelG[index] = color.g;
            kernelB[index] = color.b;
            kernelA[index] = color.a;
            index++;
        }
    }

    // sort the kernel
    for (int i = 0; i < ubo.kernelSize * ubo.kernelSize - 1; ++i) {
        for (int j = i + 1; j < ubo.kernelSize * ubo.kernelSize; ++j) {
            if (kernelR[i] > kernelR[j]) {
                float temp = kernelR[i];
                kernelR[i] = kernelR[j];
                kernelR[j] = temp;
            }
            if (kernelG[i] > kernelG[j]) {
                float temp = kernelG[i];
                kernelG[i] = kernelG[j];
                kernelG[j] = temp;
            }
            if (kernelB[i] > kernelB[j]) {
                float temp = kernelB[i];
                kernelB[i] = kernelB[j];
                kernelB[j] = temp;
            }
            if (kernelA[i] > kernelA[j]) {
                float temp = kernelA[i];
                kernelA[i] = kernelA[j];
                kernelA[j] = temp;
            }
        }
    }
    
    // get median value inside the kernel
    float medianR = kernelR[(ubo.kernelSize * ubo.kernelSize) / 2];
    float medianG = kernelG[(ubo.kernelSize * ubo.kernelSize) / 2];
    float medianB = kernelB[(ubo.kernelSize * ubo.kernelSize) / 2];
    float medianA = kernelA[(ubo.kernelSize * ubo.kernelSize) / 2];

    imageStore(outputImage, pixelCoord, vec4(medianR, medianG, medianB, medianA));
}
```

### shaders/noise_generation.comp
```
#version 450
#extension GL_ARB_separate_shader_objects : enable
#extension GL_ARB_shading_language_include: enable
#extension GL_EXT_debug_printf : enable

layout(local_size_x = 16, local_size_y = 16, local_size_z = 1) in;

layout(set = 0, binding = 0) uniform NoiseUbo{
    float min;
    float max;
    float ellapsedTime;
} noiseUbo;

layout(set = 0, binding = 1, rg32f) uniform image2D noiseImage;

// Uniform random: https://stackoverflow.com/questions/4200224/random-noise-functions-for-glsl
uint hash( uint x ) {
    x += ( x << 10u );
    x ^= ( x >>  6u );
    x += ( x <<  3u );
    x ^= ( x >> 11u );
    x += ( x << 15u );
    return x;
}

// Compound versions of the hashing algorithm I whipped together.
uint hash( uvec2 v ) { return hash( v.x ^ hash(v.y)                         ); }
uint hash( uvec3 v ) { return hash( v.x ^ hash(v.y) ^ hash(v.z)             ); }
uint hash( uvec4 v ) { return hash( v.x ^ hash(v.y) ^ hash(v.z) ^ hash(v.w) ); }



// Construct a float with half-open range [0:1] using low 23 bits.
// All zeroes yields 0.0, all ones yields the next smallest representable value below 1.0.
float floatConstruct( uint m ) {
    const uint ieeeMantissa = 0x007FFFFFu; // binary32 mantissa bitmask
    const uint ieeeOne      = 0x3F800000u; // 1.0 in IEEE binary32

    m &= ieeeMantissa;                     // Keep only mantissa bits (fractional part)
    m |= ieeeOne;                          // Add fractional part to 1.0

    float  f = uintBitsToFloat( m );       // Range [1:2]
    return f - 1.0;                        // Range [0:1]
}



// Pseudo-random value in half-open range [0:1].
float random( float x ) { return floatConstruct(hash(floatBitsToUint(x))); }
float random( vec2  v ) { return floatConstruct(hash(floatBitsToUint(v))); }
float random( vec3  v ) { return floatConstruct(hash(floatBitsToUint(v))); }
float random( vec4  v ) { return floatConstruct(hash(floatBitsToUint(v))); }


float randomInRage(vec3 seed, float min, float max) {
    return mix(min, max, random(seed));
}

void main(){
    vec2 coord = vec2(gl_GlobalInvocationID.xy);
    imageStore(noiseImage, ivec2(coord), vec4(
        mix(noiseUbo.min, noiseUbo.max, random(vec3(coord, noiseUbo.ellapsedTime))),
        0,
        0,
        1.0
    ));
}
```
