### shaders/raymarch.comp
```
#version 450
#extension GL_ARB_separate_shader_objects : enable
#extension GL_ARB_shading_language_include: enable
#extension GL_EXT_debug_printf : enable

#include "./utils.glsl"

layout(local_size_x = 16, local_size_y = 16, local_size_z = 1) in;

layout(set = 0, binding = 0) uniform General {
    // volumetric
    vec3 dimensions;
    vec3 bbmin;
    vec3 bbmax;

    vec2 rotationAngles;
    vec2 scalarMinMax;

    float stepSize;
    float illuminationScale;
    float ellapsedTime;
    
    int accumulation;
    int motionVectorCalc;

    int bVolume4D;
    float interpolationFactor;
    int pingpong4D;
};

layout(set = 0, binding = 1) uniform CamBlock {
    Cam cam;
};

layout(set = 0, binding = 2, rg32f) uniform image2D noiseImage;

layout(set = 0, binding = 3)  uniform sampler1D colorMapping;

layout(set = 1, binding = 0, rgba8) uniform image2D outputImage;

layout(set = 1, binding = 1) uniform sampler3D volumeTexture;

layout(set = 1, binding = 2) uniform sampler3D volumeTexture4DNextFrame;

layout(set = 1, binding = 3, rgba32f) uniform image2D positionDepthBufferImage;

layout(set = 1, binding = 4) buffer CamBlock {
    Cam previousCam;
};

layout(set = 1, binding = 5, rgba8) uniform image2D motionVectorImage;


bool rayBoxIntersect(vec3 ro, vec3 rd, vec3 minB, vec3 maxB, out float tMin, out float tMax) {
    vec3 t0 = (minB - ro) / rd;
    vec3 t1 = (maxB - ro) / rd;
    vec3 tMin3 = min(t0, t1);
    vec3 tMax3 = max(t0, t1);

    tMin = max(max(tMin3.x, tMin3.y), tMin3.z);
    tMax = min(min(tMax3.x, tMax3.y), tMax3.z);
    
    return tMax > max(tMin, 0.0);
}

void updateNoiseOffset(ivec2 pixelCoord, float m_stepSize){
    vec4 noiseOffset = imageLoad(noiseImage, pixelCoord);
    float noiseValue = noiseOffset.r;
    float stepOffset = noiseOffset.g;
    if (stepOffset >= m_stepSize) return;

    // calculate accumulation rate
    // stepOffset +=  (1 - m_stepSize) * 0.001;
    stepOffset +=  (1 / m_stepSize * 10) * 0.00001;

    imageStore(noiseImage, pixelCoord, vec4(noiseValue, stepOffset, 0.0, 0.0));
}

void main() {
    ivec2 pixelCoord = ivec2(gl_GlobalInvocationID.xy);
    vec2 uv = pixelCoordToUv(pixelCoord, imageSize(outputImage));

    if (cam.moved){
        imageStore(positionDepthBufferImage, pixelCoord, vec4(0, 0, 0, 0));
    }

    if (motionVectorCalc == 1){
        imageStore(motionVectorImage, pixelCoord, vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0));
    }

    float m_stepSize = stepSize;

    vec4 noiseOffset = imageLoad(noiseImage, pixelCoord);

    m_stepSize = noiseOffset.r == 0 ? stepSize : noiseOffset.r;

    vec3 rayOrigin = cam.transform[3].xyz;
    vec3 rayDir = rayDirection(uv, cam.proj, cam.camView);

    float tEntry, tExit;
    if (!rayBoxIntersect(rayOrigin, rayDir, bbmin, bbmax, tEntry, tExit)) {
        imageStore(outputImage, pixelCoord, vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0));
        imageStore(positionDepthBufferImage, pixelCoord, vec4(0, 0,0, 0));
        return;
    }

    if (accumulation == 1){
        updateNoiseOffset(pixelCoord, m_stepSize);
    } else {
        imageStore(noiseImage, pixelCoord, vec4(noiseOffset.r, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0));
    }

    vec3 stepDir = rayDir * m_stepSize;
    vec3 pos = rayOrigin + tEntry * rayDir;
    vec3 accumulatedColor = vec3(0.0);
    float transmittance = 1.0;
    bool firstHit = false;
    float rayOffset = imageLoad(noiseImage, pixelCoord).g;

    // Ray marching loop
    for (float t = tEntry + rayOffset; t < tExit; t += m_stepSize) {
        // handle bbox rotation
        vec3 uvw = (pos - bbmin) / (bbmax - bbmin);
        uvw -= 0.5;
        uvw = rotationY(rotationAngles.x) * uvw;
        uvw = rotationX(rotationAngles.y) * uvw;
        uvw += 0.5;

        float density = 0.0;
        if (bVolume4D == 1){
            float density1 = 0;
            float density2 = 0;
            if (pingpong4D == 0){
                density1 = texture(volumeTexture, uvw).r;
                density2 = texture(volumeTexture4DNextFrame, uvw).r;
            } else {
                density2 = texture(volumeTexture, uvw).r;
                density1 = texture(volumeTexture4DNextFrame, uvw).r;
            }
            density = mix(density1, density2, interpolationFactor);
        } else {
            density = texture(volumeTexture, uvw).r;
        }

        if (density > 0.01) {
            float colorMappingCoord = clamp((density - scalarMinMax.x) / (scalarMinMax.y - scalarMinMax.x), 0.0, 1.0);
            vec4 s = texture(colorMapping, colorMappingCoord);

            float extinction = s.a;
            float alpha = 1.0 - exp(-extinction * m_stepSize);
            vec3 contribution = transmittance * s.rgb * alpha;

            accumulatedColor += contribution;
            transmittance *= (1.0 - alpha);

            // store the first hit depth
            if (!firstHit && alpha > 0.0){
                firstHit = true;
                imageStore(positionDepthBufferImage, pixelCoord, vec4(rayOrigin + rayDir * t, t));
            }

            if (1.0 - transmittance > 0.95) break;
        }

        pos += stepDir;
    }

    float finalAlpha = 1.0 - transmittance;

    // gamma correction
    accumulatedColor.rgb = pow(accumulatedColor.rgb, vec3(1.0 / 2.2));

    vec4 finalColor = vec4(accumulatedColor, finalAlpha) * illuminationScale;

    if (accumulation == 1){
        if (!cam.moved){
            vec4 lastFrameColor = imageLoad(outputImage, pixelCoord);
            // Blend current and previous color using current alpha
            finalColor.rgb = lastFrameColor.rgb * (1.0 - finalAlpha) + finalColor.rgb * finalAlpha;
            finalColor.a = lastFrameColor.a * (1.0 - finalAlpha) + finalColor.a * finalAlpha;
        }
        else{
            imageStore(noiseImage, pixelCoord, vec4(noiseOffset.r, 0,0,0)); // reset offset to 0
        }
    } 

    // store the final color
    imageStore(outputImage, pixelCoord, finalColor);

    // calculate motion vector if needed
    if (motionVectorCalc == 1) {
        vec4 position = imageLoad(positionDepthBufferImage, pixelCoord);
        if (position.x == 0.0 && position.y == 0.0 && position.z == 0.0) {
            imageStore(motionVectorImage, pixelCoord, vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0));
            return;
        }
        float depth = position.w;

        vec2 uv = pixelCoordToUv(pixelCoord, imageSize(positionDepthBufferImage));
        vec3 rayOrigin = previousCam.transform[3].xyz;
        vec3 rayDir = rayDirection(uv, previousCam.proj, previousCam.camView);
        vec3 pos = rayOrigin + rayDir * depth;
        ivec2 reprojectedCoord = reproject(cam, vec4(pos, 1), imageSize(positionDepthBufferImage));

        ivec2 size = imageSize(motionVectorImage);
        vec2 diff = (pixelCoordToUv(pixelCoord, size) -pixelCoordToUv(reprojectedCoord, size)) * 0.5 + 0.5; // map to [0, 1] range
        imageStore(motionVectorImage, pixelCoord, vec4(diff, 0.0, 1.0));
    }
}
```

### shaders/rls_denoiser.comp
```
#version 450
#extension GL_ARB_separate_shader_objects : enable
#extension GL_ARB_shading_language_include: enable
#extension GL_EXT_debug_printf : enable

layout(local_size_x = 16, local_size_y = 16, local_size_z = 1) in;

#include "./defs.glsl"
#include "./utils.glsl"

layout(set = 0, binding = 0) uniform RLSUbos{
    float lambdaStatic;
    float lambdaMoving;
    float noiseMin;
    float noiseMax;
    int visualizationExport;
    vec2 visualizationExportPixel;
};

layout(set = 0, binding = 1) buffer RLSPixels {
    RLSPixel rlsPixels[];
};

layout(set = 0, binding = 2) buffer ReprojectedRLSPixels{
    RLSPixel reprojectedRlsPixels[];
};

layout(set = 0, binding = 3, rgba8) uniform image2D inputImage;

layout(set = 0, binding = 4, rgba8) uniform image2D outputImage;

layout(set = 0, binding = 5, rg32f) uniform image2D noiseImage;

layout(set = 0, binding = 6, rgba32f) uniform image2D positionDepthBufferImage;

layout(set = 0, binding = 7, rgba32f) uniform image2D previousPositionDepthBufferImage;

layout(set = 0, binding = 8) uniform CamBlock {
    Cam cam;
};

layout(set = 0, binding = 9) buffer CamPreviousBlock{
    Cam previousCam;
};


void saveCurrentToPrevious(ivec2 coord){
    vec4 currentPos = imageLoad(positionDepthBufferImage, coord);
    imageStore(previousPositionDepthBufferImage, coord, currentPos);
    // previousCam.camView = cam.camView;
    // previousCam.proj= cam.proj;
    // previousCam.transform = cam.transform;
}

float normalizeNoise(float noise) {
    return (noise - noiseMin) / (noiseMax - noiseMin);
}

void main () {
    ivec2 coord = ivec2(gl_GlobalInvocationID.xy);
    ivec2 size = imageSize(inputImage);
    if (coord.x >= size.x || coord.y >= size.y) return;

    saveCurrentToPrevious(coord);

    vec4 color = imageLoad(inputImage, coord);
    vec4 position = imageLoad(positionDepthBufferImage, coord);
    if (position.x == 0.0 && position.y == 0.0 && position.z == 0.0) {
        imageStore(outputImage, coord, vec4(0.0));
        return;
    }

    //  RLS Estimation
    RLSPixel rlsPixel = rlsPixels[coord.y * size.x + coord.x];
    RLSPixel reprojectedRlsPixel = reprojectedRlsPixels[coord.y * size.x + coord.x];

    vec4 iTilde = color; // noisy input
    vec2 p = vec2(1, imageLoad(noiseImage, coord).r * 10);
    vec4 iHat = p * reprojectedRlsPixel.beta; // prediction

    vec4 energy = iTilde - iHat;

    float lambda = cam.moved ? lambdaMoving : lambdaStatic;
    
    float denom = lambda + dot(p , reprojectedRlsPixel.prediction *p);
    vec2 temp = reprojectedRlsPixel.prediction * p;
    
    vec2 q = reprojectedRlsPixel.prediction * p / denom;

    mat4x2 beta =mat4x2(0);
    for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
        beta[i] += reprojectedRlsPixel.beta[i] + q * energy[i];
    }   

    mat2 outterPQ = mat2(
        q.x * p.x, q.y * p.x,
        q.x * p.y, q.y * p.y
    );

    mat2 prediction = (1.0/lambda) * (reprojectedRlsPixel.prediction - outterPQ * reprojectedRlsPixel.prediction);

    rlsPixels[coord.y * size.x + coord.x].beta = beta;
    rlsPixels[coord.y * size.x + coord.x].prediction = prediction;
    imageStore(outputImage, coord, iHat);

    // visualiation export
    if (visualizationExport == 1 && coord == ivec2(visualizationExportPixel)) {
        debugPrintfEXT("stepSize [%f, %f]\n", p[0], p[1]);
        debugPrintfEXT("Energy [%f, %f, %f, %f]\n", energy[0], energy[1], energy[2], energy[3]);
        debugPrintfEXT("outterPQ [%f, %f, %f, %f]\n", outterPQ[0][0], outterPQ[0][1], outterPQ[1][0], outterPQ[1][1]);
        debugPrintfEXT("Input color [%f, %f, %f, %f]\n", iTilde[0], iTilde[1], iTilde[2], iTilde[3]);
        debugPrintfEXT("beta: [%f %f %f %f %f %f %f %f]\n", beta[0][0], beta[0][1], beta[1][0], beta[1][1], beta[2][0], beta[2][1], beta[3][0], beta[3][1]);
        debugPrintfEXT("Prediction [%f %f %f %f]\n",
                       prediction[0][0], prediction[0][1], prediction[1][0], prediction[1][1]);
        debugPrintfEXT("------------------------------");
    }
}
```

### shaders/rls_reprojection.comp
```
#version 450
#extension GL_ARB_separate_shader_objects : enable
#extension GL_ARB_shading_language_include: enable
#extension GL_EXT_debug_printf : enable

#include "./defs.glsl"
#include "./utils.glsl"

layout(local_size_x = 16, local_size_y = 16, local_size_z = 1) in;

layout(set = 0, binding = 0) uniform CamBlock {
    Cam cam;
};

layout(set = 0, binding = 1) buffer RLSPixels {
    RLSPixel rlsPixels[];
};

layout(set = 0, binding = 2) buffer ReprojectedRLSPixels{
    RLSPixel reprojectedRlsPixels[];
};

layout(set = 0, binding = 3, rgba32f) uniform image2D positionDepthBufferImage;

layout(set = 0, binding = 4, rgba32f) uniform image2D previousPositionDepthBufferImage;

layout(set = 0, binding = 5) buffer CamPreviousBlock{
    Cam previousCam;
};

float depthMeanFilter(ivec2 imageSize, ivec2 coord, int kernelSize) {
    float sum = 0.0;
    int count = 0;
    int halfKernel = kernelSize / 2;

    for (int y = -halfKernel; y <= halfKernel; ++y) {
        for (int x = -halfKernel; x <= halfKernel; ++x) {
            ivec2 sampleCoord = coord + ivec2(x, y);
            sampleCoord = clamp(sampleCoord, ivec2(0), imageSize - ivec2(1));
            vec4 sampleColor = imageLoad(previousPositionDepthBufferImage, coord);
            sum += sampleColor.a;
            count++;
        }
    }
    return sum / float(count);
}


void main () {
    ivec2 coord = ivec2(gl_GlobalInvocationID.xy);
    ivec2 size  = imageSize(positionDepthBufferImage);
    if (coord.x >= size.x || coord.y >= size.y) return;

    RLSPixel rlsPixel = rlsPixels[coord.y * size.x + coord.x];

    // reset reprojected pixel to current
    reprojectedRlsPixels[coord.y * size.x + coord.x] = rlsPixel;

    vec4 position = vec4(imageLoad(previousPositionDepthBufferImage, coord).xyz, 1);
    if (position.x == 0.0 && position.y == 0.0 && position.z == 0.0) {
        return;
    }

    float depth = depthMeanFilter(imageSize(previousPositionDepthBufferImage), coord, 4);
    vec2 uv = pixelCoordToUv(coord, imageSize(previousPositionDepthBufferImage));
    vec3 rayOrigin = previousCam.transform[3].xyz;
    vec3 rayDir = rayDirection(uv, previousCam.proj, previousCam.camView);
    vec3 pos = rayOrigin + rayDir * depth;

    ivec2 reprojectedCoord = reproject(cam, vec4(pos, 1), size);

    reprojectedRlsPixels[reprojectedCoord.y * size.x + reprojectedCoord.x] = rlsPixel;
}
```

### shaders/temporal_wmean_denoiser.comp
```
#version 450
#extension GL_ARB_separate_shader_objects : enable
#extension GL_ARB_shading_language_include: enable
#extension GL_EXT_debug_printf : enable

#include "./defs.glsl"

layout(local_size_x = 16, local_size_y = 16, local_size_z = 1) in;

layout (set = 0, binding = 0) uniform ubo{
    int finalMeanFilter;
};

layout(set = 0, binding = 1, rgba8) uniform image2D inputImage;

layout(set = 0, binding = 2, rgba8) uniform image2D outputImage;

layout(set = 0, binding = 3, rgba32f) uniform image2D reprojectedInputImage;

layout(set = 0, binding = 4, r32f) uniform image2D reprojectedWeightImage;

layout(set = 0, binding = 5, rgba32f) uniform image2D positionDepthBufferImage;

layout(set = 0, binding = 6, rgba32f) uniform image2D historyBufferImage;

layout(set = 0, binding = 7, r32f) uniform image2D historyWeightImage;

layout(set = 0, binding = 8, rgba32f) uniform image2D previousPositionDepthBufferImage;

layout(set = 0, binding = 9, rg32f) uniform image2D noiseImage;   

layout(set = 0, binding = 10) uniform CamBlock {
    Cam cam;
};  
layout(set = 0, binding = 11) buffer PreviousCamBlock{
    Cam previousCam;
};

// Save the current history and position image as previous
void saveCurrentToPrevious(ivec2 coord){
    vec4 currentPos = imageLoad(positionDepthBufferImage, coord);
    imageStore(previousPositionDepthBufferImage, coord, currentPos);
    //previousCam.camView = cam.camView;
    //previousCam.proj= cam.proj;
    //previousCam.transform = cam.transform;
}

int kernel = 4;

vec4 temporalWeightedMean(){
    vec4 historyColor = imageLoad(reprojectedInputImage, ivec2(gl_GlobalInvocationID.xy)) * 0.5;
    float historyWeight = imageLoad(reprojectedWeightImage, ivec2(gl_GlobalInvocationID.xy)).r * 0.5;

    // clear up history
    imageStore(historyBufferImage, ivec2(gl_GlobalInvocationID.xy), vec4(0));
    imageStore(historyWeightImage, ivec2(gl_GlobalInvocationID.xy), vec4(0));

    int halfKernel = kernel / 2;
    float weightSum = historyWeight;
    vec4 color = historyColor;

    if (finalMeanFilter == 0){
        float weight = 1.0 / imageLoad(noiseImage, ivec2(gl_GlobalInvocationID.xy)).r;
        weightSum += weight;
        color = imageLoad(inputImage, ivec2(gl_GlobalInvocationID.xy));
        return color;
    } else {
        for (int y = -halfKernel; y <= halfKernel; ++y){
            for(int x = -halfKernel; x <= halfKernel; ++x){
                ivec2 sampleCoord = ivec2(gl_GlobalInvocationID.xy) + ivec2(x, y);
                sampleCoord = clamp(sampleCoord, ivec2(0), imageSize(inputImage) - ivec2(1));
                vec4 sampleColor = imageLoad(inputImage, sampleCoord);
                float noiseOffset = imageLoad(noiseImage, sampleCoord).r;
                float weight = 1.0 / noiseOffset;
                if (sampleColor.rgb == vec3(0)){
                    weight = 0;
                }
                color += sampleColor * weight;
                weightSum += weight;
            }
        }
    }

    imageStore(historyBufferImage, ivec2(gl_GlobalInvocationID.xy), color);
    imageStore(historyWeightImage, ivec2(gl_GlobalInvocationID.xy), vec4(weightSum, 0, 0, 0));

    color /= weightSum;

    return color;
}

void main (){
    ivec2 pixelCoords = ivec2(gl_GlobalInvocationID.xy);
    saveCurrentToPrevious(pixelCoords);

    imageStore(outputImage, pixelCoords, temporalWeightedMean());
}
```

### shaders/total_variation.comp
```
#version 450
#extension GL_ARB_separate_shader_objects : enable

layout(local_size_x = 16, local_size_y = 16, local_size_z = 1) in;

layout(set = 0, binding = 0, rgba8) uniform image2D inputImage;
layout(set = 0, binding = 1, rgba8) uniform image2D outputImage;

layout(std140, binding = 2) uniform TotalVariationUbos {
    float lambda;
    int iterations; // host controls iterations
} totalVariationUbo;

const float tau = 0.125;
const float eps = 1e-6;

ivec2 clampCoord(ivec2 p, ivec2 size) {
    return clamp(p, ivec2(0), size - 1);
}

void main() {
    ivec2 coord = ivec2(gl_GlobalInvocationID.xy);
    ivec2 size  = imageSize(inputImage);
    if (coord.x >= size.x || coord.y >= size.y) return;

    // central pixel
    vec4 u = imageLoad(inputImage, coord);
    vec4 f = u; // input data (could be original noisy image if stored elsewhere)

    // neighbors
    vec4 uR  = imageLoad(inputImage, clampCoord(coord + ivec2(1, 0), size));
    vec4 uL  = imageLoad(inputImage, clampCoord(coord + ivec2(-1, 0), size));
    vec4 uD  = imageLoad(inputImage, clampCoord(coord + ivec2(0, 1), size));
    vec4 uU  = imageLoad(inputImage, clampCoord(coord + ivec2(0, -1), size));

    // forward differences at current pixel
    vec4 ux = uR - u;
    vec4 uy = uD - u;
    vec4 mag = sqrt(ux * ux + uy * uy + vec4(eps));
    vec4 px = ux / mag;
    vec4 py = uy / mag;

    // compute px at left neighbor (backward difference at left)
    // ux_left = u - uL
    vec4 uxL = u - uL;
    // for the vertical difference at left neighbor we need (uL_down - uL)
    vec4 uLdown = imageLoad(inputImage, clampCoord(coord + ivec2(-1, 1), size));
    vec4 uyL = uLdown - uL;
    vec4 magL = sqrt(uxL * uxL + uyL * uyL + vec4(eps));
    vec4 pxL = uxL / magL;

    // compute py at up neighbor (backward difference at up)
    // uy_up = u - uU
    vec4 uyU = u - uU;
    // for the horizontal diff at up neighbor we need (uUp_right - uU)
    vec4 uUpRight = imageLoad(inputImage, clampCoord(coord + ivec2(1, -1), size));
    vec4 uxU = uUpRight - uU;
    vec4 magU = sqrt(uxU * uxU + uyU * uyU + vec4(eps));
    vec4 pyU = uyU / magU;

    // divergence = (px - px_left) + (py - py_up)
    vec4 div = (px - pxL) + (py - pyU);

    vec4 newU = u + tau * (f - u + totalVariationUbo.lambda * div);
    newU = clamp(newU, vec4(0.0), vec4(1.0));

    imageStore(outputImage, coord, newU);
}
```

### shaders/utils.glsl
```
#ifndef UTILS_GLSL
#define UTILS_GLSL

#include "defs.glsl"

ivec2 uvToPixelCoord(vec2 uv, vec2 imageSize){
    uv.y *= -1.0;
    vec2 pixelCoord = ((uv + 1.0) * 0.5) * imageSize - 0.5;
    return ivec2(round(pixelCoord));
}

vec2 pixelCoordToUv(vec2 pixelCoord, vec2 imageSize){
    vec2 uv = (pixelCoord + 0.5) / imageSize * 2.0 - 1.0;
    uv.y *= -1.0;
    return uv;
}

mat3 rotationY(float angle) {
    float c = cos(angle);
    float s = sin(angle);
    return mat3(
        c, 0.0, s,
        0.0, 1.0, 0.0,
       -s, 0.0, c
    );
}

mat3 rotationX(float angle) {
    float c = cos(angle);
    float s = sin(angle);
    return mat3(
        1.0, 0.0, 0.0,
        0.0, c, -s,
        0.0, s, c
    );
}

vec3 rayDirection(vec2 uv, mat4 proj, mat4 camView) {
    vec4 clip = vec4(uv, -1.0, 1.0);
    vec4 view = inverse(proj) * clip;
    view = vec4(view.xyz / view.w, 0.0);
    return normalize((inverse(camView) * view).xyz);
}

ivec2 reproject(Cam cam, vec4 previousPosition, vec2 imageSize) {
    vec4 clipSpace = cam.proj * cam.camView * previousPosition;
    clipSpace /= clipSpace.w;
    return uvToPixelCoord(clipSpace.xy, imageSize);
}

float rand(vec2 co){
    return fract(sin(dot(co, vec2(12.9898, 78.233))) * 43758.5453);
}

float randomInRage(float time, float min, float max) {
    vec2 seed = vec2(time * 10, time * 5);
    return mix(min, max, rand(seed));
}

#endif
```

### shaders/wiener_filter.comp
```
#version 450
#extension GL_ARB_separate_shader_objects : enable

layout(local_size_x = 16, local_size_y = 16, local_size_z = 1) in;

layout(set = 0, binding = 0, rgba8) uniform image2D inputImage;
layout(set = 0, binding = 1, rgba8) uniform image2D outputImage;
layout(set = 0, binding = 2) uniform Ubo {
    int kernelSize;
    float noiseVariance;
};

// Algorithm: https://de.mathworks.com/help/images/ref/wiener2.html
void main () {
    ivec2 coord = ivec2(gl_GlobalInvocationID.xy);
    ivec2 imgSize = imageSize(inputImage);

    vec4 mean = vec4(0.0);
    vec4 meanSq = vec4(0.0);
    int count = 0;
    int filterRadius = kernelSize / 2;
    for (int dy = -filterRadius; dy <= filterRadius; ++dy) {
        for (int dx = -filterRadius; dx <= filterRadius; ++dx) {
            ivec2 offset = clamp(coord + ivec2(dx, dy), ivec2(0), imgSize - ivec2(1));
            vec4 s = imageLoad(inputImage, offset);
            mean += s;
            meanSq += s * s;
            count++;
        }
    }

    mean /= float(count);
    meanSq /= float(count);
    vec4 variance = meanSq - mean * mean;
    vec4 noisy = imageLoad(inputImage, coord);

    vec4 factor = max(vec4(0.0), (variance - noiseVariance) / variance);
    vec4 denoised = mean + factor * (noisy - mean);

    imageStore(outputImage, coord, denoised);
}
```

### shaders/wmean_reprojection.comp
```
#version 450
#extension GL_ARB_separate_shader_objects : enable
#extension GL_ARB_shading_language_include: enable
#extension GL_EXT_debug_printf : enable

#include "./defs.glsl"
#include "./utils.glsl"

layout(local_size_x = 16, local_size_y = 16, local_size_z = 1) in;

layout(set = 0, binding = 0) uniform CamBlock {
    Cam cam;
};

layout(set = 0, binding = 1, rgba8) uniform image2D inputImage;

layout(set = 0, binding = 2, rgba32f) uniform image2D reprojectionOutputImage;

layout(set = 0, binding = 3, r32f) uniform image2D reprojectionWeightImage;

layout(set = 0, binding = 4, rgba32f) uniform image2D previousPositionDepthBufferImage;

layout(set = 0, binding = 5, rgba32f) uniform image2D positionDepthBufferImage;

layout(set = 0, binding = 6, rgba32f) uniform image2D historyColorSumImage;

layout(set = 0, binding = 7, r32f) uniform image2D historyWeightSumImage;

layout(set = 0, binding = 8) buffer PreviousCamBlock{
    Cam previousCam;
};

float depthMeanFilter(ivec2 imageSize, ivec2 coord, int kernelSize) {
    float sum = 0.0;
    int count = 0;
    int halfKernel = kernelSize / 2;

    for (int y = -halfKernel; y <= halfKernel; ++y) {
        for (int x = -halfKernel; x <= halfKernel; ++x) {
            ivec2 sampleCoord = coord + ivec2(x, y);
            sampleCoord = clamp(sampleCoord, ivec2(0), imageSize - ivec2(1));
            vec4 sampleColor = imageLoad(previousPositionDepthBufferImage, coord);
            sum += sampleColor.a;
            count++;
        }
    }
    return sum / float(count);
}

void main() {
    ivec2 coord = ivec2(gl_GlobalInvocationID.xy);
    ivec2 size  = imageSize(inputImage);
    if (coord.x >= size.x || coord.y >= size.y) return;

    // clear the reprojection output image
    imageStore(reprojectionOutputImage, coord, vec4(0.0));
    imageStore(reprojectionWeightImage, coord, vec4(0.0));

    vec4 historyColorSum = imageLoad(historyColorSumImage, coord);
    float historyWeight = imageLoad(historyWeightSumImage, coord).r;
    vec4 currentPixel = imageLoad(inputImage, coord);

    vec4 position = vec4(imageLoad(previousPositionDepthBufferImage, coord).xyz,1);
    if (position.x == 0.0 && position.y == 0.0 && position.z == 0.0) {
        return;
    }

    float depth = depthMeanFilter(imageSize(previousPositionDepthBufferImage), coord, 4);
    vec2 uv = pixelCoordToUv(coord, imageSize(previousPositionDepthBufferImage));
    vec3 rayOrigin = previousCam.transform[3].xyz;
    vec3 rayDir = rayDirection(uv, previousCam.proj, previousCam.camView);
    vec3 pos = rayOrigin + rayDir * depth;

    ivec2 pixelCoord = reproject(cam, vec4(pos, 1), size);
    
    imageStore(reprojectionOutputImage, pixelCoord, historyColorSum);
    imageStore(reprojectionWeightImage, pixelCoord, vec4(historyWeight, 0,0,0));
}
```

### nn/training/denoise_lstm_dataset.py
```
import os
import random
from PIL import Image, ImageStat

from torch.utils.data import Dataset, DataLoader
import torchvision.transforms.functional as TF
from tqdm import tqdm
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
import torch


class DenoiseLstmDataset(Dataset):
    def __init__(
        self,
        root_dir,
        patch_size=256,
        patch_stride=64,
        transform=None,
        cache_dir="cache",
        augment=True,
        sequence_length=4,
    ):
        self.root_dir = root_dir
        self.patch_size = patch_size
        self.patch_stride = patch_stride
        self.augment = augment
        self.transform = transform or TF.to_tensor
        self.cache_dir = os.path.join(root_dir, cache_dir)
        self.sequence_length = sequence_length
        self.sequences = []

        if not os.path.exists(self.cache_dir):
            print("Preprocessing patches...")
            self.preprocess_and_cache()

        self._build_sequences()

    def __len__(self):
        return len(self.sequences)

    def _build_sequences(self):
        """Build sequences of consecutive patches for LSTM training."""
        # Group patches by dataset and coordinates

        patch_groups = {}

        for patch_file in os.listdir(os.path.join(self.cache_dir, "gt")):
            if not patch_file.endswith(".jpg"):
                continue

            # Parse patch filename: prefix_filename_top_left.jpg
            parts = patch_file[:-4].split("_")
            if len(parts) < 4:
                continue

            prefix = parts[0]
            filename = "_".join(parts[1:-2])
            top = parts[-2]
            left = parts[-1]

            # Extract frame number from filename (format like Frame0, Frame1000, etc.)
            try:
                if filename.startswith("Frame"):
                    frame_num = int(filename[5:])  # Remove "Frame" prefix
                else:
                    continue
            except (ValueError, IndexError):
                continue

            key = f"{prefix}_{top}_{left}"
            if key not in patch_groups:
                patch_groups[key] = []

            patch_groups[key].append((frame_num, patch_file))

        # Create sequences from each group
        for key, patches in patch_groups.items():
            # Sort by frame number
            patches.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])

            # Create sequences of consecutive frames
            for i in range(len(patches) - self.sequence_length + 1):
                sequence_patches = []
                valid_sequence = True

                for j in range(self.sequence_length):
                    frame_num, patch_file = patches[i + j]

                    # Check if frames are consecutive
                    if j > 0 and frame_num != patches[i + j - 1][0] + 1:
                        valid_sequence = False
                        break

                    noisy_path = os.path.join(self.cache_dir, "noisy", patch_file)
                    gt_path = os.path.join(self.cache_dir, "gt", patch_file)
                    noise_path = os.path.join(self.cache_dir, "noise", patch_file)
                    motion_vec_path = os.path.join(self.cache_dir, "motion_vector", patch_file)

                    required_paths = [noisy_path, gt_path, noise_path, motion_vec_path]
                    
                    if all(os.path.exists(p) for p in required_paths):
                        # Check if motion vector exists, include if available
                        sequence_patches.append((noisy_path, gt_path, noise_path, motion_vec_path))
                    else:
                        valid_sequence = False
                        break

                if valid_sequence and len(sequence_patches) == self.sequence_length:
                    self.sequences.append(sequence_patches)

    def preprocess_and_cache(self):
        os.makedirs(os.path.join(self.cache_dir, "noisy"), exist_ok=True)
        os.makedirs(os.path.join(self.cache_dir, "gt"), exist_ok=True)
        os.makedirs(os.path.join(self.cache_dir, "noise"), exist_ok=True)
        os.makedirs(os.path.join(self.cache_dir, "motion_vector"), exist_ok=True)

        # First, determine consistent patch locations across all datasets
        # by checking the first frame of the first dataset
        first_dataset = None
        for subdir in os.listdir(self.root_dir):
            if subdir.endswith("-gt"):
                first_dataset = subdir[:-3]
                break
        
        if first_dataset is None:
            print("No dataset found!")
            return
            
        # Get image size from first frame to determine patch locations
        first_gt_dir = os.path.join(self.root_dir, f"{first_dataset}-gt")
        first_frame = sorted(os.listdir(first_gt_dir))[0]
        sample_img = Image.open(os.path.join(first_gt_dir, first_frame)).convert("RGB")
        W, H = sample_img.size
        
        # Generate all possible patch locations
        all_locations = [
            (left, top)
            for top in range(0, H - self.patch_size + 1, self.patch_stride)
            for left in range(0, W - self.patch_size + 1, self.patch_stride)
        ]
        
        # Use a fixed random seed to ensure consistent patch selection across runs
        random.seed(42)
        # Select a reasonable number of patch locations (more than 5 to get better sequences)
        num_patches = min(20, len(all_locations))  # Use up to 20 patches instead of 5
        selected_locations = random.sample(all_locations, num_patches)
        random.seed()  # Reset random seed
        
        print(f"Selected {len(selected_locations)} consistent patch locations")

        def process_directory(subdir):
            if subdir.endswith("-gt"):
                prefix = subdir[:-3]
                gt_dir = os.path.join(self.root_dir, f"{prefix}-gt")
                noisy_dir = os.path.join(self.root_dir, f"{prefix}-noisy")

                if not os.path.exists(noisy_dir):
                    return

                filenames = sorted(os.listdir(gt_dir))
                for fname in tqdm(filenames, desc=f"Processing files in {subdir}"):
                    try:
                        gt_img = Image.open(os.path.join(gt_dir, fname)).convert("RGB")
                        noisy_img = Image.open(os.path.join(noisy_dir, fname)).convert("RGB")
                        noise_img = Image.open(
                            os.path.join(noisy_dir, fname.split(".")[0] + "_noise.jpg")
                        ).convert("RGB")
                        motion_vec_img = Image.open(
                            os.path.join(noisy_dir, fname.split(".")[0] + "_motion_vector.jpg")
                        ).convert("RGB")
                    except Exception as e:
                        print(f"Error loading images for {fname}: {e}")
                        continue

                    # Use the same consistent patch locations for all frames
                    for left, top in selected_locations:
                        box = (left, top, left + self.patch_size, top + self.patch_size)

                        gt_patch = gt_img.crop(box)
                        noisy_patch = noisy_img.crop(box)
                        noise_patch = noise_img.crop(box)
                        motion_vec_patch = motion_vec_img.crop(box)

                        # Skip patches that are too dark (low signal)
                        stat = ImageStat.Stat(noisy_patch)
                        if (sum(stat.mean)) < 0.1:
                            continue

                        patch_id = f"{prefix}_{fname[:-4]}_{top}_{left}.jpg"
                        gt_patch.save(os.path.join(self.cache_dir, "gt", patch_id))
                        noisy_patch.save(
                            os.path.join(self.cache_dir, "noisy", patch_id)
                        )
                        noise_patch.save(
                            os.path.join(self.cache_dir, "noise", patch_id)
                        )
                        motion_vec_patch.save(
                            os.path.join(self.cache_dir, "motion_vector", patch_id)
                        )

        with ThreadPoolExecutor() as executor:
            list(executor.map(process_directory, os.listdir(self.root_dir)))

        print("Patch preprocessing complete.")

    def __getitem__(self, idx):
        """Load a sequence of 4 images for LSTM training."""
        sequence_paths = self.sequences[idx]

        noisy_sequence = []
        gt_sequence = []
        noise_sequence = []
        motion_vec_sequence = []

        for sequence_item in sequence_paths:
            if len(sequence_item) == 4:
                noisy_path, gt_path, noise_path, motion_vec_path = sequence_item
            else:
                noisy_path, gt_path, noise_path = sequence_item
                motion_vec_path = None

            # Load images
            noisy_img = Image.open(noisy_path).convert("RGB")
            gt_img = Image.open(gt_path).convert("RGB")
            noise_img = Image.open(noise_path).convert("RGB")
            
            # Load motion vector if available
            motion_vec_img = None
            if motion_vec_path and os.path.exists(motion_vec_path):
                motion_vec_img = Image.open(motion_vec_path).convert("RGB")

            # Apply augmentation if enabled
            if self.augment and random.random() > 0.5:
                # Random horizontal flip
                if random.random() > 0.5:
                    noisy_img = TF.hflip(noisy_img)
                    gt_img = TF.hflip(gt_img)
                    noise_img = TF.hflip(noise_img)
                    if motion_vec_img is not None:
                        motion_vec_img = TF.hflip(motion_vec_img)

                # Random vertical flip
                if random.random() > 0.5:
                    noisy_img = TF.vflip(noisy_img)
                    gt_img = TF.vflip(gt_img)
                    noise_img = TF.vflip(noise_img)
                    if motion_vec_img is not None:
                        motion_vec_img = TF.vflip(motion_vec_img)

                # Random rotation (90, 180, 270 degrees)
                if random.random() > 0.5:
                    angle = random.choice([90, 180, 270])
                    noisy_img = TF.rotate(noisy_img, angle)
                    gt_img = TF.rotate(gt_img, angle)
                    noise_img = TF.rotate(noise_img, angle)
                    if motion_vec_img is not None:
                        motion_vec_img = TF.rotate(motion_vec_img, angle)
                
                if random.random() > 0.5:
                    brightness = random.uniform(0.8, 1.2)
                    contrast = random.uniform(0.8, 1.2)
                    saturation = random.uniform(0.8, 1.2)

                    noisy_img = TF.adjust_brightness(noisy_img, brightness)
                    noisy_img = TF.adjust_contrast(noisy_img, contrast)
                    noisy_img = TF.adjust_saturation(noisy_img, saturation)

                    gt_img = TF.adjust_brightness(gt_img, brightness)
                    gt_img = TF.adjust_contrast(gt_img, contrast)
                    gt_img = TF.adjust_saturation(gt_img, saturation)

            # Convert to tensor
            noisy_tensor = self.transform(noisy_img)
            gt_tensor = self.transform(gt_img)
            noise_tensor = self.transform(noise_img)
            motion_vec_tensor = self.transform(motion_vec_img)

            noisy_sequence.append(noisy_tensor)
            gt_sequence.append(gt_tensor)
            noise_sequence.append(noise_tensor)
            
            motion_vec_sequence.append(motion_vec_tensor)

        # Stack tensors to create sequences
        # Shape: (sequence_length, channels, height, width)
        noisy_sequence = torch.stack(noisy_sequence)
        gt_sequence = torch.stack(gt_sequence)
        noise_sequence = torch.stack(noise_sequence)
        motion_vec_sequence = torch.stack(motion_vec_sequence)

        return {
            "noisy": noisy_sequence, 
            "gt": gt_sequence, 
            "noise": noise_sequence,
            "motion_vector": motion_vec_sequence
        }

    def __repr__(self):
        return f"DenoiseLstmDataset(sequences={len(self.sequences)}, sequence_length={self.sequence_length}, patch_size={self.patch_size})"
```

### nn/training/denoise_unet_dataset.py
```
import os
import random
from PIL import Image, ImageStat
from torch.utils.data import Dataset, DataLoader
from torchvision import transforms
import torchvision.transforms.functional as TF
from tqdm import tqdm
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor


class DenoiseUnetDataset(Dataset):
    def __init__(
        self,
        root_dir,
        patch_size=256,
        patch_stride=64,
        transform=None,
        cache_dir="cache",
        augment=True,
    ):
        self.root_dir = root_dir
        self.patch_size = patch_size
        self.patch_stride = patch_stride
        self.augment = augment
        self.transform = transform or transforms.ToTensor()
        self.cache_dir = os.path.join(root_dir, cache_dir)
        self.patches = []

        if not os.path.exists(self.cache_dir):
            print("Preprocessing patches...")
            self.preprocess_and_cache()

        for subdir in os.listdir(os.path.join(self.cache_dir, "gt")):
            base_name = subdir[:-4]  # remove .jpg
            noisy_path = os.path.join(self.cache_dir, "noisy", subdir)
            gt_path = os.path.join(self.cache_dir, "gt", subdir)
            noise_path = os.path.join(self.cache_dir, "noise", subdir)
            if all(os.path.exists(p) for p in [noisy_path, gt_path, noise_path]):
                self.patches.append((noisy_path, gt_path, noise_path))

    def preprocess_and_cache(self):
        os.makedirs(os.path.join(self.cache_dir, "noisy"), exist_ok=True)
        os.makedirs(os.path.join(self.cache_dir, "gt"), exist_ok=True)
        os.makedirs(os.path.join(self.cache_dir, "noise"), exist_ok=True)

        def process_directory(subdir):
            if subdir.endswith("-gt"):
                prefix = subdir[:-3]
                gt_dir = os.path.join(self.root_dir, f"{prefix}-gt")
                noisy_dir = os.path.join(self.root_dir, f"{prefix}-noisy")

                if not os.path.exists(noisy_dir):
                    return

                filenames = sorted(os.listdir(gt_dir))
                for fname in tqdm(filenames, desc=f"Processing files in {subdir}"):
                    gt_img = Image.open(os.path.join(gt_dir, fname)).convert("RGB")
                    noisy_img = Image.open(os.path.join(noisy_dir, fname)).convert(
                        "RGB"
                    )
                    noise_img = Image.open(
                        os.path.join(noisy_dir, fname.split(".")[0] + "_noise.jpg")
                    ).convert("RGB")

                    W, H = gt_img.size
                    locations = [
                        (left, top)
                        for top in range(0, H - self.patch_size + 1, self.patch_stride)
                        for left in range(0, W - self.patch_size + 1, self.patch_stride)
                    ]
                    random_locations = random.sample(locations, min(5, len(locations)))

                    for left, top in random_locations:
                        box = (left, top, left + self.patch_size, top + self.patch_size)

                        gt_patch = gt_img.crop(box)
                        noisy_patch = noisy_img.crop(box)
                        noise_patch = noise_img.crop(box)

                        # skip black patches
                        stat = ImageStat.Stat(noisy_patch)
                        if (sum(stat.mean)) < 0.1:
                            continue

                        patch_id = f"{prefix}_{fname[:-4]}_{top}_{left}.jpg"
                        gt_patch.save(os.path.join(self.cache_dir, "gt", patch_id))
                        noisy_patch.save(
                            os.path.join(self.cache_dir, "noisy", patch_id)
                        )
                        noise_patch.save(
                            os.path.join(self.cache_dir, "noise", patch_id)
                        )

        with ThreadPoolExecutor() as executor:
            list(executor.map(process_directory, os.listdir(self.root_dir)))

        print("Patch preprocessing complete.")

    def __len__(self):
        return len(self.patches)

    def __getitem__(self, idx):
        noisy_path, gt_path, noise_fname = self.patches[idx]
        noisy_img = Image.open(noisy_path).convert("RGB")
        gt_img = Image.open(gt_path).convert("RGB")

        noise_img = Image.open(noise_fname).convert("RGB")

        # Apply augmentation if enabled
        if self.augment:
            noisy_img, gt_img, noise_img = self.apply_augmentation(
                noisy_img, gt_img, noise_img
            )

        if self.transform:
            noisy_img = self.transform(noisy_img)
            gt_img = self.transform(gt_img)
            noise_img = self.transform(noise_img)
        else:
            noisy_img = self.to_tensor(noisy_img)
            gt_img = self.to_tensor(gt_img)
            noise_img = self.to_tensor(noise_img)

        noise_img = (noise_img - noise_img.min()) / (noise_img.max() - noise_img.min())
        return noisy_img, gt_img, noise_img

    def apply_augmentation(self, noisy_img, gt_img, noise_img):
        """Apply synchronized augmentations to both noisy and ground truth images"""

        if random.random() > 0.5:
            noisy_img = TF.hflip(noisy_img)
            gt_img = TF.hflip(gt_img)
            noise_img = TF.hflip(noise_img)

        if random.random() > 0.5:
            noisy_img = TF.vflip(noisy_img)
            gt_img = TF.vflip(gt_img)
            noise_img = TF.vflip(noise_img)

        if random.random() > 0.5:
            angle = random.choice([0, 90, 180, 270])
            noisy_img = TF.rotate(noisy_img, angle)
            gt_img = TF.rotate(gt_img, angle)
            noise_img = TF.rotate(noise_img, angle)

        if random.random() > 0.5:
            brightness = random.uniform(0.8, 1.2)
            contrast = random.uniform(0.8, 1.2)
            saturation = random.uniform(0.8, 1.2)
        
            noisy_img = TF.adjust_brightness(noisy_img, brightness)
            noisy_img = TF.adjust_contrast(noisy_img, contrast)
            noisy_img = TF.adjust_saturation(noisy_img, saturation)

            gt_img = TF.adjust_brightness(gt_img, brightness)
            gt_img = TF.adjust_contrast(gt_img, contrast)
            gt_img = TF.adjust_saturation(gt_img, saturation)

        return noisy_img, gt_img, noise_img
```

### nn/training/denoise_unet_model.py
```
import torch
import torch.nn as nn

class DenoisingUnet(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self):
        super(DenoisingUnet, self).__init__()

        # Encoder
        self.enc1 = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Conv2d(6, 32, kernel_size=3, padding=1),  # (B, 32, H, W)
            nn.ReLU()
        )
        self.down = nn.Conv2d(32, 64, kernel_size=3, stride=2, padding=1)  # (B, 64, H/2, W/2)
        self.enc2 = nn.ReLU()

        # Decoder
        self.up = nn.ConvTranspose2d(64, 32, kernel_size=2, stride=2)  # (B, 32, H, W)
        self.dec1 = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Conv2d(32 + 32, 32, kernel_size=3, padding=1),
            nn.ReLU()
        )

        self.out = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Conv2d(32 + 6, 3, kernel_size=3, padding=1),
            nn.Sigmoid()
        )

    def forward(self, noisy_img, noise_pattern):
        x_input = torch.cat((noisy_img, noise_pattern), dim=1)  # (B, 6, H, W)

        # Encoder
        x1 = self.enc1(x_input)  # (B, 32, H, W)
        x = self.down(x1)        # (B, 64, H/2, W/2)
        x = self.enc2(x)         # (B, 64, H/2, W/2)

        # Decoder
        x = self.up(x)           # (B, 32, H, W)
        x = torch.cat([x, x1], dim=1)  # (B, 64, H, W)
        x = self.dec1(x)         # (B, 32, H, W)

        x = torch.cat([x, x_input], dim=1)  # (B, 38, H, W)
        x = self.out(x)          # (B, 3, H, W)

        return x
```

### nn/training/denoiser_lstm_model.py
```
import torch
import torch.nn as nn


class Down(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, in_ch, out_ch):
        super().__init__()
        self.conv = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Conv2d(in_ch, out_ch, 3, padding=1),
            nn.LeakyReLU(0.1),
            nn.Conv2d(out_ch, out_ch, 3, padding=1),
            nn.LeakyReLU(0.1),
        )
        self.pool = nn.MaxPool2d(2)

    def forward(self, x):
        x = self.conv(x)
        return self.pool(x), x


class Up(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, in_ch, out_ch, skip_ch):
        super().__init__()
        self.upconv = nn.ConvTranspose2d(in_ch, out_ch, 2, stride=2)
        self.conv = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Conv2d(out_ch + skip_ch, out_ch, 3, padding=1),
            nn.LeakyReLU(0.1),
            nn.Conv2d(out_ch, out_ch, 3, padding=1),
            nn.LeakyReLU(0.1),
        )

    def forward(self, x, skip):
        x = self.upconv(x)
        x = torch.cat([x, skip], dim=1)
        return self.conv(x)


class ConvLSTMCell(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, input_dim, hidden_dim, kernel_size=3):
        super().__init__()
        padding = kernel_size // 2
        self.hidden_dim = hidden_dim

        self.conv = nn.Conv2d(
            input_dim + hidden_dim, 4 * hidden_dim, kernel_size, padding=padding
        )

    def forward(self, x, h_cur, c_cur):
        combined = torch.cat([x, h_cur], dim=1)
        combined_conv = self.conv(combined)
        cc_i, cc_f, cc_o, cc_g = torch.chunk(combined_conv, 4, dim=1)

        i = torch.sigmoid(cc_i)
        f = torch.sigmoid(cc_f)
        o = torch.sigmoid(cc_o)
        g = torch.tanh(cc_g)

        c_next = f * c_cur + i * g
        h_next = o * torch.tanh(c_next)

        return h_next, c_next


class ConvLSTM(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, input_dim, hidden_dim, kernel_size=3):
        super().__init__()
        self.cell = ConvLSTMCell(input_dim, hidden_dim, kernel_size)

    def forward(self, x):
        B, T, C, H, W = x.size()
        h = torch.zeros(B, self.cell.hidden_dim, H, W, device=x.device)
        c = torch.zeros(B, self.cell.hidden_dim, H, W, device=x.device)

        outputs = []
        for t in range(T):
            h, c = self.cell(x[:, t], h, c)
            outputs.append(h)

        return torch.stack(outputs, dim=1)


class UNetConvLSTMDenoiser(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, input_channels=3, hidden_dim=16):
        super().__init__()

        self.down1 = Down(input_channels * 3, 16)
        self.down2 = Down(16, 8)

        self.convlstm = ConvLSTM(input_dim=8, hidden_dim=hidden_dim)

        # Adjust up blocks accordingly
        self.up2 = Up(hidden_dim, 8, skip_ch=8)
        self.up1 = Up(8, 8, skip_ch=16)
        self.out = nn.Conv2d(8, 3, 1)

    def forward(self, noisy_sequence, motion_vector_sequence, noise_sequence):
        B, T, C, H, W = noisy_sequence.shape
        skips1 = []
        skips2 = []
        # skips3 = []
        bottlenecks = []
        for t in range(T):
            combined_input = torch.cat(
                [
                    noisy_sequence[:, t],
                    motion_vector_sequence[:, t],
                    noise_sequence[:, t],
                ],
                dim=1,
            )
            x1, skip1 = self.down1(combined_input)
            x2, skip2 = self.down2(x1)
            # x3, skip3 = self.down3(x2)
            skips1.append(skip1)
            skips2.append(skip2)
            # skips3.append(skip3)
            bottlenecks.append(x2)
        bottlenecks = torch.stack(bottlenecks, dim=1)  # (B, T, C, H, W)
        lstm_out = self.convlstm(bottlenecks)  # (B, T, hidden_dim, H, W)
        t = T - 1
        x = self.up2(lstm_out[:, t], skips2[t])
        x = self.up1(x, skips1[t])
        out = self.out(x)
        return out  # (B, 3, H, W)
```

### nn/training/lstm_main.py
```
import torch
from torchvision import transforms
from pytorch_msssim import MS_SSIM
import glob
import os
import shutil

from denoise_lstm_dataset import DenoiseLstmDataset
from denoiser_lstm_model import UNetConvLSTMDenoiser

if __name__ == "__main__":
    device = torch.device("cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu")
    print(f"Using device: {device}")

    transform = transforms.Compose([transforms.ToTensor()])
    dataset = DenoiseLstmDataset(
        root_dir="dataset/lstm",
        patch_size=256,
        transform=transform,
        cache_dir="cache",
        augment=True,
        sequence_length=4,
    )

    dataLoader = torch.utils.data.DataLoader(
        dataset, batch_size=16, shuffle=True, num_workers=4
    )

    model = UNetConvLSTMDenoiser().to(device)
    optimizer = torch.optim.Adam(model.parameters(), lr=1e-4)

    checkpoint_files = glob.glob("./training/checkpoints/lstm_checkpoint_batch_*.pth")
    if checkpoint_files:
        latest_checkpoint = max(checkpoint_files, key=os.path.getctime)
        print(f"Loading checkpoint: {latest_checkpoint}")
        model.load_state_dict(torch.load(latest_checkpoint))

    ms_ssim_criterion = MS_SSIM(data_range=1.0, size_average=True, channel=3)
    alpha = 0.85

    def combined_loss(output, target):
        ssim_loss = 1 - ms_ssim_criterion(output, target)
        l1_loss = torch.nn.L1Loss()(output, target)
        return alpha * ssim_loss + (1 - alpha) * l1_loss

    num_epochs = 3
    checkpoint_interval_batches = 500
    batch_count_since_last_checkpoint = 0

    print(f"Starting training for {num_epochs} epochs...")

    for epoch in range(num_epochs):
        model.train()
        total_loss = 0.0
        num_batches = 0

        for i, batch in enumerate(dataLoader):
            noisy_sequence = batch["noisy"].to(device)
            gt_sequence = batch["gt"].to(device)
            noise_sequence = batch["noise"].to(device)
            motion_vector_sequence = batch["motion_vector"].to(device)

            optimizer.zero_grad()

            denoised_frame = model(
                noisy_sequence, motion_vector_sequence, noise_sequence
            )  # (B, C, H, W)

            loss = combined_loss(denoised_frame, gt_sequence[:, -1])

            loss.backward()
            optimizer.step()

            total_loss += loss.item()
            num_batches += 1
            batch_count_since_last_checkpoint += 1

            if i % 10 == 0:
                print(
                    f"Epoch [{epoch+1}/{num_epochs}], Batch [{i+1}/{len(dataLoader)}], Loss: {loss.item():.6f}"
                )

            if batch_count_since_last_checkpoint >= checkpoint_interval_batches:
                if not os.path.exists("training/checkpoints"):
                    os.makedirs("training/checkpoints")
                torch.save(
                    model.state_dict(),
                    f"training/checkpoints/lstm_checkpoint_batch_{(epoch * len(dataLoader)) + i + 1}.pth",
                )
                print(
                    f"Model checkpoint saved at batch {(epoch * len(dataLoader)) + i + 1}"
                )
                batch_count_since_last_checkpoint = 0

        avg_loss = total_loss / num_batches
        print(f"Epoch [{epoch+1}/{num_epochs}] completed. Average Loss: {avg_loss:.6f}")

    torch.save(model.state_dict(), "final_lstm_denoiser.pth")
    print("Training completed. Final model saved.")

    # Export to ONNX
    sample_noisy = torch.randn(1, 4, 3, 720, 1080).to(device)
    sample_motion_vector = torch.randn(1, 4, 3, 720, 1080).to(device)
    sample_noise = torch.randn(1, 4, 3, 720, 1080).to(device)
    torch.onnx.export(
        model,
        (sample_noisy, sample_motion_vector, sample_noise),
        "models/lstm_model_final.onnx",
        input_names=["noisy", "motion_vector", "noise"],
        output_names=["output"],
        opset_version=17,
        do_constant_folding=True
    )
    print("Model exported to ONNX format as final_lstm_denoiser.onnx")

    # inference on full-resolution images
    from PIL import Image
    import torchvision.utils as vutils

    def load_fullres_sequence(image_paths, transform):
        imgs = [transform(Image.open(p).convert("RGB")) for p in image_paths]
        return torch.stack(imgs, dim=0)  # (T, C, H, W)

    dataset_dir = "dataset/lstm/dataset1-noisy"
    gt_dataset_dir = "dataset/lstm/dataset1-gt"
    start_index = 0
    fullres_noisy_paths = [
        f"{dataset_dir}/Frame{start_index}.jpg",
        f"{dataset_dir}/Frame{start_index + 1}.jpg",
        f"{dataset_dir}/Frame{start_index + 2}.jpg",
        f"{dataset_dir}/Frame{start_index + 3}.jpg",
    ]
    fullres_motion_paths = [
        f"{dataset_dir}/Frame{start_index}_motion_vector.jpg",
        f"{dataset_dir}/Frame{start_index + 1}_motion_vector.jpg",
        f"{dataset_dir}/Frame{start_index + 2}_motion_vector.jpg",
        f"{dataset_dir}/Frame{start_index + 3}_motion_vector.jpg",
    ]
    fullres_noise_paths = [
        f"{dataset_dir}/Frame{start_index}_noise.jpg",
        f"{dataset_dir}/Frame{start_index + 1}_noise.jpg",
        f"{dataset_dir}/Frame{start_index + 2}_noise.jpg",
        f"{dataset_dir}/Frame{start_index + 3}_noise.jpg",
    ]

    # Load and preprocess
    noisy_seq = (
        load_fullres_sequence(fullres_noisy_paths, transform).unsqueeze(0).to(device)
    )  # (1, T, C, H, W)
    motion_seq = (
        load_fullres_sequence(fullres_motion_paths, transform).unsqueeze(0).to(device)
    )
    noise_seq = (
        load_fullres_sequence(fullres_noise_paths, transform).unsqueeze(0).to(device)
    )

    model.eval()
    os.makedirs("results", exist_ok=True)
    with torch.no_grad():
        denoised_frame = model(noisy_seq, motion_seq, noise_seq)  # (1, C, H, W)
        denoised_frame = denoised_frame.clamp(0, 1)
        vutils.save_image(denoised_frame[0], "results/lstm_denoised_fullres.jpg")
        shutil.copy(
            f"{gt_dataset_dir}/Frame{start_index + 3}.jpg",
            "results/lstm_gt_fullres.jpg",
        )
        shutil.copy(
            f"{dataset_dir}/Frame{start_index + 3}.jpg",
            "results/lstm_noisy_fullres.jpg",
        )
        print("Saved denoised full-res image to results/lstm_denoised_fullres.jpg")
```

### nn/training/unet_main.py
```
import torch
import os
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.optim as optim
from nn.training.denoise_unet_dataset import DenoiseUnetDataset
from nn.training.denoise_unet_model import DenoisingUnet
from torch.utils.data import DataLoader
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
from torchvision.utils import make_grid
from torchvision import transforms
import torchvision.transforms.functional as TF
from pytorch_msssim import MS_SSIM

if __name__ == "__main__":
    device = torch.device("cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu")
    print(f"Using device: {device}")

    transform = transforms.Compose([
        transforms.ToTensor()
    ])
    dataset = DenoiseUnetDataset(root_dir="./dataset/unet", transform=transform)
    dataloader = DataLoader(dataset, batch_size=32, shuffle=True, num_workers=8)

    model = DenoisingUnet().to(device)

    ms_ssim_criterion = MS_SSIM(data_range=1.0, size_average=True, channel=3)
    alpha = 0.85
    def combined_loss(output, target):
        ssim_loss = 1 - ms_ssim_criterion(output, target)
        l1_loss = nn.L1Loss()(output, target)
        return alpha * ssim_loss + (1 - alpha) * l1_loss

    optimizer = optim.Adam(model.parameters(), lr=0.001)
    
    scheduler = optim.lr_scheduler.ReduceLROnPlateau(optimizer, mode='min', factor=0.5, patience=5)

    # Load existing checkpoint if available
    start_epoch = 0
    for epoch_num in range(10, 0, -1):  # Check from epoch 10 down to 1
        checkpoint_path = f'training/checkpoints/denoising_model_epoch_{epoch_num}.pth'
        if os.path.exists(checkpoint_path):
            print(f"Loading checkpoint from epoch {epoch_num}")
            model.load_state_dict(torch.load(checkpoint_path, map_location=device))
            start_epoch = epoch_num
            break
    
    if start_epoch > 0:
        print(f"Resuming training from epoch {start_epoch + 1}")
    else:
        print("Starting training from scratch")

    num_epochs = 3
    print_every = 100

    model.train()
    for epoch in range(start_epoch, num_epochs):
        running_loss = 0.0
        
        for batch_idx, (noisy_imgs, gt_imgs, noise_imgs) in enumerate(dataloader):
            noisy_imgs = noisy_imgs.to(device)
            gt_imgs = gt_imgs.to(device)
            noise_imgs = noise_imgs.to(device)
            
            outputs = model(noisy_imgs, noise_imgs)
            loss = combined_loss(outputs, gt_imgs)
            
            optimizer.zero_grad()
            loss.backward()
            optimizer.step()
            
            running_loss += loss.item()
            
            if batch_idx % print_every == 0:
                print(f'Epoch [{epoch+1}/{num_epochs}], Batch [{batch_idx}], Loss: {loss.item():.7f}')
        
        avg_loss = running_loss / len(dataloader)
        print(f'Epoch [{epoch+1}/{num_epochs}] completed, Average Loss: {avg_loss:.7f}')
        
        scheduler.step(avg_loss)
        
        if (epoch + 1) % 2 == 0:
            torch.save(model.state_dict(), f'training/checkpoints/unt_model_epoch_{epoch+1}.pth')
            print(f'Model saved at epoch {epoch+1}')

    torch.save(model.state_dict(), 'training/checkpoints/unet_model_final.pth')
    input_tensor = torch.randn(1, 3, 720, 1080).to(device)
    input_tensor2 = torch.randn(1, 3, 720, 1080).to(device)
    torch.onnx.export(model, (input_tensor, input_tensor2), 'models/denoising_model_final.onnx',
                    export_params=True, opset_version=11, do_constant_folding=True,
                    input_names=['noisy_image', "noise_image"], output_names=['output'])
    print('Training completed')

    model.eval()
    with torch.no_grad():
        for noisy_imgs, gt_imgs, noise_pattern in dataloader:
            noisy_imgs = noisy_imgs.to(device)
            gt_imgs = gt_imgs.to(device)
            noise_pattern = noise_pattern.to(device)
            single_noisy = noisy_imgs[0] # (3, 720, 1080)
            single_gt = gt_imgs[0]
            single_noisy_batch = single_noisy.unsqueeze(0) #(1, 3, 720, 1080)
            noise_pattern = noise_pattern[0].unsqueeze(0)
            print(single_noisy_batch.shape)
            denoised = model(single_noisy_batch, noise_pattern) #(1, 3, 720, 1080)
            denoised = denoised.squeeze(0) # (3, 720, 1080)

            os.makedirs('results', exist_ok=True)
            
            noisy_img = transforms.ToPILImage()(single_noisy.cpu())
            gt_img = transforms.ToPILImage()(single_gt.cpu())
            output_img = transforms.ToPILImage()(denoised.cpu().clamp(0, 1))
            
            noisy_img.save('results/noisy.jpg')
            gt_img.save('results/ground_truth.jpg')
            output_img.save('results/denoised.jpg')
            
            print('Saved single image to results/')
            
            break
```

### renderer/denoisers/bilateral_filter.cpp
```
#include "bilateral_filter.h"

#include "../utils/container.h"
#include "../utils/utils.h"
#include "imgui.h"

BilateralFilter::BilateralFilter(GFX::VkContext &context, AppConfig &appConfig) : IDenoiser(context, appConfig)
{
    m_bilateralUbos.sigmaSpatial = appConfig.Denoiser.ScreenSpaced.BilateralFilter.sigmaSpatial;
    m_bilateralUbos.sigmaColor   = appConfig.Denoiser.ScreenSpaced.BilateralFilter.sigmaColor;
    m_bilateralUbos.kernelSize   = appConfig.Denoiser.ScreenSpaced.BilateralFilter.kernelSize;
}

void BilateralFilter::InitPass(const GFX::VkImageObject &inputImage)
{
    IDenoiser::InitPass(inputImage);

    Utils::CreateAndCopyBuffer(m_context, &m_bilateralUbos, sizeof(BilateralUbos),
                               vk::BufferUsageFlagBits::eUniformBuffer | vk::BufferUsageFlagBits::eTransferDst,
                               VMA_MEMORY_USAGE_GPU_ONLY, m_bilateralUboDeviceBuffer);
    GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::ElementUniformBuffer bilateralFilterDescriptorElement{*m_bilateralUboDeviceBuffer};
    InitPassScreenspace(inputImage, m_appConfig.Denoiser.ScreenSpaced.BilateralFilter.shaderPath,
                        {&bilateralFilterDescriptorElement});
}

void BilateralFilter::DrawDenoiserUI()
{
    bool result = false;
    result |= ImGui::SliderFloat("Sigma Spatial", &m_bilateralUbos.sigmaSpatial, 0.0f, 10.0f);
    result |= ImGui::SliderFloat("Sigma Color", &m_bilateralUbos.sigmaColor, 0.0f, 10.0f);
    result |= ImGui::SliderInt("Kernel Size", &m_bilateralUbos.kernelSize, 2, 6);
    if (result)
    {
        m_bilateralUbos.kernelSize = std::clamp(m_bilateralUbos.kernelSize, 2, 6);
        g_DirtyBuffers.insert({m_bilateralUboDeviceBuffer->Buffer, 0, sizeof(BilateralUbos), &m_bilateralUbos});
    }
}
```

### renderer/denoisers/bilateral_filter.h
```
#pragma once

#include "denoiser.h"

/**
 * @brief Bilateral filter denoiser.
 */
class BilateralFilter : public IDenoiser
{
  public:
    BilateralFilter(GFX::VkContext &context, AppConfig &appconfig);
    void InitPass(const GFX::VkImageObject &inputImage) override;
    void DrawDenoiserUI() override;

  private:
    struct BilateralUbos
    {
        alignas(4) float sigmaSpatial;
        alignas(4) float sigmaColor;
        alignas(4) int kernelSize;
    } m_bilateralUbos;

    std::optional<GFX::VkBufferObject> m_bilateralUboDeviceBuffer;
};
```

### renderer/denoisers/denoiser.cpp
```
#include "denoiser.h"
#include "../utils/utils.h"

IDenoiser::IDenoiser(GFX::VkContext &context, AppConfig &appconfig) : m_context(context), m_appConfig{appconfig}
{
    m_inputImage = nullptr;
}

void IDenoiser::InitPass(const GFX::VkImageObject &inputImage)
{
    CacheInputImage(inputImage);
    InitOutputImage();
}

void IDenoiser::InitOutputImage()
{
    OutputImage.emplace(m_context, m_context.Config().WindowWidth, m_context.Config().WindowHeight, 1,
                        vk::Format::eR8G8B8A8Unorm, true);
    OutputImage->TransitionImageLayout(vk::ImageLayout::eGeneral);
    OutputImage->SetName("Denoiser Output Image");
}

void IDenoiser::BarrierInputImage(GFX::VkCommandBufferObject &commandBuffer) const
{
    vk::ImageMemoryBarrier barrier{};
    barrier.srcAccessMask                   = vk::AccessFlagBits::eShaderWrite;
    barrier.dstAccessMask                   = vk::AccessFlagBits::eShaderRead;
    barrier.oldLayout                       = vk::ImageLayout::eGeneral;
    barrier.newLayout                       = vk::ImageLayout::eGeneral;
    barrier.image                           = m_inputImage->Image;
    barrier.subresourceRange.aspectMask     = vk::ImageAspectFlagBits::eColor;
    barrier.subresourceRange.baseMipLevel   = 0;
    barrier.subresourceRange.levelCount     = 1;
    barrier.subresourceRange.baseArrayLayer = 0;
    barrier.subresourceRange.layerCount     = 1;

    commandBuffer.GetCommandBuffer().pipelineBarrier(vk::PipelineStageFlagBits::eComputeShader,
                                                     vk::PipelineStageFlagBits::eComputeShader, {}, {}, {}, {barrier});
}

void IDenoiser::RecordPass(GFX::VkCommandBufferObject &commandBuffer, uint32_t currentFrame)
{
    BarrierInputImage(commandBuffer);
    commandBuffer.StartComputePass(*Pass);
    commandBuffer.BindDescriptorSet(*Pass, {DescriptorSet->DescriptorSets[currentFrame]});
    commandBuffer.Dispatch((m_context.Config().WindowWidth + 15) / 16, (m_context.Config().WindowHeight + 15) / 16, 1);
}

void IDenoiser::CacheInputImage(const GFX::VkImageObject &inputImage)
{
    m_inputImage = const_cast<GFX::VkImageObject *>(&inputImage);
}

void IDenoiser::InitPassScreenspace(const GFX::VkImageObject &inputImage, std::string shaderPath,
                                    std::vector<GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::IDescriptorElement *> additionalElements)
{
    auto shaderCode = Utils::ReadFile(shaderPath);

    GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::ElementStorageImage inputDescriptorImage{inputImage};
    GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::ElementStorageImage outputDescriptorImage{*OutputImage};

    std::vector<GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::IDescriptorElement *> descriptorElements{&inputDescriptorImage,
                                                                                     &outputDescriptorImage};
    descriptorElements.insert(descriptorElements.end(), additionalElements.begin(), additionalElements.end());

    DescriptorSet.emplace(m_context, descriptorElements);
    Pass.emplace(m_context, shaderCode, std::vector<vk::DescriptorSetLayout>{DescriptorSet->DescriptorSetLayout});
}
```

  protected:

    void InitPassScreenspace(const GFX::VkImageObject &inputImage, std::string shaderPath);

  protected:

    AppConfig          &m_appConfig;

GaussianFilter::GaussianFilter(GFX::VkContext &context, AppConfig &appconfig) : IDenoiser(context, appconfig)
{
    m_gaussianFilterUbos.kernelSize = appconfig.Denoiser.ScreenSpaced.GaussianFilter.kernelSize;
}

void GaussianFilter::InitPass(const GFX::VkImageObject &inputImage)
{
    IDenoiser::InitPass(inputImage);

    Utils::CreateAndCopyBuffer(m_context, &m_gaussianFilterUbos, sizeof(GaussianFilterUbos),
                               vk::BufferUsageFlagBits::eUniformBuffer | vk::BufferUsageFlagBits::eTransferDst,
                               VMA_MEMORY_USAGE_GPU_ONLY, m_gaussianFilterUboDeviceBuffer);
    m_gaussianFilterUboDeviceBuffer->SetName("Gaussian Filter UBO");
    GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::ElementUniformBuffer gaussianFilterDescriptorElement{*m_gaussianFilterUboDeviceBuffer};
    InitPassScreenspace(inputImage, m_appConfig.Denoiser.ScreenSpaced.GaussianFilter.shaderPath,
                        {&gaussianFilterDescriptorElement});
}

void GaussianFilter::DrawDenoiserUI()
{
    if (ImGui::InputInt("Kernel Size", &m_gaussianFilterUbos.kernelSize, 2))
    {
        m_gaussianFilterUbos.kernelSize = std::clamp(m_gaussianFilterUbos.kernelSize, 0, 10);
        g_DirtyBuffers.insert(
            {m_gaussianFilterUboDeviceBuffer->Buffer, 0, sizeof(GaussianFilterUbos), &m_gaussianFilterUbos});
    }
}

### renderer/denoisers/lstm_denoiser.cpp
```
#ifdef TRAINED_DENOISER_ON
#include <numeric>

#include "../utils/exporter.h"
#include "imgui.h"
#include "lstm_denoiser.h"

LSTMDenoiser::LSTMDenoiser(GFX::VkContext &context, AppConfig &config, Noise &noise,
                           GFX::VkImageObject &motionVectorImage) :
    IDenoiser(context, config),
    m_context(context),
    m_config(config),
    m_noise(noise),
    m_motionVectorImage(motionVectorImage),
    m_memoryInfo(Ort::MemoryInfo::CreateCpu(OrtArenaAllocator, OrtMemTypeDefault))
{
    m_windowWidth  = context.Config().WindowWidth;
    m_windowHeight = context.Config().WindowHeight;

    Ort::ThrowOnError(OrtSessionOptionsAppendExecutionProvider_CUDA(m_sessionOptions, 0));
    m_sessionOptions.SetGraphOptimizationLevel(GraphOptimizationLevel::ORT_ENABLE_ALL);

#if defined(__linux__)
    m_session.emplace(m_env, config.Denoiser.LSTM.nnModelPath.c_str(), m_sessionOptions);
#elif defined(_WIN32) || defined(_WIN64)
    std::wstring modelPathW(config.Denoiser.LSTM.nnModelPath.begin(), config.Denoiser.LSTM.nnModelPath.end());
    m_session.emplace(m_env, modelPathW.c_str(), m_sessionOptions);
#endif

    size_t        numInputs  = m_session->GetInputCount();
    auto          inputName  = m_session->GetInputNameAllocated(0, m_allocator);
    Ort::TypeInfo typeInfo   = m_session->GetInputTypeInfo(0);
    auto          tensorInfo = typeInfo.GetTensorTypeAndShapeInfo();
    m_inputShape             = tensorInfo.GetShape();

    Ort::TypeInfo outputTypeInfo   = m_session->GetOutputTypeInfo(0);
    auto          tensorOutputInfo = outputTypeInfo.GetTensorTypeAndShapeInfo();
    m_outputShape                  = tensorOutputInfo.GetShape();

    if (m_inputShape[3] != m_windowHeight || m_inputShape[4] != m_windowWidth)
    {
        throw std::runtime_error("Input shape does not match expected dimensions");
    }

    m_inputSize = std::accumulate(m_inputShape.begin(), m_inputShape.end(), int64_t(1),
                                  [](int64_t a, int64_t b) { return a * b; });

    m_outputSize = std::accumulate(m_outputShape.begin(), m_outputShape.end(), int64_t(1),
                                   [](int64_t a, int64_t b) { return a * b; });
    m_colorInputData.resize(m_inputSize);
    m_noiseInputData.resize(m_inputSize);
    m_motionVectorInputData.resize(m_inputSize);
}

void LSTMDenoiser::InitPass(const GFX::VkImageObject &inputImage)
{
    CacheInputImage(inputImage);
    InitOutputImage();

    size_t numPixels  = inputImage.Extent.width * inputImage.Extent.height;
    size_t bufferSize = numPixels * 4;

    // 3 history image sets + 1 current image set for lstm
    for (int i = 0; i < 3; ++i)
    {
        m_historyColorImages.emplace_back(std::make_unique<GFX::VkImageObject>(
            m_context, inputImage.Extent.width, inputImage.Extent.height, 1, vk::Format::eR8G8B8A8Unorm, true));
        m_historyColorImages[i]->TransitionImageLayout(vk::ImageLayout::eGeneral);
        m_historyNoiseImages.emplace_back(std::make_unique<GFX::VkImageObject>(
            m_context, inputImage.Extent.width, inputImage.Extent.height, 1, vk::Format::eR32G32Sfloat, true));
        m_historyNoiseImages[i]->TransitionImageLayout(vk::ImageLayout::eGeneral);
        m_historyMotionVectorImages.emplace_back(std::make_unique<GFX::VkImageObject>(
            m_context, inputImage.Extent.width, inputImage.Extent.height, 1, vk::Format::eR8G8B8A8Unorm, true));
        m_historyMotionVectorImages[i]->TransitionImageLayout(vk::ImageLayout::eGeneral);
    }
}

void LSTMDenoiser::RecordPass(GFX::VkCommandBufferObject &commandBuffer, uint32_t currentFrame)
{
    InferencePreprocess();

    m_inferenceTimer.start();
    std::vector<Ort::Value> inputTensors = {};
    inputTensors.push_back(std::move(*m_noisyImageTensor));
    inputTensors.push_back(std::move(*m_motionVectorImageTensor));
    inputTensors.push_back(std::move(*m_noiseImageTensor));

    m_session->Run(Ort::RunOptions{nullptr}, inputNames, inputTensors.data(), 3, &outputNames,
                   &m_outputImageTensor.value(), 1);
    auto                &outputTensor = m_outputImageTensor.value();
    float               *outputData   = outputTensor.GetTensorMutableData<float>();
    std::vector<int64_t> outputShape  = outputTensor.GetTensorTypeAndShapeInfo().GetShape();

    if (outputShape[2] != m_windowHeight || outputShape[3] != m_windowWidth)
    {
        throw std::runtime_error("Output shape does not match expected dimensions");
    }

    m_inferenceTimer.stop();

    InferencePostprocess(outputData, outputShape);

    // shift one image in history
    for (int i = 0; i < 3; ++i)
    {
        if (i == 0)
        {
            continue;
        }

        commandBuffer.ImageCopy(m_historyColorImages[i].get(), m_historyColorImages[i - 1].get());
        commandBuffer.ImageCopy(m_historyNoiseImages[i].get(), m_historyNoiseImages[i - 1].get());
        commandBuffer.ImageCopy(m_historyMotionVectorImages[i].get(), m_historyMotionVectorImages[i - 1].get());
    }

    // save current into history
    commandBuffer.ImageCopy(m_inputImage, m_historyColorImages[2].get());
    commandBuffer.ImageCopy(&m_noise.NoiseTexture.value(), m_historyNoiseImages[2].get());
    commandBuffer.ImageCopy(&m_motionVectorImage, m_historyMotionVectorImages[2].get());
}

void LSTMDenoiser::InferencePreprocess()
{
    m_preprocessingTimer.start();
    const int sequenceLength = 4;        // 4 frames
    const int channels       = 3;
    const int height         = m_windowHeight;
    const int width          = m_windowWidth;

    m_colorInputData.resize(sequenceLength * channels * height * width);
    m_noiseInputData.resize(sequenceLength * channels * height * width);
    m_motionVectorInputData.resize(sequenceLength * channels * height * width);

    for (int seq = 0; seq < sequenceLength; ++seq)
    {
        std::vector<uint8_t> singleInputImageBuffer;
        std::vector<uint8_t> singleNoiseImageBuffer;
        std::vector<uint8_t> singleMotionVectorImageBuffer;

        if (seq == 3)        // current frame
        {
            singleInputImageBuffer        = m_exporter.ExportImageToBuffer(*m_inputImage, false);
            singleNoiseImageBuffer        = m_exporter.ExportImageToBuffer(*m_noise.NoiseTexture, false);
            singleMotionVectorImageBuffer = m_exporter.ExportImageToBuffer(m_motionVectorImage, false);
        }
        else        // history frames
        {
            singleInputImageBuffer        = m_exporter.ExportImageToBuffer(*m_historyColorImages[seq], false);
            singleNoiseImageBuffer        = m_exporter.ExportImageToBuffer(*m_historyNoiseImages[seq], false);
            singleMotionVectorImageBuffer = m_exporter.ExportImageToBuffer(*m_historyMotionVectorImages[seq], false);
        }

#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
        for (int idx = 0; idx < width * height; ++idx)
        {
            int   pixelIndex = idx * 4;
            float alphaInput = singleInputImageBuffer[pixelIndex + 3] / 255.0f;

            for (int c = 0; c < channels; ++c)
            {
                size_t nchwIndex = ((seq * channels + c) * height * width) + idx;

                m_colorInputData[nchwIndex]        = (singleInputImageBuffer[pixelIndex + c] / 255.0f) * alphaInput;
                m_noiseInputData[nchwIndex]        = singleNoiseImageBuffer[pixelIndex + c] / 255.0f;
                m_motionVectorInputData[nchwIndex] = singleMotionVectorImageBuffer[pixelIndex + c] / 255.0f;
            }
        }
    }

    Ort::Value colorTensor = Ort::Value::CreateTensor<float>(
        m_memoryInfo, m_colorInputData.data(), m_colorInputData.size(), m_inputShape.data(), m_inputShape.size());
    m_noisyImageTensor.emplace(std::move(colorTensor));

    Ort::Value noiseTensor = Ort::Value::CreateTensor<float>(
        m_memoryInfo, m_noiseInputData.data(), m_noiseInputData.size(), m_inputShape.data(), m_inputShape.size());
    m_noiseImageTensor.emplace(std::move(noiseTensor));

    Ort::Value motionVectorTensor =
        Ort::Value::CreateTensor<float>(m_memoryInfo, m_motionVectorInputData.data(), m_motionVectorInputData.size(),
                                        m_inputShape.data(), m_inputShape.size());
    m_motionVectorImageTensor.emplace(std::move(motionVectorTensor));

    m_outputImageTensor.emplace(Ort::Value::CreateTensor<float>(
        m_memoryInfo, m_colorInputData.data(), m_colorInputData.size(), m_outputShape.data(), m_outputShape.size()));
    m_preprocessingTimer.stop();
}

void LSTMDenoiser::InferencePostprocess(float *outputData, std::vector<int64_t> &outputShape)
{
    m_postprocessingTimer.start();
    std::vector<uint8_t> outputImageBuffer(m_windowWidth * m_windowHeight * 4);

#pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static)
    for (int h = 0; h < m_windowHeight; ++h)
    {
        for (int w = 0; w < m_windowWidth; ++w)
        {
            int r_idx = 0 * m_windowHeight * m_windowWidth + h * m_windowWidth + w;
            int g_idx = 1 * m_windowHeight * m_windowWidth + h * m_windowWidth + w;
            int b_idx = 2 * m_windowHeight * m_windowWidth + h * m_windowWidth + w;

            int out_idx = (h * m_windowWidth + w) * 4;

            outputImageBuffer[out_idx + 0] = static_cast<uint8_t>(std::clamp(outputData[r_idx], 0.0f, 1.0f) * 255.0f);
            outputImageBuffer[out_idx + 1] = static_cast<uint8_t>(std::clamp(outputData[g_idx], 0.0f, 1.0f) * 255.0f);
            outputImageBuffer[out_idx + 2] = static_cast<uint8_t>(std::clamp(outputData[b_idx], 0.0f, 1.0f) * 255.0f);
            outputImageBuffer[out_idx + 3] = 255;
        }
    }

    GFX::VkBufferObject stagingBuffer(m_context, m_windowWidth * m_windowHeight * 4 * sizeof(uint8_t),
                                      vk::BufferUsageFlagBits::eTransferSrc | vk::BufferUsageFlagBits::eTransferDst,
                                      VMA_MEMORY_USAGE_CPU_TO_GPU);

    stagingBuffer.CopyData(outputImageBuffer.data());
    OutputImage->TransitionImageLayout(vk::ImageLayout::eTransferDstOptimal);
    OutputImage->CopyBufferToImage(stagingBuffer);
    OutputImage->TransitionImageLayout(vk::ImageLayout::eGeneral);
    m_postprocessingTimer.stop();
}

void LSTMDenoiser::DrawDenoiserUI()
{
    ImGui::Text("Loaded model: %s", m_appConfig.Denoiser.LSTM.nnModelPath.c_str());
    ImGui::Text("Preprocessing time: %f ms", m_preprocessingTimer.elapsedMilliseconds());
    ImGui::Text("Inference time: %f ms", m_inferenceTimer.elapsedMilliseconds());
    ImGui::Text("Postprocessing time: %f ms", m_postprocessingTimer.elapsedMilliseconds());
}
#endif
```

#ifdef TRAINED_DENOISER_ON
#pragma once

/**

class LSTMDenoiser : public IDenoiser
{
  public:

  protected:

    Ort::Env                         m_env;

    Ort::MemoryInfo                  m_memoryInfo;

    std::optional<Ort::Value> m_noisyImageTensor;
    std::optional<Ort::Value> m_noiseImageTensor;

    const char *inputNames[3] = {"noisy", "motion_vector", "noise"};
    const char *outputNames   = "output";

    // buffers for history images

    std::vector<std::unique_ptr<GFX::VkImageObject>> m_historyNoiseImages;

    uint32_t                                         m_windowWidth;
    uint32_t                                         m_windowHeight;

    GFX::VkContext &m_context;
    AppConfig      &m_config;
    Noise          &m_noise;

    Exporter m_exporter{m_context};

    Timer m_inferenceTimer;
    Timer m_preprocessingTimer;
    Timer m_postprocessingTimer;
};
#endif
