### renderer/denoisers/lstm_denoiser_gpu.cu
```
#ifdef TRAINED_DENOISER_ON
#include "lstm_denoiser_gpu.h"

#include "../vk_cuda_interop.h"

//----------------------------------------------------------------------
// Kernels
//----------------------------------------------------------------------
__global__ void LSTMPreprocessKernel(cudaTextureObject_t colorTextures[4], cudaTextureObject_t noiseTextures[4],
                                     cudaTextureObject_t motionVectorTextures[4], float *processedColorInput,
                                     float *processedMotionVectorInput, float *processedNoiseInput, int width,
                                     int height, float noiseMin, float noiseMax)
{
    int x           = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
    int y           = blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
    int totalPixels = width * height;
    int pixelIndex  = y * width + x;

    if (x >= width || y >= height)
        return;

    for (int seq = 0; seq < 4; ++seq)
    {
        // --- Color ---
        float4 rgba  = tex2D<float4>(colorTextures[seq], x, y);
        float  alpha = rgba.w;

        for (int c = 0; c < 3; ++c)
        {
            size_t nchwIndex               = ((seq * 3 + c) * totalPixels) + pixelIndex;
            processedColorInput[nchwIndex] = (&rgba.x)[c] * alpha;
        }

        // --- Noise ---
        float2 noiseRG = tex2D<float2>(noiseTextures[seq], x, y);
        for (int c = 0; c < 2; ++c)
        {
            size_t nchwIndex = ((seq * 2 + c) * totalPixels) + pixelIndex;
            processedNoiseInput[nchwIndex] = (&noiseRG.x)[c];
        }

        // --- Motion Vectors ---
        float4 motionRGBA = tex2D<float4>(motionVectorTextures[seq], x, y);
        for (int c = 0; c < 3; ++c)
        {
            size_t nchwIndex                      = ((seq * 3 + c) * totalPixels) + pixelIndex;
            processedMotionVectorInput[nchwIndex] = (&motionRGBA.x)[c];
        }
    }
}

__device__ inline uchar4 packColor(float r, float g, float b, float a)
{
    return make_uchar4(static_cast<unsigned char>(r * 255.0f), static_cast<unsigned char>(g * 255.0f),
                       static_cast<unsigned char>(b * 255.0f), static_cast<unsigned char>(a * 255.0f));
}

__device__ __forceinline__ float clamp(float v, float minVal, float maxVal)
{
    return fminf(fmaxf(v, minVal), maxVal);
}   

__global__ void LSTMPostProcessKernel(float *outputData, cudaSurfaceObject_t outputSurface, int width, int height)
{
    // convert NCHW to RGBA format
    int idx         = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
    int idy         = blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
    int totalPixels = width * height;
    int pixelIndex  = idy * width + idx;

    if (idx >= width || idy >= height)
        return;

    float r = clamp(outputData[0 * totalPixels + pixelIndex], 0, 1);
    float g = clamp(outputData[1 * totalPixels + pixelIndex], 0 ,1);
    float b = clamp(outputData[2 * totalPixels + pixelIndex], 0 ,1);

    // Convert to uchar4 (0–255)
    uchar4 outColor = packColor(r,g,b,1);

    // Write to surface
    surf2Dwrite(outColor, outputSurface, idx * sizeof(uchar4), idy);
}

//----------------------------------------------------------------------
// Class LSTMDenoiserGPU
//----------------------------------------------------------------------
LSTMDenoiserGPU::LSTMDenoiserGPU(GFX::VkContext &context, AppConfig &config, Noise &noise,
                                 GFX::VkImageObject &motionVectorImage) :
    LSTMDenoiser(context, config, noise, motionVectorImage)
{}

LSTMDenoiserGPU::~LSTMDenoiserGPU()
{
    cudaFree(m_processedColorInput);
    cudaFree(m_processedMotionVectorInput);
    cudaFree(m_processedNoiseInput);
    cudaFree(m_output);

    for (int i = 0; i < 4; ++i)
    {
        cudaDestroyTextureObject(m_colorTextureObject[i]);
        cudaDestroyTextureObject(m_motionVectorTextureObject[i]);
        cudaDestroyTextureObject(m_noiseTextureObject[i]);

        cudaDestroyExternalMemory(m_colorExtMemory[i]);
        cudaDestroyExternalMemory(m_motionVectorExtMemory[i]);
        cudaDestroyExternalMemory(m_noiseExtMemory[i]);
    }

    cudaDestroySurfaceObject(m_outputSurfaceObject);
    cudaDestroyExternalMemory(m_outputExtMemory);
}

void LSTMDenoiserGPU::InitPass(const GFX::VkImageObject &inputImage)
{
    LSTMDenoiser::InitPass(inputImage);
    cudaMalloc(&m_processedColorInput, m_inputSize * sizeof(float));
    cudaMalloc(&m_processedMotionVectorInput, m_inputSize * sizeof(float));
    cudaMalloc(&m_processedNoiseInput, m_inputSize * sizeof(float));
    cudaMalloc(&m_output, m_outputSize * sizeof(float));

    for (int i = 0; i < 4; ++i)
    {
        if (i != 3)
        {
            VkCudaInterop::ImportVulkanImage(*m_historyColorImages[i], m_colorExtMemory[i], &m_colorTextureObject[i],
                                             nullptr, cudaCreateChannelDesc<uchar4>(), cudaReadModeNormalizedFloat);
            VkCudaInterop::ImportVulkanImage(*m_historyMotionVectorImages[i], m_motionVectorExtMemory[i],
                                             &m_motionVectorTextureObject[i], nullptr, cudaCreateChannelDesc<uchar4>(),
                                             cudaReadModeNormalizedFloat);
            VkCudaInterop::ImportVulkanImage(*m_historyNoiseImages[i], m_noiseExtMemory[i], &m_noiseTextureObject[i],
                                             nullptr, cudaCreateChannelDesc(32, 32, 0, 0, cudaChannelFormatKindFloat),
                                             cudaReadModeElementType);
        }
        else
        {
            VkCudaInterop::ImportVulkanImage(inputImage, m_colorExtMemory[i], &m_colorTextureObject[i], nullptr,
                                             cudaCreateChannelDesc<uchar4>(), cudaReadModeNormalizedFloat);
            VkCudaInterop::ImportVulkanImage(m_motionVectorImage, m_motionVectorExtMemory[i],
                                             &m_motionVectorTextureObject[i], nullptr, cudaCreateChannelDesc<uchar4>(),
                                             cudaReadModeNormalizedFloat);
            VkCudaInterop::ImportVulkanImage(*m_noise.NoiseTexture, m_noiseExtMemory[i], &m_noiseTextureObject[i],
                                             nullptr, cudaCreateChannelDesc(32, 32, 0, 0, cudaChannelFormatKindFloat),
                                             cudaReadModeElementType);
        }
    }

    VkCudaInterop::ImportVulkanImage(*OutputImage, m_outputExtMemory, nullptr, &m_outputSurfaceObject,
                                     cudaCreateChannelDesc<uchar4>(), cudaReadModeNormalizedFloat);
}

void LSTMDenoiserGPU::InferencePreprocess()
{
    m_preprocessingTimer.start();
    const int height = m_windowHeight;
    const int width  = m_windowWidth;

    dim3 blockSize(16, 16);
    dim3 gridSize((width + blockSize.x - 1) / blockSize.x, (height + blockSize.y - 1) / blockSize.y);

    auto [noiseMin, noiseMax] = m_noise.GetMinMaxStepSize();
    LSTMPreprocessKernel<<<gridSize, blockSize>>>(
        m_colorTextureObject.data(), m_noiseTextureObject.data(), m_motionVectorTextureObject.data(),
        m_processedColorInput, m_processedMotionVectorInput, m_processedNoiseInput, width, height, noiseMin, noiseMax);

    auto err = cudaDeviceSynchronize();
    if (err != cudaSuccess)
    {
        throw std::runtime_error("CUDA error during LSTM preprocessing: " + std::string(cudaGetErrorString(err)));
    }

    Ort::MemoryInfo memInfo("Cuda", OrtDeviceAllocator, 0, OrtMemTypeDefault);
    m_noisyImageTensor.emplace(Ort::Value::CreateTensor<float>(memInfo, m_processedColorInput, m_inputSize,
                                                               m_inputShape.data(), m_inputShape.size()));
    m_motionVectorImageTensor.emplace(Ort::Value::CreateTensor<float>(
        memInfo, m_processedMotionVectorInput, m_inputSize, m_inputShape.data(), m_inputShape.size()));
    m_noiseImageTensor.emplace(Ort::Value::CreateTensor<float>(memInfo, m_processedNoiseInput, m_inputSize,
                                                               m_inputShape.data(), m_inputShape.size()));
    m_outputImageTensor.emplace(
        Ort::Value::CreateTensor<float>(memInfo, m_output, m_outputSize, m_outputShape.data(), m_outputShape.size()));
    m_preprocessingTimer.stop();
}

void LSTMDenoiserGPU::InferencePostprocess(float *outputData, std::vector<int64_t> &outputShape)
{
    m_postprocessingTimer.start();
    dim3 blockSize(16, 16);
    dim3 gridSize((m_windowWidth + blockSize.x - 1) / blockSize.x, (m_windowHeight + blockSize.y - 1) / blockSize.y);

    LSTMPostProcessKernel<<<gridSize, blockSize>>>(outputData, m_outputSurfaceObject, m_windowWidth, m_windowHeight);

    auto err = cudaDeviceSynchronize();
    if (err != cudaSuccess)
    {
        throw std::runtime_error("CUDA error during LSTM postprocessing: " + std::string(cudaGetErrorString(err)));
    }
    m_postprocessingTimer.stop();
}
#endif
```

```
#ifdef TRAINED_DENOISER_ON

#include "lstm_denoiser.h"

/**
 * @brief LSTM denoiser class processing with GPU acceleration using CUDA.

class LSTMDenoiserGPU : public LSTMDenoiser
{

    LSTMDenoiserGPU(GFX::VkContext &context, AppConfig &config, Noise &noise, GFX::VkImageObject &motionVectorImage);
```

### renderer/denoisers/mean_filter.cpp
```
#include "mean_filter.h"
#include "../utils/container.h"
#include "../utils/utils.h"
#include "imgui.h"

MeanFilter::MeanFilter(GFX::VkContext &context, AppConfig &appconfig, GFX::VkImageObject &noiseTexture) :
    IDenoiser(context, appconfig), m_noiseTexture(noiseTexture)
{
    m_meanFilterUbos.kernelSize   = appconfig.Denoiser.ScreenSpaced.MeanFilter.kernelSize;
    m_meanFilterUbos.weightedMean = m_isWeightedMean;
}

void MeanFilter::InitPass(const GFX::VkImageObject &inputImage)
{
    IDenoiser::InitPass(inputImage);

    Utils::CreateAndCopyBuffer(m_context, &m_meanFilterUbos, sizeof(MeanFilterUbos),
                               vk::BufferUsageFlagBits::eUniformBuffer | vk::BufferUsageFlagBits::eTransferDst,
                               VMA_MEMORY_USAGE_GPU_ONLY, m_meanFilterUboDeviceBuffer);
    m_meanFilterUboDeviceBuffer->SetName("Mean Filter UBO");
    GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::ElementUniformBuffer meanFilterDescriptorElement{*m_meanFilterUboDeviceBuffer};
    GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::ElementStorageImage  noiseDescriptorImage{m_noiseTexture};
    InitPassScreenspace(inputImage, m_appConfig.Denoiser.ScreenSpaced.MeanFilter.shaderPath,
                        {&meanFilterDescriptorElement, &noiseDescriptorImage});
}

void MeanFilter::DrawDenoiserUI()
{
    if (ImGui::InputInt("Kernel Size", &m_meanFilterUbos.kernelSize, 2) ||
        ImGui::Checkbox("Weighted Mean", &m_isWeightedMean))
    {
        m_meanFilterUbos.kernelSize   = std::clamp(m_meanFilterUbos.kernelSize, 0, 10);
        m_meanFilterUbos.weightedMean = m_isWeightedMean;
        g_DirtyBuffers.insert({m_meanFilterUboDeviceBuffer->Buffer, 0, sizeof(MeanFilterUbos), &m_meanFilterUbos});
    }
}
```

### renderer/denoisers/oidn_denoiser.cpp
```
#ifdef OIDN_ON
#include "oidn_denoiser.h"
#include "../utils/exporter.h"
#include "../utils/timer.h"
#include "imgui.h"

OIDN_Denoiser::OIDN_Denoiser(GFX::VkContext &context, AppConfig &config) :
    IDenoiser(context, config), m_exporter(context)
{
    m_width  = context.Config().WindowWidth;
    m_height = context.Config().WindowHeight;
    InitOIDN();
}

void OIDN_Denoiser::InitOIDN()
{
#ifdef __APPLE__
    m_device = oidn::newDevice(oidn::DeviceType::Metal);
#else
    m_device = oidn::newDevice(oidn::DeviceType::CUDA);
#endif

    if (m_device == 0)
    {
        std::cout << "Fall back to CPU, running on CPU would have huge performance issue!" << std::endl;
        m_device = oidn::newDevice(oidn::DeviceType::CPU);
    }

    m_device.commit();
}

void OIDN_Denoiser::SetupBuffers()
{
    m_inputBuffer  = m_device.newBuffer(m_width * m_height * 3 * sizeof(float));
    m_outputBuffer = m_device.newBuffer(m_width * m_height * 3 * sizeof(float));
}

/**
 * @brief: no pass initialized, only caching buffers for transfering
 */
void OIDN_Denoiser::InitPass(const GFX::VkImageObject &inputImage)
{
    CacheInputImage(inputImage);
    InitOutputImage();

    size_t numPixels  = inputImage.Extent.width * inputImage.Extent.height;
    size_t bufferSize = numPixels * 4;
    m_stagingBuffer.emplace(m_context, bufferSize,
                            vk::BufferUsageFlagBits::eTransferSrc | vk::BufferUsageFlagBits::eTransferDst,
                            VMA_MEMORY_USAGE_GPU_TO_CPU);

    SetupBuffers();

    m_filter = m_device.newFilter("RT");
    m_filter.set("hdr", false);
    m_filter.setImage("color", m_inputBuffer, oidn::Format::Float3, m_width, m_height);
    m_filter.setImage("output", m_outputBuffer, oidn::Format::Float3, m_width, m_height);
}

/**
 * @brief: no compute/rendering pass, only perform oidn execute
 */
void OIDN_Denoiser::RecordPass(GFX::VkCommandBufferObject &commandBuffer, uint32_t currentFrame)
{
    BarrierInputImage(commandBuffer);
    auto imageData = m_exporter.ExportImageToBuffer(*m_inputImage, *m_stagingBuffer, false);

    DenoiseImage(imageData);

    m_stagingBuffer->CopyData(imageData.data());
    OutputImage->TransitionImageLayout(vk::ImageLayout::eTransferDstOptimal);
    OutputImage->CopyBufferToImage(*m_stagingBuffer);
    OutputImage->TransitionImageLayout(vk::ImageLayout::eGeneral);
}

void OIDN_Denoiser::DenoiseImage(std::vector<uint8_t> &image)
{
    m_preprocessTimer.start();
    auto   pixelCount = m_width * m_height;
    float *inputPtr   = static_cast<float *>(m_inputBuffer.getData());
    float *outputPtr  = static_cast<float *>(m_outputBuffer.getData());

#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
    for (int i = 0; i < pixelCount; ++i)
    {
        float alpha         = (float) image[i * 4 + 3] / 255;
        inputPtr[i * 3 + 0] = image[i * 4 + 0] * alpha / 255.0f;
        inputPtr[i * 3 + 1] = image[i * 4 + 1] * alpha / 255.0f;
        inputPtr[i * 3 + 2] = image[i * 4 + 2] * alpha / 255.0f;
    }
    m_preprocessTimer.stop();

    OIDNExecute();

    m_postprocessTimer.start();
#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
    for (int i = 0; i < pixelCount; ++i)
    {
        image[i * 4 + 0] = static_cast<uint8_t>(std::clamp(outputPtr[i * 3 + 0], 0.0f, 1.0f) * 255.0f);
        image[i * 4 + 1] = static_cast<uint8_t>(std::clamp(outputPtr[i * 3 + 1], 0.0f, 1.0f) * 255.0f);
        image[i * 4 + 2] = static_cast<uint8_t>(std::clamp(outputPtr[i * 3 + 2], 0.0f, 1.0f) * 255.0f);
        image[i * 4 + 3] = 255;
    }
    m_postprocessTimer.stop();
}

void OIDN_Denoiser::OIDNExecute()
{
    m_inferenceTimer.start();
    m_filter.commit();
    m_filter.execute();

    const char *errorMessage;
    if (m_device.getError(errorMessage) != oidn::Error::None)
    {
        std::cout << "Error: " << errorMessage << std::endl;
        return;
    }
    oidnSyncDevice(m_device.getHandle());

    m_inferenceTimer.stop();
}

void OIDN_Denoiser::DrawDenoiserUI()
{
    ImGui::Text("Preprocess time: %f ms", m_preprocessTimer.elapsedMilliseconds());
    ImGui::Text("Inference time: %f ms", m_inferenceTimer.elapsedMilliseconds());
    ImGui::Text("Postprocess time: %f ms", m_postprocessTimer.elapsedMilliseconds());
}
#endif
```

### renderer/denoisers/oidn_denoiser_gpu.cpp
```
#ifdef OIDN_ON
#include "oidn_denoiser_gpu.h"
#include "../utils/utils.h"

OIDN_DenoiserGPU::OIDN_DenoiserGPU(GFX::VkContext &context, AppConfig &config) : OIDN_Denoiser(context, config)
{}

void OIDN_DenoiserGPU::SetupBuffers()
{
    m_stagingData.resize(m_width * m_height);
    Utils::CreateAndCopyBuffer(m_context, m_stagingData.data(), m_width * m_height * sizeof(oidnPixel),
                               vk::BufferUsageFlagBits::eStorageBuffer | vk::BufferUsageFlagBits::eTransferDst,
                               VMA_MEMORY_USAGE_GPU_ONLY, m_vkInputBuffer, true);

    Utils::CreateAndCopyBuffer(m_context, m_stagingData.data(), m_width * m_height * sizeof(oidnPixel),
                               vk::BufferUsageFlagBits::eStorageBuffer | vk::BufferUsageFlagBits::eTransferDst,
                               VMA_MEMORY_USAGE_GPU_ONLY, m_vkOutputBuffer, true);

    OIDNDevice device = m_device.getHandle();
#if defined(__linux__)
    m_inputBuffer  = oidnNewSharedBufferFromFD(device, OIDN_EXTERNAL_MEMORY_TYPE_FLAG_OPAQUE_FD, m_vkInputBuffer->Fd,
                                               m_vkInputBuffer->Size);
    m_outputBuffer = oidnNewSharedBufferFromFD(device, OIDN_EXTERNAL_MEMORY_TYPE_FLAG_OPAQUE_FD, m_vkOutputBuffer->Fd,
                                               m_vkOutputBuffer->Size);
#elif defined(_WIN32) || defined(_WIN64)
    m_inputBuffer =
        oidnNewSharedBufferFromWin32Handle(device, OIDN_EXTERNAL_MEMORY_TYPE_FLAG_OPAQUE_WIN32,
                                           m_vkInputBuffer->WinHandle, "inputBuffer", m_vkInputBuffer->Size);
    m_outputBuffer =
        oidnNewSharedBufferFromWin32Handle(device, OIDN_EXTERNAL_MEMORY_TYPE_FLAG_OPAQUE_WIN32,
                                           m_vkOutputBuffer->WinHandle, "outputBuffer", m_vkOutputBuffer->Size);
#endif

    GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::ElementStorageImage  inputImageElement{*m_inputImage};
    GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::ElementStorageImage  outputImageElement{*OutputImage};
    GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::ElementStorageBuffer inputBufferElement{*m_vkInputBuffer};
    GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::ElementStorageBuffer outputBufferElement{*m_vkOutputBuffer};

    // texture to buffer
    m_descriptorSetTextureToBuffer.emplace(m_context, std::vector<GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::IDescriptorElement *>{
                                                          &inputImageElement, &inputBufferElement});

    auto shaderCode = Utils::ReadFile(m_appConfig.Denoiser.OIDN.preprocessShaderPath);
    m_passTextureToBuffer.emplace(
        m_context, shaderCode,
        std::vector<vk::DescriptorSetLayout>{m_descriptorSetTextureToBuffer->DescriptorSetLayout});

    // buffer to texture
    m_descriptorSetBufferToTexture.emplace(m_context, std::vector<GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::IDescriptorElement *>{
                                                          &outputBufferElement, &outputImageElement});
    shaderCode = Utils::ReadFile(m_appConfig.Denoiser.OIDN.postprocessShaderPath);
    m_passBufferToTexture.emplace(
        m_context, shaderCode,
        std::vector<vk::DescriptorSetLayout>{m_descriptorSetBufferToTexture->DescriptorSetLayout});
}

void OIDN_DenoiserGPU::RecordPass(GFX::VkCommandBufferObject &commandBuffer, uint32_t currentFrame)
{
    // convert input image to buffer
    commandBuffer.StartComputePass(*m_passTextureToBuffer);
    commandBuffer.BindDescriptorSet(*m_passTextureToBuffer,
                                    {m_descriptorSetTextureToBuffer->DescriptorSets[currentFrame]});
    commandBuffer.Dispatch((m_width + 15) / 16, (m_height + 15) / 16, 1);
    commandBuffer.BarrierImage(OutputImage->Image, vk::ImageLayout::eGeneral, vk::ImageLayout::eGeneral,
                               vk::AccessFlagBits::eShaderRead, vk::AccessFlagBits::eShaderWrite,
                               vk::PipelineStageFlagBits::eComputeShader, vk::PipelineStageFlagBits::eComputeShader);
    // run filter
    OIDNExecute();

    // convert buffer to output image
    commandBuffer.StartComputePass(*m_passBufferToTexture);
    commandBuffer.BindDescriptorSet(*m_passBufferToTexture,
                                    {m_descriptorSetBufferToTexture->DescriptorSets[currentFrame]});
    commandBuffer.Dispatch((m_width + 15) / 16, (m_height + 15) / 16, 1);
}
#endif
```

### renderer/denoisers/oidn_denoiser_gpu.h
```
#ifdef OIDN_ON
#pragma once

#include "oidn_denoiser.h"

/**
 * @brief Intel Open Image Denoise (OIDN) denoisers processing with GPU
 */
class OIDN_DenoiserGPU : public OIDN_Denoiser
{
  public:
    OIDN_DenoiserGPU(GFX::VkContext &context, AppConfig &config);
    void RecordPass(GFX::VkCommandBufferObject &commandBuffer, uint32_t currentFrame) override;

  private:
    void SetupBuffers() override;

  private:
    struct oidnPixel
    {
        float r, g, b;
    };
    std::vector<oidnPixel> m_stagingData;

    std::optional<GFX::VkBufferObject> m_vkInputBuffer;
    std::optional<GFX::VkBufferObject> m_vkOutputBuffer;

    std::optional<GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject> m_descriptorSetTextureToBuffer;
    std::optional<GFX::VkPassObject>          m_passTextureToBuffer;
    std::optional<GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject> m_descriptorSetBufferToTexture;
    std::optional<GFX::VkPassObject>          m_passBufferToTexture;
};
#endif
```

### renderer/denoisers/temporal_rls.cpp
```
#include "temporal_rls.h"

#include "imgui.h"

#include "../utils/container.h"
#include "../utils/utils.h"

RLSDenoiser::RLSDenoiser(GFX::VkContext &context, AppConfig &appconfig, GFX::VkImageObject &positionDepthBufferImage,
                         Noise &noise, Camera &cam) :
    IDenoiser(context, appconfig),
    m_positionDepthBufferImage(positionDepthBufferImage),
    m_noise(noise),
    m_camera(cam),
    m_extent(m_positionDepthBufferImage.Extent.width, m_positionDepthBufferImage.Extent.height)
{
    m_rlsPixels.resize(m_extent.width * m_extent.height);
    for (auto &pixel : m_rlsPixels)
    {
        pixel.beta       = glm::mat4x2(1, 1, 1, 1, 1, 1, 1, 1);
        pixel.prediction = glm::mat2(2);
    }

    rlsUbos.noiseMin     = appconfig.NoiseConfig.UniformNoise.min;
    rlsUbos.noiseMax     = appconfig.NoiseConfig.UniformNoise.max;
    rlsUbos.lambdaStatic = appconfig.Denoiser.TemporalRLS.lambdaStatic;
    rlsUbos.lambdaMoving = appconfig.Denoiser.TemporalRLS.lambdaMoving;

    /**
     * export noise and input for final visualization for paper
     * impact on performance, should always be false
     */
    rlsUbos.visualizationExport      = m_appConfig.InternalConfig.visualizeRLS;
    rlsUbos.visualizationExportPixel = glm::vec2(m_extent.width / 2, m_extent.height / 2);
}

void RLSDenoiser::InitPass(const GFX::VkImageObject &inputImage)
{
    IDenoiser::InitPass(inputImage);

    m_intermediateOutputImage.emplace(m_context, m_extent.width, m_extent.height, 1, vk::Format::eR8G8B8A8Unorm);
    m_intermediateOutputImage->TransitionImageLayout(vk::ImageLayout::eGeneral);

    m_medianFilterUbos.kernelSize = 4;
    m_meanFilterUbos.kernelSize   = 4;
    m_meanFilterUbos.weightedMean = 1;
    if (m_appConfig.Denoiser.TemporalRLS.filterType == "mean")
    {
        Utils::CreateAndCopyBuffer(m_context, &m_meanFilterUbos, sizeof(MeanFilterUbos),
                                   vk::BufferUsageFlagBits::eUniformBuffer | vk::BufferUsageFlagBits::eTransferDst,
                                   VMA_MEMORY_USAGE_GPU_ONLY, m_denoiserFilterUboDeviceBuffer);
    }
    else if (m_appConfig.Denoiser.TemporalRLS.filterType == "median")
    {
        Utils::CreateAndCopyBuffer(m_context, &m_medianFilterUbos, sizeof(MeanFilterUbos),
                                   vk::BufferUsageFlagBits::eUniformBuffer | vk::BufferUsageFlagBits::eTransferDst,
                                   VMA_MEMORY_USAGE_GPU_ONLY, m_denoiserFilterUboDeviceBuffer);
    }

    GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::ElementStorageImage  inputDescriptorImage{inputImage};
    GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::ElementStorageImage  outputDescriptorImage{*OutputImage};
    GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::ElementStorageImage  intermediateOutputDescriptorImage{*m_intermediateOutputImage};
    GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::ElementUniformBuffer cameraDescriptorElement{*m_camera.CameraDeviceBuffer};
    GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::ElementStorageBuffer previousCamDescriptorElement{*m_camera.PreviousCameraDeviceBuffer};

    // create RLS and reprojected RLS buffer for each pixel
    Utils::CreateAndCopyBuffer(m_context, m_rlsPixels.data(), m_rlsPixels.size() * sizeof(RLSPixel),
                               vk::BufferUsageFlagBits::eStorageBuffer | vk::BufferUsageFlagBits::eTransferDst,
                               VMA_MEMORY_USAGE_GPU_ONLY, m_rlsPixelsBuffer);

    Utils::CreateAndCopyBuffer(m_context, m_rlsPixels.data(), m_rlsPixels.size() * sizeof(RLSPixel),
                               vk::BufferUsageFlagBits::eStorageBuffer | vk::BufferUsageFlagBits::eTransferDst,
                               VMA_MEMORY_USAGE_GPU_ONLY, m_reprojectedRlsPixelsBuffer);

    Utils::CreateAndCopyBuffer(m_context, &rlsUbos, sizeof(RLSUbos),
                               vk::BufferUsageFlagBits::eUniformBuffer | vk::BufferUsageFlagBits::eTransferDst,
                               VMA_MEMORY_USAGE_GPU_ONLY, m_rlsUbosBuffer);

    m_previousPositionDepthBufferImage.emplace(m_context, m_extent.width, m_extent.height, 1,
                                               vk::Format::eR32G32B32A32Sfloat);
    m_previousPositionDepthBufferImage->TransitionImageLayout(vk::ImageLayout::eGeneral);

    GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::ElementUniformBuffer rlsUbosElement{*m_rlsUbosBuffer};
    GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::ElementStorageBuffer rlsPixelsElement{*m_rlsPixelsBuffer};
    GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::ElementStorageBuffer reprojectedRlsPixelsElement{*m_reprojectedRlsPixelsBuffer};

    GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::ElementStorageImage noiseTextureElement{*m_noise.NoiseTexture};

    GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::ElementStorageImage positionDepthDescriptorImage{m_positionDepthBufferImage};
    GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::ElementStorageImage previousPositionDepthDescriptorImage{
        *m_previousPositionDepthBufferImage};

    GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::ElementUniformBuffer              filterUboDescriptor{*m_denoiserFilterUboDeviceBuffer};
    GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::ElementStorageImage              *finalOutputDescriptorImage = &outputDescriptorImage;
    GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::ElementStorageImage              *rlsInputDescriptorImage    = &inputDescriptorImage;
    std::vector<GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::IDescriptorElement *> filterElements;
    if (m_appConfig.Denoiser.TemporalRLS.filterLocation == "after")
    {
        finalOutputDescriptorImage = &intermediateOutputDescriptorImage;
        filterElements             = {
            &intermediateOutputDescriptorImage,
            &outputDescriptorImage,
            &filterUboDescriptor,
        };
        if (m_appConfig.Denoiser.TemporalRLS.filterType == "mean")
        {
            filterElements.push_back(&noiseTextureElement);
        }
    }
    else if (m_appConfig.Denoiser.TemporalRLS.filterLocation == "before")
    {
        rlsInputDescriptorImage = &intermediateOutputDescriptorImage;
        filterElements          = {
            &inputDescriptorImage,
            &intermediateOutputDescriptorImage,
            &filterUboDescriptor,
        };
        if (m_appConfig.Denoiser.TemporalRLS.filterType == "mean")
        {
            filterElements.push_back(&noiseTextureElement);
        }
    }
    else
    {
        // no filter
        finalOutputDescriptorImage = &outputDescriptorImage;
        rlsInputDescriptorImage    = &inputDescriptorImage;
        filterElements             = {};
    }

    CreatePasses(
        {
            &cameraDescriptorElement,
            &rlsPixelsElement,
            &reprojectedRlsPixelsElement,
            &positionDepthDescriptorImage,
            &previousPositionDepthDescriptorImage,
            &previousCamDescriptorElement,
        },
        {
            &rlsUbosElement,
            &rlsPixelsElement,
            &reprojectedRlsPixelsElement,
            rlsInputDescriptorImage,
            finalOutputDescriptorImage,
            &noiseTextureElement,
            &positionDepthDescriptorImage,
            &previousPositionDepthDescriptorImage,
            &cameraDescriptorElement,
            &previousCamDescriptorElement,
        },
        filterElements);
}

void RLSDenoiser::CreatePasses(
    const std::vector<GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::IDescriptorElement *> &reprojectionElements,
    const std::vector<GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::IDescriptorElement *> &rlsElements,
    const std::vector<GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::IDescriptorElement *> &filterElements)
{
    // reprojection pass
    m_reprojectionDescriptorSet.emplace(m_context, reprojectionElements);
    auto reprojectionShaderCode = Utils::ReadFile(m_appConfig.Denoiser.TemporalRLS.reprojectionShaderPath);
    m_reprojectionPass.emplace(m_context, reprojectionShaderCode,
                               std::vector<vk::DescriptorSetLayout>{m_reprojectionDescriptorSet->DescriptorSetLayout});

    // rls pass
    m_rlsDescriptorSet.emplace(m_context, rlsElements);
    auto rlsShaderCode = Utils::ReadFile(m_appConfig.Denoiser.TemporalRLS.denoiserShaderPath);
    m_rlsPass.emplace(m_context, rlsShaderCode,
                      std::vector<vk::DescriptorSetLayout>{m_rlsDescriptorSet->DescriptorSetLayout});

    // mean filter pass
    if (!filterElements.empty())
    {
        m_denoiserFilterDescriptorSet.emplace(m_context, filterElements);

        auto shaderPath           = m_appConfig.Denoiser.TemporalRLS.filterType == "mean" ?
                                        m_appConfig.Denoiser.ScreenSpaced.MeanFilter.shaderPath :
                                        m_appConfig.Denoiser.ScreenSpaced.MedianFilter.shaderPath;
        auto meanFilterShaderCode = Utils::ReadFile(shaderPath);
        m_denoiserFilterPass.emplace(
            m_context, meanFilterShaderCode,
            std::vector<vk::DescriptorSetLayout>{m_denoiserFilterDescriptorSet->DescriptorSetLayout});
    }
}

void RLSDenoiser::RecordPass(GFX::VkCommandBufferObject &commandBuffer, uint32_t currentFrame)
{
    if (m_appConfig.Denoiser.TemporalRLS.filterLocation == "before")
    {
        commandBuffer.StartComputePass(*m_denoiserFilterPass);
        commandBuffer.BindDescriptorSet(*m_denoiserFilterPass,
                                        {m_denoiserFilterDescriptorSet->DescriptorSets[currentFrame]});
        commandBuffer.Dispatch((m_inputImage->Extent.width + 15) / 16, (m_inputImage->Extent.height + 15) / 16, 1);
        BarrierGeneralImage(commandBuffer, *m_intermediateOutputImage->Image);
    }

    BarrierInputImage(commandBuffer);
    commandBuffer.StartComputePass(*m_reprojectionPass);
    commandBuffer.BindDescriptorSet(*m_reprojectionPass, {m_reprojectionDescriptorSet->DescriptorSets[currentFrame]});
    commandBuffer.Dispatch((m_inputImage->Extent.width + 15) / 16, (m_inputImage->Extent.height + 15) / 16, 1);

    BarrierReprojectionOutput(commandBuffer);

    commandBuffer.StartComputePass(*m_rlsPass);
    commandBuffer.BindDescriptorSet(*m_rlsPass, {m_rlsDescriptorSet->DescriptorSets[currentFrame]});
    commandBuffer.Dispatch((m_inputImage->Extent.width + 15) / 16, (m_inputImage->Extent.height + 15) / 16, 1);

    if (m_appConfig.Denoiser.TemporalRLS.filterLocation == "after")
    {
        BarrierGeneralImage(commandBuffer, *m_intermediateOutputImage->Image);
        commandBuffer.StartComputePass(*m_denoiserFilterPass);
        commandBuffer.BindDescriptorSet(*m_denoiserFilterPass,
                                        {m_denoiserFilterDescriptorSet->DescriptorSets[currentFrame]});
        commandBuffer.Dispatch((m_inputImage->Extent.width + 15) / 16, (m_inputImage->Extent.height + 15) / 16, 1);
    }
}

void RLSDenoiser::BarrierReprojectionOutput(GFX::VkCommandBufferObject &commandBuffer) const
{
    vk::BufferMemoryBarrier bufferMemoryBarrier;
    vk::ImageMemoryBarrier  barrier{};
    bufferMemoryBarrier.srcAccessMask = vk::AccessFlagBits::eShaderWrite;
    bufferMemoryBarrier.dstAccessMask = vk::AccessFlagBits::eShaderRead;
    bufferMemoryBarrier.buffer        = *m_reprojectedRlsPixelsBuffer->Buffer;
    bufferMemoryBarrier.size          = m_reprojectedRlsPixelsBuffer->Size;
    commandBuffer.GetCommandBuffer().pipelineBarrier(vk::PipelineStageFlagBits::eComputeShader,
                                                     vk::PipelineStageFlagBits::eComputeShader, {}, {},
                                                     {bufferMemoryBarrier}, {});
}
void RLSDenoiser::BarrierGeneralImage(GFX::VkCommandBufferObject &commandBuffer, VkImage image) const
{
    vk::ImageMemoryBarrier barrier{};
    barrier.srcAccessMask                   = vk::AccessFlagBits::eShaderWrite;
    barrier.dstAccessMask                   = vk::AccessFlagBits::eShaderRead;
    barrier.oldLayout                       = vk::ImageLayout::eGeneral;
    barrier.newLayout                       = vk::ImageLayout::eGeneral;
    barrier.image                           = image;
    barrier.subresourceRange.aspectMask     = vk::ImageAspectFlagBits::eColor;
    barrier.subresourceRange.baseMipLevel   = 0;
    barrier.subresourceRange.levelCount     = 1;
    barrier.subresourceRange.baseArrayLayer = 0;
    barrier.subresourceRange.layerCount     = 1;
    commandBuffer.GetCommandBuffer().pipelineBarrier(vk::PipelineStageFlagBits::eComputeShader,
                                                     vk::PipelineStageFlagBits::eComputeShader, {}, {}, {}, {barrier});
}
void RLSDenoiser::DrawDenoiserUI()
{
    if (ImGui::SliderFloat("Lambda Static", &rlsUbos.lambdaStatic, 0.9, 0.998) ||
        ImGui::SliderFloat("Lambda Moving", &rlsUbos.lambdaMoving, 0.1, 0.9))
    {
        g_DirtyBuffers.insert({m_rlsUbosBuffer->Buffer, 0, sizeof(RLSUbos), &rlsUbos});
    }
}
```

### renderer/denoisers/temporal_rls.h
```
#pragma once

#include "../camera.h"
#include "../noise.h"
#include "denoiser.h"

/**
 * @brief Recursive Least Squares (RLS) denoiser
 */
class RLSDenoiser : public IDenoiser
{
  public:
    RLSDenoiser(GFX::VkContext &context, AppConfig &appconfig, GFX::VkImageObject &positionDepthBufferImage,
                Noise &noise, Camera &cam);

    void InitPass(const GFX::VkImageObject &inputImage) override;
    void RecordPass(GFX::VkCommandBufferObject &commandBuffer, uint32_t currentFrame) override;
    void DrawDenoiserUI() override;

  private:
    void CreatePasses(const std::vector<GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::IDescriptorElement *> &reprojectionElements,
                      const std::vector<GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::IDescriptorElement *> &rlsElements,
                      const std::vector<GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::IDescriptorElement *> &medianFilterElements);
    void BarrierReprojectionOutput(GFX::VkCommandBufferObject &commandBuffer) const;
    void BarrierGeneralImage(GFX::VkCommandBufferObject &commandBuffer, VkImage image) const;

  private:
    GFX::VkImageObject &m_positionDepthBufferImage;
    Camera             &m_camera;
    vk::Extent2D        m_extent;
    Noise              &m_noise;

    struct RLSPixel
    {
        glm::mat4x2 beta;        // coefficients
        glm::mat2   prediction;
    };

    struct RLSUbos
    {
        alignas(4) float lambdaStatic;
        alignas(4) float lambdaMoving;
        alignas(4) float noiseMin;
        alignas(4) float noiseMax;
        alignas(4) int visualizationExport;
        alignas(8) glm::vec2 visualizationExportPixel;
    } rlsUbos;
    std::optional<GFX::VkBufferObject> m_rlsUbosBuffer;

    std::vector<RLSPixel>              m_rlsPixels;
    std::optional<GFX::VkBufferObject> m_rlsPixelsBuffer;
    std::optional<GFX::VkBufferObject> m_reprojectedRlsPixelsBuffer;

    std::optional<GFX::VkImageObject> m_previousPositionDepthBufferImage;

    // reprojection pass
    std::optional<GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject> m_reprojectionDescriptorSet;
    std::optional<GFX::VkPassObject>          m_reprojectionPass;

    // rls pass
    std::optional<GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject> m_rlsDescriptorSet;
    std::optional<GFX::VkPassObject>          m_rlsPass;

    // mean filter pass
    struct MeanFilterUbos
    {
        alignas(4) int kernelSize;
        alignas(4) int weightedMean;
    } m_meanFilterUbos;

    struct MedianFilterUbos
    {
        alignas(4) int kernelSize;
    } m_medianFilterUbos;

    std::optional<GFX::VkBufferObject>        m_denoiserFilterUboDeviceBuffer;
    std::optional<GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject> m_denoiserFilterDescriptorSet;
    std::optional<GFX::VkPassObject>          m_denoiserFilterPass;

    std::optional<GFX::VkImageObject> m_intermediateOutputImage;
};
```

### renderer/denoisers/temporal_weighted_mean.cpp
```
#include "temporal_weighted_mean.h"
#include "../utils/container.h"
#include "../utils/utils.h"
#include "imgui.h"

TemporalWMDenoiser::TemporalWMDenoiser(GFX::VkContext &context, AppConfig &appconfig,
                                       GFX::VkImageObject &positionDepthBufferImage, GFX::VkImageObject &noiseImage,
                                       Camera &camera) :
    IDenoiser(context, appconfig),
    m_positionDepthBufferImage(positionDepthBufferImage),
    m_camera{camera},
    m_noiseImage{noiseImage}
{
    m_extent = m_positionDepthBufferImage.Extent;
}

void TemporalWMDenoiser::InitPass(const GFX::VkImageObject &inputImage)
{
    IDenoiser::InitPass(inputImage);

    // initialize history buffers
    m_historyColorSumImage.emplace(m_context, m_extent.width, m_extent.height, 1, vk::Format::eR32G32B32A32Sfloat);
    m_historyColorSumImage->TransitionImageLayout(vk::ImageLayout::eGeneral);
    m_historyColorSumImage->SetName("Current Color sum buffer image");

    m_historyWeightImage.emplace(m_context, m_extent.width, m_extent.height, 1, vk::Format::eR32Sfloat);
    m_historyWeightImage->TransitionImageLayout(vk::ImageLayout::eGeneral);
    m_historyWeightImage->SetName("Current weight image");

    m_previousPositionDepthBufferImage.emplace(m_context, m_context.Config().WindowWidth,
                                               m_context.Config().WindowHeight, 1, vk::Format::eR32G32B32A32Sfloat);
    m_previousPositionDepthBufferImage->TransitionImageLayout(vk::ImageLayout::eGeneral);
    m_previousPositionDepthBufferImage->SetName("Previous position depth buffer image");

    // initialize ubo
    Utils::CreateAndCopyBuffer(m_context, &wmeanUbos, sizeof(WMDenoiserUbos),
                               vk::BufferUsageFlagBits::eStorageBuffer | vk::BufferUsageFlagBits::eUniformBuffer |
                                   vk::BufferUsageFlagBits::eTransferDst,
                               VMA_MEMORY_USAGE_GPU_ONLY, m_wmeanUbosBuffer);
    GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::ElementUniformBuffer uboDescriptorElement{*m_wmeanUbosBuffer};

    // temporal reprojection pass
    m_reprojectionOutputImage.emplace(m_context, m_context.Config().WindowWidth, m_context.Config().WindowHeight, 1,
                                      vk::Format::eR32G32B32A32Sfloat);
    m_reprojectionOutputImage->TransitionImageLayout(vk::ImageLayout::eGeneral);
    m_reprojectionOutputImage->SetName("Reprojection color image");

    m_reprojectionWeightImage.emplace(m_context, m_context.Config().WindowWidth, m_context.Config().WindowHeight, 1,
                                      vk::Format::eR32Sfloat);
    m_reprojectionWeightImage->TransitionImageLayout(vk::ImageLayout::eGeneral);
    m_reprojectionOutputImage->SetName("Reprojection weight image");

    auto reprojectionShader = Utils::ReadFile(m_appConfig.Denoiser.TemporalWeightedMean.reprojectionShaderPath);
    GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::ElementUniformBuffer cameraDescriptorElement{*m_camera.CameraDeviceBuffer};
    GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::ElementStorageBuffer previousCameraDescriptorElement{
        *m_camera.PreviousCameraDeviceBuffer};
    GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::ElementStorageImage inputDescriptorImage{inputImage};

    GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::ElementStorageImage positionDepthDescriptorImage{m_positionDepthBufferImage};
    GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::ElementStorageImage previousPositionDepthDescriptorImage{
        *m_previousPositionDepthBufferImage};

    GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::ElementStorageImage historyColorSumDescriptorImage{*m_historyColorSumImage};
    GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::ElementStorageImage historyWeightSumDescriptorImage{*m_historyWeightImage};

    GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::ElementStorageImage reprojectionOutputDescriptorImage{*m_reprojectionOutputImage};
    GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::ElementStorageImage reprojectionWeightDescriptorImage{*m_reprojectionWeightImage};
    GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::ElementStorageImage noiseImageSampler{m_noiseImage};

    m_reprojectionDescriptorSet.emplace(m_context, std::vector<GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::IDescriptorElement *>{
                                                       &cameraDescriptorElement,
                                                       &inputDescriptorImage,
                                                       &reprojectionOutputDescriptorImage,
                                                       &reprojectionWeightDescriptorImage,
                                                       &previousPositionDepthDescriptorImage,
                                                       &positionDepthDescriptorImage,
                                                       &historyColorSumDescriptorImage,
                                                       &historyWeightSumDescriptorImage,
                                                       &previousCameraDescriptorElement,
                                                   });
    m_reprojectionPass.emplace(m_context, reprojectionShader,
                               std::vector<vk::DescriptorSetLayout>{m_reprojectionDescriptorSet->DescriptorSetLayout});

    // denoiser pass
    auto denoiserShaderCode = Utils::ReadFile(m_appConfig.Denoiser.TemporalWeightedMean.denoiserShaderPath);
    GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::ElementStorageImage outputDescriptorImage{*OutputImage};
    DescriptorSet.emplace(m_context, std::vector<GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::IDescriptorElement *>{
                                         &uboDescriptorElement,
                                         &inputDescriptorImage,
                                         &outputDescriptorImage,
                                         &reprojectionOutputDescriptorImage,
                                         &reprojectionWeightDescriptorImage,
                                         &positionDepthDescriptorImage,
                                         &historyColorSumDescriptorImage,
                                         &historyWeightSumDescriptorImage,
                                         &previousPositionDepthDescriptorImage,
                                         &noiseImageSampler,
                                         &cameraDescriptorElement,
                                         &previousCameraDescriptorElement,
                                     });
    Pass.emplace(m_context, denoiserShaderCode,
                 std::vector<vk::DescriptorSetLayout>{DescriptorSet->DescriptorSetLayout});
}

void TemporalWMDenoiser::RecordPass(GFX::VkCommandBufferObject &commandBuffer, uint32_t currentFrame)
{
    // reprojection pass
    BarrierInputImage(commandBuffer);
    commandBuffer.StartComputePass(*m_reprojectionPass);
    commandBuffer.BindDescriptorSet(*m_reprojectionPass, {m_reprojectionDescriptorSet->DescriptorSets[currentFrame]});
    commandBuffer.Dispatch((m_inputImage->Extent.width + 15) / 16, (m_inputImage->Extent.height + 15) / 16, 1);

    BarrierReprojectionOutput(commandBuffer);

    // output pass
    commandBuffer.StartComputePass(*Pass);
    commandBuffer.BindDescriptorSet(*Pass, {DescriptorSet->DescriptorSets[currentFrame]});
    commandBuffer.Dispatch((m_inputImage->Extent.width + 15) / 16, (m_inputImage->Extent.height + 15) / 16, 1);
}

void TemporalWMDenoiser::BarrierReprojectionOutput(GFX::VkCommandBufferObject &commandBuffer) const
{
    vk::ImageMemoryBarrier barrier{};
    barrier.srcAccessMask                   = vk::AccessFlagBits::eShaderWrite;
    barrier.dstAccessMask                   = vk::AccessFlagBits::eShaderRead;
    barrier.oldLayout                       = vk::ImageLayout::eGeneral;
    barrier.newLayout                       = vk::ImageLayout::eGeneral;
    barrier.image                           = m_reprojectionOutputImage->Image;
    barrier.subresourceRange.aspectMask     = vk::ImageAspectFlagBits::eColor;
    barrier.subresourceRange.baseMipLevel   = 0;
    barrier.subresourceRange.levelCount     = 1;
    barrier.subresourceRange.baseArrayLayer = 0;
    barrier.subresourceRange.layerCount     = 1;
    commandBuffer.GetCommandBuffer().pipelineBarrier(vk::PipelineStageFlagBits::eComputeShader,
                                                     vk::PipelineStageFlagBits::eComputeShader, {}, {}, {}, {barrier});
}

void TemporalWMDenoiser::DrawDenoiserUI()
{
    bool finalMeanFilterBool = (wmeanUbos.finalMeanFilter != 0);
    if (ImGui::Checkbox("Final mean filter", &finalMeanFilterBool))
    {
        wmeanUbos.finalMeanFilter = finalMeanFilterBool ? 1 : 0;
        g_DirtyBuffers.insert({m_wmeanUbosBuffer->Buffer, 0, sizeof(WMDenoiserUbos), &wmeanUbos});
    }
}
```

### renderer/denoisers/temporal_weighted_mean.h
```
#pragma once

#include "../camera.h"
#include "denoiser.h"

/**
 * @brief Temporal weighted mean denoiser, reproject the denoising information into current frame
 */
class TemporalWMDenoiser : public IDenoiser
{
  public:
    TemporalWMDenoiser(GFX::VkContext &context, AppConfig &appconfig, GFX::VkImageObject &positionDepthBufferImage,
                       GFX::VkImageObject &noiseImage, Camera &cam);
    void InitPass(const GFX::VkImageObject &inputImage) override;
    void RecordPass(GFX::VkCommandBufferObject &commandBuffer, uint32_t currentFrame) override;
    void DrawDenoiserUI() override;

  private:
    void BarrierReprojectionOutput(GFX::VkCommandBufferObject &commandBuffer) const;

  private:
    struct WMDenoiserUbos
    {
        int finalMeanFilter = 1;
    } wmeanUbos;
    std::optional<GFX::VkBufferObject> m_wmeanUbosBuffer;

    vk::Extent3D m_extent;

    Camera                           &m_camera;
    GFX::VkImageObject               &m_positionDepthBufferImage;
    GFX::VkImageObject               &m_noiseImage;
    std::optional<GFX::VkImageObject> m_previousPositionDepthBufferImage;
    std::optional<GFX::VkImageObject> m_historyColorSumImage;
    std::optional<GFX::VkImageObject> m_historyWeightImage;

    // reprojection pass
    std::optional<GFX::VkImageObject>         m_reprojectionOutputImage;
    std::optional<GFX::VkImageObject>         m_reprojectionWeightImage;
    std::optional<GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject> m_reprojectionDescriptorSet;
    std::optional<GFX::VkPassObject>          m_reprojectionPass;
};
```

### renderer/denoisers/total_variation.cpp
```
#include "total_variation.h"
#include "../utils/container.h"
#include "../utils/utils.h"
#include "imgui.h"

TotalVariation::TotalVariation(GFX::VkContext &context, AppConfig &appconfig) : IDenoiser(context, appconfig)
{
    m_totalVariationUbos.lambda     = appconfig.Denoiser.ScreenSpaced.TotalVariation.lambda;
    m_totalVariationUbos.iterations = appconfig.Denoiser.ScreenSpaced.TotalVariation.iterations;
}

void TotalVariation::InitPass(const GFX::VkImageObject &inputImage)
{
    CacheInputImage(inputImage);
    InitOutputImage();

    Utils::CreateAndCopyBuffer(m_context, &m_totalVariationUbos, sizeof(TotalVariationUbos),
                               vk::BufferUsageFlagBits::eUniformBuffer | vk::BufferUsageFlagBits::eTransferDst,
                               VMA_MEMORY_USAGE_GPU_ONLY, m_totalVariationUboDeviceBuffer);
    GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::ElementUniformBuffer uboDescriptor{*m_totalVariationUboDeviceBuffer};

    GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::ElementStorageImage inputDescriptorImage{*m_inputImage};
    GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::ElementStorageImage outputDescriptorImage{*OutputImage};

    m_descriptorSetA.emplace(m_context, std::vector<GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::IDescriptorElement *>{
                                            &inputDescriptorImage, &outputDescriptorImage, &uboDescriptor});
    m_descriptorSetB.emplace(m_context, std::vector<GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::IDescriptorElement *>{
                                            &outputDescriptorImage, &inputDescriptorImage, &uboDescriptor});
    auto tvShaderCode = Utils::ReadFile(m_appConfig.Denoiser.ScreenSpaced.TotalVariation.shaderPath);
    m_passA.emplace(m_context, tvShaderCode,
                    std::vector<vk::DescriptorSetLayout>({m_descriptorSetA->DescriptorSetLayout}));

    m_passB.emplace(m_context, tvShaderCode,
                    std::vector<vk::DescriptorSetLayout>({m_descriptorSetB->DescriptorSetLayout}));
}

void TotalVariation::RecordPass(GFX::VkCommandBufferObject &commandBuffer, uint32_t currentFrame)
{
    GFX::VkImageObject *readImage  = m_inputImage;
    GFX::VkImageObject *writeImage = &OutputImage.value();

    if (m_totalVariationUbos.iterations % 2 == 0)
    {
        m_totalVariationUbos.iterations += 1;
    }

    for (int i = 0; i < m_totalVariationUbos.iterations; ++i)
    {
        if (i % 2 == 0)
        {
            // PassA: read from m_inputImage, write to OutputImage
            commandBuffer.StartComputePass(*m_passA);
            commandBuffer.BindDescriptorSet(*m_passA, {m_descriptorSetA->DescriptorSets[currentFrame]});
            readImage  = m_inputImage;
            writeImage = &OutputImage.value();
        }
        else
        {
            // PassB: read from OutputImage, write to m_inputImage
            commandBuffer.StartComputePass(*m_passB);
            commandBuffer.BindDescriptorSet(*m_passB, {m_descriptorSetB->DescriptorSets[currentFrame]});
            readImage  = &OutputImage.value();
            writeImage = m_inputImage;
        }

        commandBuffer.Dispatch((m_inputImage->Extent.width + 15) / 16, (m_inputImage->Extent.height + 15) / 16, 1);

        commandBuffer.BarrierImage(writeImage->Image, vk::ImageLayout::eGeneral, vk::ImageLayout::eGeneral,
                                   vk::AccessFlagBits::eShaderWrite, vk::AccessFlagBits::eShaderRead,
                                   vk::PipelineStageFlagBits::eComputeShader,
                                   vk::PipelineStageFlagBits::eComputeShader);
    }
}

void TotalVariation::DrawDenoiserUI()
{
    if (ImGui::InputFloat("Lambda", &m_totalVariationUbos.lambda) ||
        ImGui::InputInt("Iterations", &m_totalVariationUbos.iterations))
    {
        m_totalVariationUbos.iterations = std::clamp(m_totalVariationUbos.iterations, 0, 100);
        m_totalVariationUbos.lambda     = std::clamp(m_totalVariationUbos.lambda, 0.0f, 1.0f);
        g_DirtyBuffers.insert(
            {m_totalVariationUboDeviceBuffer->Buffer, 0, sizeof(TotalVariationUbos), &m_totalVariationUbos});
    }
}
```

### renderer/denoisers/total_variation.h
```
#pragma once

#include "denoiser.h"

/**
 * @brief Total Variation denoiser
 */
class TotalVariation : public IDenoiser
{
  public:
    TotalVariation(GFX::VkContext &context, AppConfig &appconfig);
    void InitPass(const GFX::VkImageObject &inputImage) override;
    void RecordPass(GFX::VkCommandBufferObject &commandBuffer, uint32_t currentFrame) override;
    void DrawDenoiserUI() override;

  private:
    struct TotalVariationUbos
    {
        float lambda;
        int   iterations;
    } m_totalVariationUbos;

    std::optional<GFX::VkBufferObject> m_totalVariationUboDeviceBuffer;

    // ping pong for iterations
    std::optional<GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject> m_descriptorSetA;
    std::optional<GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject> m_descriptorSetB;
    std::optional<GFX::VkPassObject>          m_passA;
    std::optional<GFX::VkPassObject>          m_passB;
};
```

### renderer/denoisers/unet_denoiser.cpp
```
#ifdef TRAINED_DENOISER_ON
#include "unet_denoiser.h"
#include "../utils/timer.h"
#include "imgui.h"
#include <numeric>

UNetDenoiser::UNetDenoiser(GFX::VkContext &context, AppConfig &appconfig, Noise &noise) :
    IDenoiser(context, appconfig),
    m_memoryInfo(Ort::MemoryInfo::CreateCpu(OrtArenaAllocator, OrtMemTypeDefault)),
    m_noise(noise),
    m_noiseImage(noise.NoiseTexture)
{
    m_width  = context.Config().WindowWidth;
    m_height = context.Config().WindowHeight;

    Ort::ThrowOnError(OrtSessionOptionsAppendExecutionProvider_CUDA(m_sessionOptions, 0));
    m_sessionOptions.SetGraphOptimizationLevel(GraphOptimizationLevel::ORT_ENABLE_ALL);

#if defined(__linux__)
    m_session.emplace(m_env, appconfig.Denoiser.UNet.nnModelPath.c_str(), m_sessionOptions);
#elif defined(_WIN32) || defined(_WIN64)
    std::wstring modelPathW(appconfig.Denoiser.UNet.nnModelPath.begin(), appconfig.Denoiser.nnModelPath.end());
    m_session.emplace(m_env, modelPathW.c_str(), m_sessionOptions);
#endif

    size_t numInputs = m_session->GetInputCount();
    auto   inputName = m_session->GetInputNameAllocated(0, m_allocator);

    Ort::TypeInfo typeInfo   = m_session->GetInputTypeInfo(0);
    auto          tensorInfo = typeInfo.GetTensorTypeAndShapeInfo();
    m_inputShape             = tensorInfo.GetShape();
    if (m_inputShape[2] != m_height || m_inputShape[3] != m_width)
    {
        throw std::runtime_error("Input shape does not match expected dimensions");
    }

    m_inputSize = std::accumulate(m_inputShape.begin(), m_inputShape.end(), int64_t(1),
                                  [](int64_t a, int64_t b) { return a * b; });

    m_inputData.resize(m_inputSize);
    m_noiseInputData.resize(m_inputSize);
}

void UNetDenoiser::InitPass(const GFX::VkImageObject &inputImage)
{
    CacheInputImage(inputImage);
    InitOutputImage();

    size_t numPixels  = inputImage.Extent.width * inputImage.Extent.height;
    size_t bufferSize = numPixels * 4;
    m_stagingBuffer.emplace(m_context, bufferSize,
                            vk::BufferUsageFlagBits::eTransferSrc | vk::BufferUsageFlagBits::eTransferDst,
                            VMA_MEMORY_USAGE_GPU_TO_CPU);
}

void UNetDenoiser::RecordPass(GFX::VkCommandBufferObject &commandBuffer, uint32_t currentFrame)
{
    InferencePreprocess();
    // inference
    m_inferenceTimer.start();

    std::vector<Ort::Value> tensors = {};
    tensors.push_back(std::move(*m_noisyImageTensor));
    tensors.push_back(std::move(*m_noiseImageTensor));

    std::vector<Ort::Value> outputTensors = {};
    outputTensors.push_back(std::move(*m_outputImageTensor));

    m_session->Run(Ort::RunOptions{nullptr}, inputNames, tensors.data(), 2, &outputNames, outputTensors.data(), 1);
    auto                &outputTensor = outputTensors.front();
    float               *outputData   = outputTensor.GetTensorMutableData<float>();
    std::vector<int64_t> outputShape  = outputTensor.GetTensorTypeAndShapeInfo().GetShape();

    if (outputShape[2] != m_height || outputShape[3] != m_width)
    {
        throw std::runtime_error("output shape does not match expected dimensions");
    }

    m_inferenceTimer.stop();

    InferencePostprocess(outputData, outputShape);
}

void UNetDenoiser::InferencePreprocess()
{
    m_preprocessingTimer.start();
    // export image and load as the input shape
    auto inputImageBuffer = m_exporter.ExportImageToBuffer(*m_inputImage, *m_stagingBuffer, false);
    auto noiseImageBuffer = m_exporter.ExportImageToBuffer(*m_noiseImage, false);

    if (inputImageBuffer.size() != m_width * m_height * 4)
    {
        throw std::runtime_error("Input image size does not match expected input shape");
    }

    auto  noiseMinMax = m_noise.GetMinMaxStepSize();
    float noiseMin    = noiseMinMax.first;
    float noiseMax    = noiseMinMax.second;

#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
    for (int idx = 0; idx < m_width * m_height; ++idx)
    {
        int pixelIndex = idx * 4;

        // only alpha multiply for the input image, noise image doesn't have alpha channel
        float alphaInput = inputImageBuffer[pixelIndex + 3] / 255.0f;

        for (int c = 0; c < 3; ++c)
        {
            int   nchwIndex        = c * m_width * m_height + idx;
            float inputColor       = inputImageBuffer[pixelIndex + c] / 255.0f;
            float normalizedNoise  = (noiseImageBuffer[pixelIndex + c] / 255.0f - noiseMin) / (noiseMax - noiseMin);
            m_inputData[nchwIndex] = inputColor * alphaInput;
            m_noiseInputData[nchwIndex] = std::clamp(normalizedNoise, 0.0f, 1.0f);
        }
    }

    m_noisyImageTensor.emplace(Ort::Value::CreateTensor<float>(m_memoryInfo, m_inputData.data(), m_inputSize,
                                                               m_inputShape.data(), m_inputShape.size()));
    m_noiseImageTensor.emplace(Ort::Value::CreateTensor<float>(m_memoryInfo, m_noiseInputData.data(), m_inputSize,
                                                               m_inputShape.data(), m_inputShape.size()));

    m_outputImageTensor.emplace(Ort::Value::CreateTensor<float>(m_memoryInfo, m_inputData.data(), m_inputSize,
                                                                m_inputShape.data(), m_inputShape.size()));
    m_preprocessingTimer.stop();
}

void UNetDenoiser::InferencePostprocess(float *outputData, std::vector<int64_t> &outputShape)
{
    m_postprocessingTimer.start();
    std::vector<uint8_t> inputImageBuffer(m_width * m_height * 4);

#pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static)
    for (int h = 0; h < m_height; ++h)
    {
        for (int w = 0; w < m_width; ++w)
        {
            int r_idx = 0 * m_height * m_width + h * m_width + w;
            int g_idx = 1 * m_height * m_width + h * m_width + w;
            int b_idx = 2 * m_height * m_width + h * m_width + w;

            int out_idx = (h * m_width + w) * 4;

            inputImageBuffer[out_idx + 0] = static_cast<uint8_t>(std::clamp(outputData[r_idx], 0.0f, 1.0f) * 255.0f);
            inputImageBuffer[out_idx + 1] = static_cast<uint8_t>(std::clamp(outputData[g_idx], 0.0f, 1.0f) * 255.0f);
            inputImageBuffer[out_idx + 2] = static_cast<uint8_t>(std::clamp(outputData[b_idx], 0.0f, 1.0f) * 255.0f);
            inputImageBuffer[out_idx + 3] = 255;
        }
    }

    m_stagingBuffer->CopyData(inputImageBuffer.data());
    OutputImage->TransitionImageLayout(vk::ImageLayout::eTransferDstOptimal);
    OutputImage->CopyBufferToImage(*m_stagingBuffer);
    OutputImage->TransitionImageLayout(vk::ImageLayout::eGeneral);
    m_postprocessingTimer.stop();
}

void UNetDenoiser::DrawDenoiserUI()
{
    ImGui::Text("Loaded model: %s", m_appConfig.Denoiser.UNet.nnModelPath.c_str());
    ImGui::Text("Preprocess time: %f ms", m_preprocessingTimer.elapsedMilliseconds());
    ImGui::Text("Inference time: %f ms", m_inferenceTimer.elapsedMilliseconds());
    ImGui::Text("Postprocess time: %f ms", m_postprocessingTimer.elapsedMilliseconds());
}
#endif
```

### renderer/denoisers/unet_denoiser.h
```
#ifdef TRAINED_DENOISER_ON
#pragma once

#include "../noise.h"
#include "../utils/exporter.h"
#include "../utils/timer.h"

#include "denoiser.h"
#include "onnxruntime_cxx_api.h"

/**
 * @brief UNet denoiser class using ONNX Runtime for inference.
 */
class UNetDenoiser : public IDenoiser
{
  public:
    UNetDenoiser(GFX::VkContext &context, AppConfig &appconfig, Noise &noise);
    void InitPass(const GFX::VkImageObject &inputImage) override;
    void RecordPass(GFX::VkCommandBufferObject &commandBuffer, uint32_t currentFrame) override;
    void DrawDenoiserUI() override;

  protected:
    virtual void InferencePreprocess();
    virtual void InferencePostprocess(float *outputData, std::vector<int64_t> &outputShape);

    Ort::Env                         m_env;
    Ort::SessionOptions              m_sessionOptions;
    std::optional<Ort::Session>      m_session;
    Ort::AllocatorWithDefaultOptions m_allocator;
    Ort::MemoryInfo                  m_memoryInfo;

    std::optional<Ort::Value> m_noisyImageTensor;
    std::optional<Ort::Value> m_noiseImageTensor;
    std::optional<Ort::Value> m_outputImageTensor;

    // predefined input shape
    std::vector<int64_t> m_inputShape;
    size_t               m_inputSize;
    std::vector<float>   m_inputData;
    std::vector<float>   m_noiseInputData;

    const char *inputNames[2] = {"noisy_image", "noise_image"};
    const char *outputNames   = {"output"};

    Noise                             &m_noise;
    std::optional<GFX::VkImageObject> &m_noiseImage;
    std::optional<GFX::VkBufferObject> m_stagingBuffer;
    const Exporter                     m_exporter{m_context};

    uint32_t m_width;
    uint32_t m_height;

    Timer m_inferenceTimer;
    Timer m_preprocessingTimer;
    Timer m_postprocessingTimer;
};
#endif
```

### renderer/denoisers/unet_denoiser_gpu.cu
```
#ifdef TRAINED_DENOISER_ON
#include "unet_denoiser_gpu.h"

#include "imgui.h"
#include <cuda_runtime.h>
#include <unistd.h>

#include "../vk_cuda_interop.h"

//----------------------------------------------------------------------
// Kernels
//----------------------------------------------------------------------
__global__ void UnetPreprocessKernel(cudaTextureObject_t inputTex, cudaTextureObject_t noiseTex, float *processedInput,
                                 float *processedNoiseInput, int width, int height, float noiseMin, float noiseMax)
{
    // convert RGBA to NCHW format
    int idx         = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
    int idy         = blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
    int totalPixels = width * height;
    int pixelIndex  = idy * width + idx;

    if (idx >= width || idy >= height)
        return;

    float4 rgba                                  = tex2D<float4>(inputTex, idx, idy);
    float  inputAlpha                            = rgba.w;
    processedInput[0 * totalPixels + pixelIndex] = rgba.x * inputAlpha;
    processedInput[1 * totalPixels + pixelIndex] = rgba.y * inputAlpha;
    processedInput[2 * totalPixels + pixelIndex] = rgba.z * inputAlpha;

    float2 noiseRG                                    = tex2D<float2>(noiseTex, idx, idy);
    processedNoiseInput[0 * totalPixels + pixelIndex] = (noiseRG.x - noiseMin) / (noiseMax - noiseMin);
    processedNoiseInput[1 * totalPixels + pixelIndex] = (noiseRG.y - noiseMin) / (noiseMax - noiseMin);
}
__device__ inline uchar4 packColor(float r, float g, float b, float a)
{
    return make_uchar4(static_cast<unsigned char>(r * 255.0f), static_cast<unsigned char>(g * 255.0f),
                       static_cast<unsigned char>(b * 255.0f), static_cast<unsigned char>(a * 255.0f));
}

__device__ __forceinline__ float clamp(float v, float minVal, float maxVal)
{
    return fminf(fmaxf(v, minVal), maxVal);
}

__global__ void UnetPostProcessKernel(float *outputData, cudaSurfaceObject_t outputSurface, int width, int height)
{
    // convert NCHW to RGBA format
    int idx         = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
    int idy         = blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
    int totalPixels = width * height;
    int pixelIndex  = idy * width + idx;

    if (idx >= width || idy >= height)
        return;

    float r = clamp(outputData[0 * totalPixels + pixelIndex], 0.0f, 1.0f);
    float g = clamp(outputData[1 * totalPixels + pixelIndex], 0.0f, 1.0f);
    float b = clamp(outputData[2 * totalPixels + pixelIndex], 0.0f, 1.0f);

    uchar4 outColor = packColor(r,g, b, 1.0f);

    surf2Dwrite(outColor, outputSurface, idx * sizeof(uchar4), idy);
}

//----------------------------------------------------------------------
// Class UNetDenoiserGPU
//----------------------------------------------------------------------

UNetDenoiserGPU::UNetDenoiserGPU(GFX::VkContext &context, AppConfig &appconfig, Noise &noise) :
    UNetDenoiser(context, appconfig, noise)
{}

void UNetDenoiserGPU::InitPass(const GFX::VkImageObject &inputImage)
{
    UNetDenoiser::InitPass(inputImage);

    // Allocate CUDA memory for preprocessed data (NCHW format)
    cudaMalloc(&m_processedInput, m_inputSize * sizeof(float));
    cudaMalloc(&m_processedNoiseInput, m_inputSize * sizeof(float));

    ExportInputImageToCudaMemory();
    ExportNoiseImageToCudaMemory();
    ExportOutputImageToCudaMemory();
}

UNetDenoiserGPU::~UNetDenoiserGPU()
{
    cudaFree(m_processedInput);
    cudaFree(m_processedNoiseInput);
    if (m_inputExtMemory)
    {
        cudaDestroyTextureObject(m_inputTextureObject);
        cudaDestroyExternalMemory(m_inputExtMemory);
    }
    if (m_noiseExtMemory)
    {
        cudaDestroyTextureObject(m_noiseTextureObject);
        cudaDestroyExternalMemory(m_noiseExtMemory);
    }

    cudaDestroySurfaceObject(m_outputSurfaceObject);
    cudaDestroyExternalMemory(m_outputExtMemory);
}

void UNetDenoiserGPU::ExportInputImageToCudaMemory()
{
    if (!m_inputImage)
        return;
    VkCudaInterop::ImportVulkanImage(*m_inputImage, m_inputExtMemory, &m_inputTextureObject, nullptr, cudaCreateChannelDesc<uchar4>(),
                      cudaReadModeNormalizedFloat);
}

void UNetDenoiserGPU::ExportNoiseImageToCudaMemory()
{
    if (!m_noiseImage)
        return;
    VkCudaInterop::ImportVulkanImage(*m_noiseImage, m_noiseExtMemory, &m_noiseTextureObject, nullptr,
                      cudaCreateChannelDesc(32, 32, 0, 0, cudaChannelFormatKindFloat), cudaReadModeElementType);
}

void UNetDenoiserGPU::ExportOutputImageToCudaMemory()
{
    if (!OutputImage)
        return;
    VkCudaInterop::ImportVulkanImage(*OutputImage, m_outputExtMemory, nullptr, &m_outputSurfaceObject, cudaCreateChannelDesc<uchar4>(),
                      cudaReadModeNormalizedFloat);
}

void UNetDenoiserGPU::InferencePreprocess()
{
    m_preprocessingTimer.start();
    auto [noiseMin, noiseMax] = m_noise.GetMinMaxStepSize();
    dim3 blockSize(16, 16);
    dim3 gridSize((m_width + blockSize.x - 1) / blockSize.x, (m_height + blockSize.y - 1) / blockSize.y);
    UnetPreprocessKernel<<<gridSize, blockSize>>>(m_inputTextureObject, m_noiseTextureObject, m_processedInput,
                                              m_processedNoiseInput, m_inputImage->Extent.width,
                                              m_inputImage->Extent.height, noiseMin, noiseMax);

    auto err = cudaDeviceSynchronize();
    if (err != cudaSuccess)
    {
        cudaFree(m_processedInput);
        cudaDestroyExternalMemory(m_inputExtMemory);
        throw std::runtime_error("CUDA kernel execution failed");
    }

    Ort::MemoryInfo memoryInfo("Cuda", OrtDeviceAllocator, 0, OrtMemTypeDefault);

    m_noisyImageTensor.emplace(Ort::Value::CreateTensor<float>(memoryInfo, m_processedInput, m_inputSize,
                                                               m_inputShape.data(), m_inputShape.size()));

    m_noiseImageTensor.emplace(Ort::Value::CreateTensor<float>(memoryInfo, m_processedNoiseInput, m_inputSize,
                                                               m_inputShape.data(), m_inputShape.size()));

    m_outputImageTensor.emplace(Ort::Value::CreateTensor<float>(memoryInfo, m_processedInput, m_inputSize,
                                                                m_inputShape.data(), m_inputShape.size()));
    m_preprocessingTimer.stop();
}

void UNetDenoiserGPU::InferencePostprocess(float *outputData, std::vector<int64_t> &outputShape)
{
    m_postprocessingTimer.start();
    dim3 blockSize(16, 16);
    dim3 gridSize((m_width + blockSize.x - 1) / blockSize.x, (m_height + blockSize.y - 1) / blockSize.y);
    UnetPostProcessKernel<<<gridSize, blockSize>>>(outputData, m_outputSurfaceObject, m_width, m_height);
    auto err = cudaDeviceSynchronize();
    if (err != cudaSuccess)
    {
        cudaFree(m_processedInput);
        cudaDestroyExternalMemory(m_outputExtMemory);
        throw std::runtime_error("CUDA kernel execution failed");
    }
    m_postprocessingTimer.stop();
}
#endif
```

### renderer/denoisers/unet_denoiser_gpu.h
```
#ifdef TRAINED_DENOISER_ON
#pragma once

#include "unet_denoiser.h"

#include <cuda.h>
#include <cuda_runtime_api.h>

/**
 * @brief UNet denoiser class processing with GPU acceleration using CUDA.
 */
class UNetDenoiserGPU : public UNetDenoiser
{
  public:
    UNetDenoiserGPU(GFX::VkContext &context, AppConfig &appconfig, Noise &noise);
    ~UNetDenoiserGPU();
    void InitPass(const GFX::VkImageObject &inputImage) override;
    void InferencePreprocess() override;
    void InferencePostprocess(float *outputData, std::vector<int64_t> &outputShape) override;

  private:
    void ExportInputImageToCudaMemory();
    void ExportNoiseImageToCudaMemory();
    void ExportOutputImageToCudaMemory();

    // Helper to import Vulkan image to CUDA memory and create texture
    void ImportVulkanImage(const GFX::VkImageObject &image, cudaExternalMemory_t &extMem,
                           cudaTextureObject_t *outTextureObject, cudaSurfaceObject_t *outSurfaceObject,
                           cudaChannelFormatDesc formatDesc, cudaTextureReadMode readMode);

  private:
    cudaExternalMemory_t m_inputExtMemory;
    cudaTextureObject_t  m_inputTextureObject{0};
    float               *m_processedInput = nullptr;

    cudaExternalMemory_t m_noiseExtMemory;
    cudaTextureObject_t  m_noiseTextureObject{0};
    float               *m_processedNoiseInput = nullptr;

    cudaExternalMemory_t m_outputExtMemory;
    cudaSurfaceObject_t  m_outputSurfaceObject{0};
};
#endif
```

### renderer/denoisers/wiener_filter.cpp
```
#include "wiener_filter.h"
#include "../utils/container.h"
#include "../utils/utils.h"
#include "imgui.h"

WienerFilter::WienerFilter(GFX::VkContext &context, AppConfig &config, Noise &noise) :
    IDenoiser(context, config), m_noise(noise)
{
    m_wienerFilterUbos.kernelSize    = config.Denoiser.ScreenSpaced.WienerFilter.kernelSize;
    m_wienerFilterUbos.noiseVariance = m_noise.GetMinMaxStepSize().second;
}

void WienerFilter::InitPass(const GFX::VkImageObject &inputImage)
{
    IDenoiser::InitPass(inputImage);

    Utils::CreateAndCopyBuffer(m_context, &m_wienerFilterUbos, sizeof(WienerFilterUbos),
                               vk::BufferUsageFlagBits::eUniformBuffer | vk::BufferUsageFlagBits::eTransferDst,
                               VMA_MEMORY_USAGE_GPU_ONLY, m_wienerFilterUboDeviceBuffer);
    m_wienerFilterUboDeviceBuffer->SetName("Wiener Filter UBO");
    GFX::VkDescriptorSetObject::ElementUniformBuffer wienerFilterDescriptorElement{*m_wienerFilterUboDeviceBuffer};
    InitPassScreenspace(inputImage, m_appConfig.Denoiser.ScreenSpaced.WienerFilter.shaderPath,
                        {&wienerFilterDescriptorElement});
}

void WienerFilter::DrawDenoiserUI()
{
    if (ImGui::InputInt("Kernel Size", &m_wienerFilterUbos.kernelSize, 1))
    {
        m_wienerFilterUbos.kernelSize = std::clamp(m_wienerFilterUbos.kernelSize, 1, 7);
        g_DirtyBuffers.insert(
            {m_wienerFilterUboDeviceBuffer->Buffer, 0, sizeof(WienerFilterUbos), &m_wienerFilterUbos});
    }
}
```

### renderer/utils/container.cpp
```
#include "container.h"

// Define the global static set here
std::set<BufferContainer::DirtyBuffer> g_DirtyBuffers;

void BufferContainer::UpdateDirtyBuffers(const vk::CommandBuffer &commandBuffer)
{
    for (const auto &dirtyUniformBuffer : g_DirtyBuffers)
    {
        commandBuffer.updateBuffer(dirtyUniformBuffer.Buffer, dirtyUniformBuffer.Offset, dirtyUniformBuffer.size,
                                   dirtyUniformBuffer.data);
    }
    g_DirtyBuffers.clear();
}
```

### renderer/utils/container.h
```
#pragma once

#include "buffer.h"
#include "image.h"
#include <set>
#include <vulkan/vulkan.hpp>

/**
 * @brief A container for dirty buffers that need to be updated in the command buffer.
 */
struct BufferContainer
{
    struct DirtyBuffer
    {
        vk::Buffer     Buffer;
        size_t         Offset;
        vk::DeviceSize size;
        void          *data;

        bool operator<(const DirtyBuffer &other) const
        {
            return std::tie(Buffer, Offset) < std::tie(other.Buffer, other.Offset);
        }
    };
    static void UpdateDirtyBuffers(const vk::CommandBuffer &commandBuffer);
};

extern std::set<BufferContainer::DirtyBuffer> g_DirtyBuffers;
```

### renderer/utils/exporter.cpp
```
#define STB_IMAGE_WRITE_IMPLEMENTATION
#include "../third-party/stb_image_write.h"

#include "buffer.h"
#include "exporter.h"
#include "utils.h"

Exporter::Exporter(GFX::VkContext &context) : m_context(context)
{}

std::vector<uint8_t> Exporter::ExportImageToBuffer(GFX::VkImageObject &image, bool alphaMultiply) const
{
    // prepare cpu buffer
    size_t numPixels  = image.Extent.width * image.Extent.height;
    size_t bufferSize = 0;
    switch (image.Format)
    {
        case vk::Format::eR8G8B8A8Unorm:
            bufferSize = numPixels * 4;        // 4 channels, 1 byte each
            break;
        case vk::Format::eR32G32Sfloat:
            bufferSize = numPixels * 8;        // 2 channels, 4 bytes each
            break;
        // add more formats if needed
        default:
            std::cerr << "Unimplemented image format for export: " << vk::to_string(image.Format) << std::endl;
            return {};
    }

    GFX::VkBufferObject stagingBuffer(m_context, bufferSize, vk::BufferUsageFlagBits::eTransferDst,
                                      VMA_MEMORY_USAGE_GPU_TO_CPU);
    return ExportImageToBuffer(image, stagingBuffer, alphaMultiply);
}

std::vector<uint8_t> Exporter::ExportImageToBuffer(GFX::VkImageObject &image, GFX::VkBufferObject &stagingBuffer,
                                                   bool alphaMultiply) const
{
    // prepare image for buffer copy
    auto oldLayout = image.ImageLayout;
    image.TransitionImageLayout(vk::ImageLayout::eTransferSrcOptimal);

    // prepare cpu buffer
    size_t numPixels  = image.Extent.width * image.Extent.height;
    size_t bufferSize = stagingBuffer.Size;

    // copy
    image.CopyImageToBuffer(stagingBuffer);
    void *data = stagingBuffer.MapMemory();

    // cleanup
    stagingBuffer.UnMapMemory();
    image.TransitionImageLayout(oldLayout);

    switch (image.Format)
    {
        case vk::Format::eR8G8B8A8Unorm:
        {
            uint8_t *pixels = static_cast<uint8_t *>(data);

            if (alphaMultiply)
            {
#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
                for (int i = 0; i < numPixels; ++i)
                {
                    uint8_t &r     = pixels[i * 4 + 0];
                    uint8_t &g     = pixels[i * 4 + 1];
                    uint8_t &b     = pixels[i * 4 + 2];
                    uint8_t &a     = pixels[i * 4 + 3];
                    float    alpha = a / 255.0f;
                    r              = static_cast<uint8_t>(r * alpha);
                    g              = static_cast<uint8_t>(g * alpha);
                    b              = static_cast<uint8_t>(b * alpha);
                    a              = 255;
                }
            }

            return std::vector<uint8_t>(pixels, pixels + numPixels * 4);
        }
        case vk::Format::eR32G32Sfloat:
        {
            float               *pixels = static_cast<float *>(data);
            std::vector<uint8_t> outputBuffer;
            outputBuffer.resize(numPixels * 4);
#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
            for (int i = 0; i < numPixels; ++i)
            {
                float r = pixels[i * 2 + 0];
                float g = pixels[i * 2 + 1];

                uint8_t rByte = static_cast<uint8_t>(std::clamp(r, 0.0f, 1.0f) * 255.0f);
                uint8_t gByte = static_cast<uint8_t>(std::clamp(g, 0.0f, 1.0f) * 255.0f);

                outputBuffer[i * 4 + 0] = rByte;
                outputBuffer[i * 4 + 1] = gByte;
                outputBuffer[i * 4 + 2] = 0;
                outputBuffer[i * 4 + 3] = 255;
            }
            return outputBuffer;
        }
        // add more formats if needed
        default:
            std::cerr << "Unimplemented image format for export: " << vk::to_string(image.Format) << std::endl;
            return {};
    }
}

void Exporter::ExportImage(GFX::VkImageObject &image, const std::string &filename, bool alphaMultiply) const
{
    auto buffer = ExportImageToBuffer(image, alphaMultiply);

    if (Utils::GetFileExtension(filename) == "png")
    {
        stbi_write_png(filename.c_str(), image.Extent.width, image.Extent.height, 4, buffer.data(),
                       image.Extent.width * 4);
    }
    else if (Utils::GetFileExtension(filename) == "jpg")
    {
        stbi_write_jpg(filename.c_str(), image.Extent.width, image.Extent.height, 4, buffer.data(),
                       image.Extent.width * 4);
    }
    else
    {
        std::cerr << "Unsupported file format for export: " << Utils::GetFileExtension(filename)
                  << ". Supported formats are png and jpg." << std::endl;
        return;
    }
}
```
